Isandulelo

'Bhavishya Malika' iqoqo lamakhulu emibhalo eyabhalwa eminyakeni engaba ngu-600 edlule yi-Panchasakhas (abangane abahlanu/ozakwethu) beNkosi uShri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Le mibhalo yahlala ifihlekile amakhulu eminyaka, futhi ngaleso sikhathi, abantu abaningi babengayazi. Nokho, isiphepho esikhulu esashaya i-Odisha ngo-1999 sadonsa ukunaka kwabantu abaningi ku-'Bhavishya Malika' ngoba kwathiwa iziprofetho eziphathelene naleso siphepho zase zenziwe kuleyo mibhalo.

Nokho, njengoba yabhalwa ngolimi lwesi-Odia, i-Bhavishya Malika yahlala ikhawulelwe e-Odisha ngaleso sikhathi. Cishe akekho ngaphandle kwe-Odisha owayenolwazi ngale mibhalo. Kodwa kancane kancane, izikhathi zashintsha. Ngonyaka ka-2020, ubhubhane lwe-COVID-19 lwaphinde lwadonsa ukunaka kwabantu ku-Bhavishya Malika. Kwakungemuva kwalolu bhubhane kuphela lapho ukuphromotha nokusabalalisa imibhalo ye-Malika kwaqala ngaphandle kwe-Odisha, futhi le nqubo iqhubekile kusukela ngaleso sikhathi.

Ngenxa yalokhu, abantu abaningi abangenakubalwa sebeqalile ukufunda nge-Bhavishya Malika eminyakeni embalwa edlule. Nokho, njengoba kuvame ukwenzeka, kanye nalokhu kumenyezelwa okuhle, ukuhunyushwa okungalungile nolwazi olungelona iqiniso mayelana namanye amavesi e-Malika nakho kwaqala ukusabalala. Ngokwesibonelo, abanye abantu baqala ukusabalalisa amahlebezi okuthi iminyaka ye-Kaliyuga ingamashumi ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi; ukuthi igama lenkosi yokugcina yase-Puri kuzoba uMukunda Dev; ukuthi imicimbi emikhulu ye-Malika kanye nezenzo zokubhujiswa zaphezulu (Sanhaar-Leela) zeNkosi uKalki zizokwenzeka ngesikhathi esilandelayo se-Meena-Shani Yoga (isikhathi esiphakathi kuka-2054-57); ukuthi isifunda sase-Khandagiri asikho e-Bhubaneswar kodwa sitholakala kwenye indawo; nokuthi kusekuningi okusele ngaphambi kokuqala kwe-Satyayuga, njll.

Lolu lwazi olungelona iqiniso lwaqala ukudala ukungabaza okuningi ezingqondweni zabazinikele. Ngenxa yokuntuleka kwesixazululo esifanele kulokhu kungabaza, ukholo lwabo ku-Malika lwaqala ukuncipha. Inhloso enkulu yalesi sihloko akukhona nje ukuhlinzeka abafundi abasha ngolwazi olulungile nolunembile nge-Bhavishya Malika kodwa futhi nokuxazulula ukungabaza okuvela ezingqondweni zabazinikele (bhaktas). Kunethemba lokuthi ngokuthola ulwazi olulungile mayelana nalezi zakhi eziyisisekelo, abathembekile bazokwazi ukuqinisa kakhulu ukholo lwabo ku-Malika.

1 I-Bhavishya Malika – Isingeniso Esifushane

1.1 Isingeniso

Phakathi kwekhulu le-15 nele-16, osanta abahlanu abakhulu abazalelwe e-Odisha (uShishu Ananta Das (Shishu Ananta Das), uYashobanta Das, u-Achyutananda Das, uBalarama Das, noJagannatha Das) baqamba amakhulu emibhalo enjalo lapho kwabhalwa khona iziprofetho ezingenakubalwa. Le mibhalo yaziwa ngokuthi 'i-Bhavishya Malika'. USri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu, owazalwa ngo-1486 futhi obhekwa njengokuzalwa kabili kukaBhagawan Sri Krishna noJaganmata Radharani, wayenobudlelwane obujulile nalaba osanta abahlanu. USri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu wabiza laba abahlanu ngokuthi 'i-Panchasakha' (Abangane Abahlanu). Laba ba-Panchasakha baqamba imibhalo ye-Malika ngomyalo kaMahaprabhu uqobo. Ngaphandle kwaba-Panchasakha, abanye osanta abakhulu nabo baqhubekisela phambili isiko lokubhala i-Malika. Ngokwesibonelo, umbhakti uSalabega owazalwa ngekhulu le-17, uSanta Arkhit Das noSanta Hadidas abazalwa ngekhulu le-18, uSanta Bhima Bhoi wekhulu le-19, uThakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa wekhulu lama-20, njll.

Iziprofetho ezinemininingwane zabhalwa kule mibhalo ye-Malika ezindabeni ezifana neminyaka ye-Kaliyuga, ukuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki, ukubhujiswa kwababi, ukuvikelwa kwabazinikele, ukusungulwa kweDharma, nokufika kweSatyayuga.

1.2 Babengobani aba-Panchasakha?

Omunye waba-Panchasakha, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das, wabhala komunye wemibhalo yakhe ye-Malika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବଳବନ୍ତ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମେ ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Balabanta Yaśhobanta Ananta Achyuta,
Jagannātha Dāsa Pañchasakhā nāme khyāta.

Incazelo:
UBalarama Das, uYashobanta Das, uShishu Ananta Das, u-Achyutananda Das, noJagannatha Das baba abadumile njengaba-Panchasakha.

Kuyaphawuleka ukuthi umlando waba-Panchasakha mdala kakhulu. Ekupheleni kwesikhathi ngasinye (Yuga), aba-Panchasakha behlela eMhlabeni, bezalwa engxenyeni yeNkosi, ukuze banikele ngomnikelo wabo ongenakulinganiswa emsebenzini weNkosi wokubuyisela iDharma.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe we-Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ପଞ୍ଚକୁଳରେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଆଜ୍ଞାରେ।
ଯୁଗ ଯୁଗରେ ଯେ ଜନମ ହେଉ,
କୃଷ୍ଣଲୀଳାମାନ ମୁଖରେ ଗାଉ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ambhe Pañchasakhā pañchakulare,
janama hoilu Kṛṣhṇa ājñāre.
yuga yugare je janama heu,
Kṛṣhṇalīḷāmāna mukhare gau.

Incazelo:
Kuleli vesi, uSanta Achyutananda Das uthi: "Thina, abangane abahlanu, sizalwa ezinhlotsheni ezinhlanu ezahlukene ngomyalo weNkosi uKrishna. Kuwo wonke unyaka, siyazalwa futhi sihlele izenzo zaphezulu (Leelas) zikaKrishna ngemilomo yethu."

1.3 Ukubonakaliswa kwaba-Panchasakha Kuzo Zonke Izikhathi Ezine

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, embhalweni wakhe we-Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’, uchaza izimo ezathathwa osanta abahlanu abakhulu kuwo wonke ama-Yuga amane:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଆଦିଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ଅନ୍ତରେ, ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରବେଶ ହୋଇଲା ମହୀରେ।
ଅସୁର ବିନାଶ କରିବା ପାଇଁ, ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିଲେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣେ ଯାଇଁ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଋଷିତନୁ ବହିଲେ, କୃପାଜଳ ଋଷି ଗାଗର୍ବ ହେଲେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଯେ ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ନାରଦ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ କହିଲି ଭେଦ।
ରାମ ଜନମ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଗୁଣ କହିଲେ ନ ସରେ।
ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନଳ ନୀଳ ସୁଷେଣ, ଜାମ୍ବବ ହନୁ ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନ।
ତ୍ରେତୟା ଅନ୍ତେ ଦ୍ୱାପର ପ୍ରବେଶ, ଗୋପାଳ ମୂରତି ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବେଶ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନେ ହେଲେ ଜନମ, ସୁବଳ ସୁବାହୁ ସୁଦାମ ଦାମ।
ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଘେନି ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ହେଲେ, ଦ୍ୱାପର ଲୀଳାମାନ ସାଙ୍ଗ କଲେ।
ଅବନୀ ମଧ୍ୟରେ ନାମ ସ୍ଥାପିଲେ, ହରିନାମରେ ଜଗତ ମୋହିଲେ।
ସ୍ତ୍ରୀ ଶୂଦ୍ର ଆଦି ପାଷାଣ୍ଡ ଯେତେ, ହରିନାମ ଶୁଣି ହେଲେ ମୁକତେ।
ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ ହେଲୁ, ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନାମ ବହିଲୁ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୋଇଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ମେଳ ବଳରାମରେ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ନାମରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ, କହିଦେଲୁ ବାବୁ ତୋର ଆଗେଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Adi Ananta Yugaṭi Antare, Satya Prabesha Hoilā Mahire.
Asura Bināsha Karibā Pāiñ, Prabhu Janamile Brāhmaṇe Jāiñ.
Pañchasakhā Ṛuṣi Tanu Bahile, Kṛupājala Ṛuṣi Gāgarba Hele.
Mārkaṇḍa Je Swayambhū Nārada, Pañchasakhā Janama Kahili Bheda.
Rāma Janama Tretayā Yugare, Tretayā Guṇa Kahile Na Sare.
Tretayāre Naḷa Nīḷa Suṣeṇa, Jāmbaba Hanu Pañchasakhāmāna.
Tretayā Ante Dwāpara Prabesha, Gopāḷa Mūrati Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Besha.
Pañchasakhāmāne Hele Janama, Subaḷa Subāhu Sudāma Dāma.
Shrībatsa Gheni Pañchasakhā Hele, Dwāpara Līḷāmāna Sāṅga Kale.
Abanī Madhyare Nāma Sthāpile, Harināmare Jagata Mohile.
Strī Shūdra Ādi Pāṣāṇḍa Jete, Harināma Shuṇi Hele Mukate.
Ambhe Pañchasakhā Janama Helu, Ananta Achyuta Nāma Bahilu.
Jagannātha Jashobanta Hoile, Pañchasakhā Meḷa Baḷarāmare.
Emanta Nāmare Ambhe Janama, Kahidelu bābu tora āgeṇa.

Incazelo:

Okusho ukuthi, ezikhathini zasendulo, ekupheleni kwe-Ananta Yuga lapho i-Satyayuga iqala, iNkosi Ephakeme yazalwa ngesimo seBrahmin ukuze ibhubhise amademoni. Ngaleso sikhathi, osanta abahlanu bavela emzimbeni weNkosi uqobo njengama-Sage: uKripajala, uGarga, uMarkandeya, uSwayambhu, noNarada.

Kulandela i-Satyayuga, e-Tretayuga, lapho iNkosi yenza izenzo zaphezulu ezingenakubalwa ekuzalweni kwayo njengoRama (Rama), laba bangane abahlanu behlela eMhlabeni ngesimo sikaNala (Nala), uNila, uSushena, uJambavan, noHanuman.

Ekupheleni kwe-Tretayuga, kwafika i-Dwaparyuga, futhi iNkosi yazalwa ngesimo somfana womelusi, uShri Krishna. Ngalesi sikhathi, uSubala, uSubahu, uSudama, uDama, noShribatsa baba aba-Panchasakha futhi babamba iqhaza ezenzweni zaphezulu noShri Krishna.

Ngaphezu kwalokho, e-Kaliyuga, lapho iNkosi yavela ekuzalweni kwayo njengoChaitanya, laba bangane abahlanu bazalwa ukuze basakaze igama elingcwele (Hari-naam), besungula indlela elula nelula yensindiso yabo bonke. E-Kaliyuga, baba abadumile njengoShishu Ananta Das, u-Achyutananda Das, uJagannatha Das, uYashobanta Das, noBalarama Das.

1.4 Uhlaka Olunemininingwane Lwaba-Panchasakha Kuzo Zonke Izikhathi

Lapho umfundi uRamadas ebuza ngamagama athile aba-Panchasakha kuzo zonke izikhathi ezine, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wanikeza le ncazelo enemininingwane embhalweni wakhe we-Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’.

I-Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ଋଷି କୃପାଜଳ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ସଖାଟି ଅଟଇ ନଳ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁଦାମ ସଖାଟି ହୋଇ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲାଇ।
ଗାର୍ଗବ ଋଷି ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ହେଲେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଜାମ୍ବବ ନାମ ବହିଲେ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁବାହୁ ବ୍ରଜ ଅଟନ୍ତି, ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ନାମ କଳିରେ ପାନ୍ତି।
ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସେ ଜାଣେ, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ଅଟେ ସେହି ସୁଷେଣ।
ଗୋପରେ ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ଜାତ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ନାମ ଶିଶୁ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସଖା ନାରଦ ଋଷି, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନୀଳ ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶି।
ଦାମ ଗୋପାଳ ଦ୍ୱାପର ଯୁଗରେ, ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ନାମ କଳିରେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଋଷି ଅଟେ ହନୁମାନ, ସୁବଳ ସଖାଟି ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ପୁଣ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ନାମ କଳିରେ ହେଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶ କଲେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Satya Yugare Ṛuṣi Kṛupājala, Tretayā Sakhāṭi Aṭai Naḷa.
Dwāpare Sudāma Sakhāṭi Hoi, Kaḷi Yugare Achyuta Bolāi.
Gārgaba Ṛuṣi Satya Yugare Hele, Tretayā Jāmbaba Nāma Bahile.
Dwāpare Subāhu Braja Aṭanti, Jashobanta Nāma Kaḷire Pānti.
Swayambhū Satya Yugare Se Jāṇe, Tretayāre Aṭe Sehi Suṣeṇa.
Gopare Shrībatsa Dwāpare Jāta, Kaḷi Yugare Nāma Shishu Ananta.
Satya Yugare Sakhā Nārada Ṛuṣi, Tretayāre Nīḷa Nāma Prakāshi.
Dāma Gopāḷa Dwāpara Yugare, Baḷarāma Dāsa Nāma Kaḷire.
Mārkaṇḍa Ṛuṣi Aṭe Hanumāna, Subaḷa Sakhāṭi Dwāpare Puṇa.
Jagannātha Nāma Kaḷire Hele, Pañchasakhā Nāma Prakāsha Kale.

Ukuhumusha Kobunikazi:
  • U-Achyutananda Das: Wayengu-Sage Kripajala e-Satyayuga, uNala e-Tretayuga, noSudama e-Dwaparayuga.
  • UYashobanta Das: Wayengu-Sage Garga e-Satyayuga, uJambavan e-Tretayuga, noSubahu e-Dwaparayuga.
  • UShishu Ananta Das: Wayengu-Sage Swayambhu e-Satyayuga, uSushena e-Tretayuga, noShribatsa e-Dwaparayuga.
  • UBalarama Das: Wayengu-Sage Narada e-Satyayuga, uNila e-Tretayuga, noDama e-Dwaparayuga.
  • UJagannatha Das: Wayengu-Sage Markandeya e-Satyayuga, uHanuman e-Tretayuga, noSubala e-Dwaparayuga.

1.5 Amandla Akhethekile Nobuchwepheshe Bama-Panchasakha

Aba-Panchasakha babengosanta abazi konke, abazibonile, nababone uNkulunkulu. Babengama-Yogi aphakeme, abafuni, nabazinikele, ngamunye ephumelela emkhakheni ohlukile wesayensi engokomoya. UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ukuchaza lokhu e-Malika yakhe:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଆଗମ୍ୟ ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ,
ଗାର କଟା ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଜାଣେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ଆଗତ ଅନାଗତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭଣେ,
ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ବଖାଣେ।
ଭକ୍ତିର ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମହନ୍ତ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Āgamya Bhāba Jāṇe Jashobanta,
Gāra Kaṭā Jantra Jāṇe Ananta.
Āgata Anāgata Achyuta Bhaṇe,
Baḷarāma Dāsa Tattwa Bakhāṇe.
Bhaktira Bhāba Jāṇe Jagannātha,
Pañchasakhā E Oḍiśā Mahanta.

Incazelo:
Phakathi kwamaPanchasakhas azalelwe e-Odisha:
  • U-Yashobanta Das wayengumazi wezimfihlakalo ezijulile zesikhathi esidlule nesizayo (Agam-Nigam).
  • U-Shishu Ananta Das wayenguchwepheshe kwisayensi yamadiagramu angaqondakali nemishini (Yantra-Vigyan).
  • U-Achyutananda Das wayengumboni wesikhathi esidlule, samanje nesizayo (Trikal-Darshi).
  • U-Balarama Das wayeyisazi esijulile seqiniso eliphelele nefilosofi (Tattwa-Vigyan).
  • U-Jagannatha Das wayengenakuqhathaniswa emzweni wokuzinikela okumsulwa (Bhakti-Bhaav).

1.6 Izikhungo Ezingcwele (Ashrams) zamaPanchasakhas

AmaPanchasakhas asungula izihlalo zawo ezingcwele (Peethas) nezindawo zokuzindla ezindaweni ezinhlanu ezihlukene kulo lonke elase-Odisha. U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das uchaza lokhu embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅଢ଼ଙ୍ଗେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ, ବାଲିପାଟଣାରେ ରହିଲେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସାତ ଲହଡ଼ିରେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ରହି, କ୍ଷେତ୍ରବରେ ବଳବନ୍ତ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Nemāḷe Achyuta Aḍhaṅge Jashobanta, Bālipāṭaṇāre rahiḷe Ananta.
Sāta Lahaḍire Jagannātha rahi, Khetrabare Baḷabanta.

Incazelo:
  • U-Achyutananda Das wasungula isihlalo sakhe eNemalo (esifundeni saseCuttack (Cuttack)).
  • U-Yashobanta Das wasungula iPeetha yakhe e-Adhanga (esifundeni saseJagatsinghpur (Jagatsinghpur)).
  • U-Shishu Ananta Das (Ananta (Ananta)) wasungula indawo yakhe yokuzindla eBalipatna (esifundeni saseKhurda (Khurda)).
  • U-Jagannatha Das wasungula i-Ashram yakhe eSata Lahadi.
  • U-Balarama Das (Balabanta (Balabanta)) wenza iShrikhetra (Puri (Puri)) indawo yakhe yokuhlala.

1.7 AmaPanchasakhas – Ukuzalwa Kwaphezulu KweNkosi

AmaPanchasakhas asho izikhathi eziningi emibhalweni yawo yeMalika ukuthi angukuzalwa okuyingxenye (Anshavatar (Anshavatar)) kweNkosi Ephakeme (Supreme Lord) uqobo. U-Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe ‘Chumbak Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲିଣ କଳିଯୁଗେ ନାମ ସୁଦାମ ସଖା ଅଟନ୍ତି,
ଶ୍ରୀଅଙ୍ଗରୁ ଜାତ କଟିରୁ ଭକତ ଶୁଣ ହୋ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଚେତି।
ମୁଖୁଁ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୃଦୁଁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜାତ ବାହୁ ମୂଁଳୁ ବଳରାମ,
ରାଧାଙ୍କ ହାସ୍ୟରୁ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରେ ଘେନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Achyuta boliṇa Kaḷiyuge nāma Sudāma sakhā aṭanti,
Shrī-aṅgaru jāta kaṭiru bhakata shuṇa ho Bāraṅga cheti.
Mukhuṃ Jashobanta hṛuduṃ ambhe jāta bāhu mūṃḷu Baḷarāma,
Rādhāṅka hāsyaru Jagannātha Dāsa Bāraṅga heture ghena.

Incazelo:
E-Kaliyuga, u-Achyutananda Das ungumngane uSudama wase-Dwaparayuga futhi wazalwa okhalweni (Kati (Kati)) lweNkosi. U-Yashobanta Das wazalwa emlonyeni ongcwele (Shri-mukh (Shri-mukh)) kaMahaprabhu. U-Shishu Ananta Das wazibonakalisa enhliziyweni (Hriday (Hriday)) yeNkosi, futhi u-Balarama Das wazalwa ezingalweni (Bahu (Bahu)) zeNkosi. Ngaphezu kwalokho, u-Jagannatha Das wehla ekuhlekeni (Haasya (Haasya)) kukaMama uRadharani.

Akukhona nje u-Shishu Ananta Das ji, kodwa no-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhale ngalokhu embhalweni wakhe weMalika, ‘Chakada Madana’. U-Mahapurush ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ନିରାକାର ଦେହୁ ପାଞ୍ଚଟି ଜ୍ୟୋତି,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅନନ୍ତ ପଞ୍ଚକ ମୂରତି।
ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ବଳରାମ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ ଏ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ଧାମ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Nirākāra dehu pāṃchaṭi jyoti,
Achyuta Anaṃta paṃchaka mūrati.
Jashobaṃta Jagannātha Balarāma,
janama hoilu e martya dhāma.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, emzimbeni ofana nokukhanya weNkosi Engenamumo (Formless Lord), thina maPanchasakhas abahlanu sehla, futhi sazala kulomhlaba ngaphansi kwamagama ka-Achyutananda, Shishu Ananta, Jashobanta, Jagannatha, kanye no-Balarama.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni weMalika, ‘Barnatika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଶରୀର ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତରୁ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନ୍ମ କହିଛି ଗୁରୁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shrī Kṛiṣhṇa sharīra paṃchabhūtaru,
paṃchasakhā janma kahichi guru.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ukuzalwa kwamaPanchasakhas kwenzeka ezintweni ezinhlanu zomzimba weNkosi uShri Krishna.

1.8 Iziprofetho Ngomyalo Waphezulu

Amavesi eMalika athathwa njengamazwi kaMahaprabhu uqobo. Omunye wamaPanchasakhas, u-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୁଁ କହୁଛି ବୋଲି ମୋର କଥା ନୁହେଁ ଆଦ୍ୟୁଁ ଖଞ୍ଜିଛନ୍ତି ଧାତା,
କର୍ମବାମ ହେଲେ କଳ୍ପନା ଗ୍ରାସିବ ପ୍ରକୃତି କାଟିବେ ଚିତା।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Muṃ kahuchi boli mora kathā nuheṃ ādyuṃ khañjichanti dhātā,
Karmabāma hele kalpanā grāsiba prakṛuti kāṭibe chitā.

Incazelo:
U-Mahapurush uthi: "Noma yini engiyibhalayo, lawa akusiyo amazwi ami. Konke lokhu yizinto ezimiselwe yiNkosi kusukela ekuqaleni."

U-Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das futhi watshela umfundi wakhe ku-‘Patta Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରଖ ଯାକୁସାର,
ଭାବବିନୋଦିଆ ହରିଙ୍କ ଏ ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ସିନା ଅଗୋଚର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Āhuri kathāe shuṇare Bāraṅga heturakha jākusāra,
Bhābabinodiā Hariṅka e bāṇī aṭe sinā agochara.

Incazelo:
"O mfundi Baranga, lalela! Lawa akusiyo awami, kodwa kunalokho amazwi eNkosi uShri Hari (Lord Shri Hari)."

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhalela embhalweni wakhe weMalika, ‘Anakara Samhita’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଛତିଶ ସଂହିତା ଅଠସ୍ତରୀ ଗୀତା ବଂଶାନୁ ସପ୍ତବିଂଶରେ,
ଉପବଂଶାନୁ ଦ୍ୱାଦଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ବେନି ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଶତ ଖଣ୍ଡ ରେ।
ପଦ ପଦ୍ୟାବଳି ଲକ୍ଷେକ ଯେ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ସବୁ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ମହିମା,
ତୋ ଆଗେ କହିଲୁଁ ବ୍ରଜ କୁମର ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ସାରସ୍ୱତ ସୀମା।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chatisha saṃhitā aṭhastarī gītā baṃshānu saptabiṃshare,
upabaṃshānu dwādasha khaṃḍa beni bhabiṣhya shata khaṃḍa re.
Pada padyābali lakṣheka je graṃtha sabu Shrīkṛiṣhṇa mahimā,
to āge kahilu braja kumara brahma sāraswata sīmā.

Incazelo:
U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das uthi imibhalo ayiqambile ihlanganisa ama-Samhitas angu-36, ama-Gitas angu-78, ama-Banshanucharitras angu-27, ama-Upabanshanucharitras angu-24, imibhalo yeMalika engu-100, kanye namakhulu ezinkulungwane zamavesi (padas (padas)). U-Mahapurush uphinde wathi yonke le mibhalo iqanjwe ngenkazimulo noma umusa weNkosi uShri Krishna.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhalela embhalweni wakhe weMalika, ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀରଙ୍ଗା ଚରଣ ବିନୁ ନ ଜାଣଇ ନୀଳାଚଳେ ମନ ମୋର,
ତାଙ୍କ କଥାମାନ ଉଲ୍ଲେଖ କରଇ ନ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଛାର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shrī raṃgācharaṇa binu na jāṇai nīlāchale mana mora,
tāṃka kathāmāna ullekha karai na kahe Achyuta chāra.

Incazelo:
Kulesi sloka, u-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das uthi ingqondo yakhe ayinamathiseliwe kunoma yini enye ngaphandle kwezinyawo zeNkosi Ephakeme. Ingqondo yakhe ihlala igxile kuleyo Nilachala Dhama, Puri, okungukuthi, ekuzinikeleni okuthandayo eNkosini. Uphinde athi noma yini ayibhalayo emibhalweni yeMalika, konke kuyiwo kanye amazwi akhulunywe yiNkosi; akabhalanga lutho ngokwentando yakhe.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das waphinda wabhalela embhalweni wakhe weMalika, ‘Parardha Jataka’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଲା ଯେପରି,
ଆମ୍ଭେ ଲେଖିଲୁ ପୋଥି ସେହିପରି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Prabhunkara ājñā helā jepari,
āmbhe lekhilu pothi sehipari.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, njengoba nje iNkosi Ephakeme yasho futhi yayala, ngiqambe umbhalo weMalika ngendlela efanayo ncamashi.

U-Achyutanandaji wabhalela ku-‘Agata Bhabishya Prabandha Malika’ yakhe:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ମୁଖରୁ ଯାହା ମୋତେ ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଉଅଛି,
ସେହି ପଦମାନ ସବୁ ପୁରାଣେ ଲେଖୁଛି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shrī mukharu jāhā mote ājñā heuachi,
sehi padamāna sabu purāṇe lekhuchi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngomyalo weNkosi Ephakeme, ngokuqondisa Kwayo uqobo, ngibhala iMalika. Noma yini ephume eNkosini, ngichaza lawo ma-slokas kuMalika Purana.

1.9 IMalika Ingumbhalo Oyimfihlo Futhi Iziprofetho Zayo Zifihliwe

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku-‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଯୋଗୀମାନେ ଯୋଗ ଅନ୍ତ ନ ପାଇବେ ଆଉ କେବା ସାମରଥା।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Yogīmāne yoga anta na pāibe āu kebā sāmarathā.

Incazelo:
Ngisho nama-Yogis amakhulu nezihlakaniphi abakwazi ukuthola imfihlo yalezi zindaba ezifihliwe nge-Yoga, ingasaphathwa eyabanye abantu.

U-Achyutananda Das uphinde abhale mayelana nesimo esifihliwe sombhalo:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଓଲଟ ପାଲଟ କକ୍ଷା ଏହି ଅଙ୍କ, କର ହୃଦେ ତୁହି ଠାବ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଲୀଳା ଶେଷ ଯେ ହୋଇବ, ମସିହାରେ ନାହିଁ ଠାବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Olaṭa pālaṭa kakshā ehi aṅka, kara hṛude tuhi ṭhāba.
Kaḷiyuga līḷā shesha je hoiba, masihāre nāhiṃ ṭhāba.

Incazelo:
Iziprofetho zibhalwe ngokusekelwe ekubaleni kwezinombolo, futhi lezi zinombolo zifihliwe ngokuziphendulela. Izehlakalo zizokwenzeka kodwa hhayi ngokuya ngeminyaka yekhalenda ejwayelekile; zilandela izibalo eziyimfihlo ezingokomfanekiso.

Mayelana nokuzalwa kukaKalki (Kalki), ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହେବୁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସନ୍ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବୁ।
ଗୁପତ ଭକତ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଯାହା,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ଏ ଅଟେ ଏହା।
ଗୁପତରେ କେତେ ଖେଳ କରିବୁ,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କକୁ ଚଳାଇ ଥିବୁ।
ମୋର ଭକତଟି ଜାଣିବେ ସେହି,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ବୋଲିଟି ଯେ ଏହି।
ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ,
କହୁଅଛନ୍ତି ସେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Gupatare ambhe janama hebu,
gupatare san sāla chālibu.
Gupata bhakata jāṇanti jāhā,
gupata aṅka e aṭe ehā.
Gupatare kete kheḷa karibu,
gupata aṅkaku chaḷāi thibu.
Mora bhakataṭi jāṇibe sehi,
gupata aṅka boliṭi ye ehi.
Gupata gupata ati gupata,
kahuachanti se dīna Achyuta.

Incazelo:
Ukuzalwa kweNkosi kuzoba ngasese. Izikhathi neminyaka kuzoqhubeka ngendlela efihliwe. Abazinikele (devotees) abayimfihlo kuphela abazokwazi lokhu ngoba izinombolo zifihliwe.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika, 'Chakada Madana':

I-Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଆନେ ନ କହିବୁ ତୁହି,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଗୀତରେ କହଇ ମୋ ପାଦକୁ ଥାଅ ଛୁଇଁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata āne na kahibu tuhi,
dīna Achyuta je gītare kahai mo pādaku thāa chuiṃ.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lawa akuzona nje izimfihlo, kodwa angamazwi ayimfihlo kakhulu u-Achyutananda Das ji awabhale ngokukhumbula iNkosi nangokuzindla ngezinyawo zayo ezinjenge-lotus.

Kwenye incwadi, uMahapurush utshela umfundi wakhe uRamadasa:

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେଲେ ତୋହ ମୋହ ଦେଖା,
ତେରଜଣ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଥିବୁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା।
ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ ଅଟଇ ହେ ରାମ,
କହୁଅଛି ବ୍ରଜ ବାଳ ହେତୁରେ ତୁ ଘେନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kalki prakāsha hele toha moha dekhā,
terajaṇa madhye thibu āmbhe pañchasakhā.
Gupata ati gupata aṭai he Rāma,
kahuachi braja bāla heture tu ghena.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi Kalki izofika, ukubonakaliswa kwayo kwaphezulu kuzokwenzeka, futhi thina maPanchasakhas sizothola ithuba elihle lokukhonza nokuhlala sizinikele ezinyaweni zayo. Kodwa le midlalo yaphezulu izokwenzeka hhayi nje ngasese, kodwa ngendlela efihlekile kakhulu.

UMahapurush ubhala futhi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଶୁଣ ତୁ ରେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର,
ବ୍ରହ୍ମା ଶଙ୍କରକୁ ଭେଦ ତ ନଥିବ ନ ଜାଣିବେ ଇନ୍ଦ୍ର ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata shuṇa tu re Rāmachandra,
Brahmā Shaṅkaraku bheda ta nathiba na jāṇibe Indra Chandra.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, O mfundi Ramachandra, amazwi eMalika akuzona nje izimfihlo, kodwa ayimfihlo kakhulu. Ngisho noPrajapati Brahma neNkosi Shankar ngeke bakwazi ukuqonda izimfihlakalo zawo, ingasaphathwa iNkosi Indra neNkosi Chandra.

USant Bhima Bhoi naye ubhale ngalesi sihloko encwadini yakhe yeMalika, 'Stuti Chintamani':

I-Odia Sloka:

ସଂସାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ କେମନ୍ତେ ଜାଣିବେ ନର ଅଙ୍ଗେ ଦେହ ବହି,
ଗତ ଆଗତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଜଣାନାହିଁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Saṃsāra madhyare kemaṃte jāṇibe nara aṅge deha bahi,
gata āgata yugara byabasthā samastaṃku jaṇānāhiṃ.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, amadoda nabesifazane abaningi emhlabeni ngeke bakwazi ukuthola ulwazi mayelana nokulandelana kwezikhathi, ukuphela kweKali Yuga, iMalika, neziprofetho zayo. Kuphela labo iNkosi ebapha umusa wayo, ababengabazinikele beNkosi ezikhathini ezedlule, abazokwazi ngalezi ziprofetho ezijulile kakhulu.

Manje kuphakama umbuzo, bangobani labo bazinikele beNkosi abavela ezikhathini ezedlule iNkosi ezobapha umusa wayo futhi abazokholwa emazwini eMalika, bakwazi ukwazi ngayo, futhi babe nolwazi oluqondile mayelana nokuphela kweKali Yuga ne-avatar kaKalki!

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wanikeza impendulo yalokhu encwadini yakhe yeMalika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'. UMahapurush ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

କୋଟିକେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଏ ରସ ତିନି ସସ୍ରେ ଗଣାସେହି,
ମହିମା ପ୍ରକାଶ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ସେହି ଦାସ ଆନେ ମୁଁ କହନ୍ତି ନାହିଁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Koṭike goṭie jāṇaṃti e rasa tini sasre gaṇāसेhi,
mahimā prakāsha nishche sehi dāsa āne muṃ kahaṃti nāhiṃ.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, umuntu oyedwa kuphela ku-crore (izigidi eziyishumi) ozokwazi ngalezi ziprofetho, futhi inani eliphelele labantu abanjalo emhlabeni wonke lizoba ngu-3,000 kuphela.

1.10 Iziprofetho zeMalika Ngeke Zishintshe

IBhavishya Malika iqukethe amazwi eNkosi uqobo, ngakho-ke akukho mbuzo wokuthi azohluleka. Encwadini yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ପୃଥିବୀ ଦୁଇଫାଳ ହେଲେ ହୋଇବ, ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମେ ଯିବ।
ପର୍ବତ ଶିଖରେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ମିଥ୍ୟା ନୁହଇଁ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତର ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ କ୍ଷମତା କାହାର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Pṛuthibī duiphāḷa hele hoiba, pūrba sūrya abā pashchime jiba.
Parbata shikhare phuṭiba kaiñ, Achyuta bachana mithyā nuhaiñ.
Achyutara bāṇī aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ khyamatā kāhāra.

Incazelo:
"Umhlaba ungahlukana kabili, ilanga eliphuma empumalanga lingaphuma entshonalanga, i-lotus (kamala) ingaqhakaza esiqongweni sentaba—kodwa amazwi ka-Achyutananda ngeke abe amanga. Afana nomugqa oqoshwe etsheni, futhi akekho onamandla okuwafakazela ukuthi awamanga."

Ku-‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’, ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ସୀତା ଠାକୁରାଣୀ ଅସତୀ ହୋଇବେ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ତିଳେ ନ ଟଳିବ ପାଷାଣର ଗାର ଏହି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Sītā ṭhākurāṇī asatī hoibe parbate phuṭiba kaiñ,
Achyuta bachana tiḷe na taḷiba pāṣāṇara gāra ehi.

Incazelo:
E-Kaliyuga, abantu bangaze babize uMama Sita (Sītā Mā) ngokuthi akathembekile, futhi i-lotus ingaqhakaza esiqongweni sentaba, kodwa amazwi ka-Achyutananda Das ngeke ashintshe, ngoba afana nomugqa oqoshwe etsheni.

1.11 Ku-‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, ecaphuna amazwi eNkosi engenamzimba (Nirākāra), ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଥା ଯେବେ ନ ହୋଇବ, ଆମ୍ଭ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ମିଥ୍ୟା ହେବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

E kathā jebe na hoiba, ambha Shrīmukha mithyā heba.

Incazelo:
Kuleli vesi, iNkosi engenamzimba uqobo ithi: “Uma amazwi eMalika engaphumeleli, khona-ke umlomo wami ongcwele uzofakazelwa ukuthi awamanga.” Ngoba lawa angamazwi avela emlonyeni weNkosi uqobo, ngeke ahluleke.

UMahapurush wabhala futhi ku-'Chakada Madana':

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ବାଣୀ ପଥରର ଗାର ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମକୁ ଯିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ ଏହି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shrī Achyuta bāṇī patharara gāra parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
pūrba sūrya abā pashchimaku jiba mo bachana satya ehi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ilanga lingaphuma entshonalanga esikhundleni sempumalanga, i-lotus ingaqhakaza esiqongweni sentaba, kodwa amazwi ka-Achyutananda ngeke ashintshwe. Amazwi ka-Achyutananda aqoshwe etsheni; ayiqiniso eliphelele.

Ukuzalwa kabusha kuka-Achyutananda Das ji, uSant Hadi Das, owazalwa ngekhulu le-18 eduze neChatia esifundeni saseJajpur e-Odisha, naye waqamba imibhalo eminingi yeMalika.

Encwadini yakhe yeMalika ethi 'Kali Chautisha', wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଟଳିବ ନାହିଁଟି ଏ କଥା ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହୋଇବ ଦିନେ,
ଟାକି ରହିଛନ୍ତି ଭରସା କରି ଭକତମାନେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ṭaliba nāhiṃṭi e kathā nishche hoiba dine,
ṭāki rahichaṃti bharasā kari bhakatamāne.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, izinto ezibhalwe emibhalweni yeMalika ngeke zishintshwe neze – zizokwenzeka nakanjani ngelinye ilanga. Bonke abazinikele balindele lezi zenzakalo ngokholo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala futhi encwadini yakhe yeMalika, 'Agata Bhabishyat Malika':

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ ବାରମ୍ବାର,
ବୁଝାଏ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଧୀର।
ନମଣ ଏହାକୁ ଯେ ଆନ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ କରେ ଧ୍ୟାନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶଯିବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା,
ସାରିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକାଡୋଳା।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Achyuta dāsa bārambāra,
bujhāe bhakataṃku dhīra.
Namaṇa ehāku je āna,
Achyuta dāsa kare dhyāna.
Kali kalmaṣha nāshajiba,
Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Anaṃta Achyutanka līlā,
sāribe prabhu chakāḍolā.

Incazelo:
Ubhala ukuthi ngichaza ngokuphindaphindiwe kubazinikele ngokubekezela. Abazinikele akufanele babheke la mazwi njengamanga, ngoba u-Achyuta waqamba le mibhalo esesimweni sokuzindla. Izono nezinhlupheko zeKali Yuga zizobhujiswa, futhi imidlalo yaphezulu yeNkosi Kalki iqinisekile. UMahapurush ubhala futhi ukuthi amazwi akhe ngeke ashintshwe neze.

UMahapurush wabhala futhi kwenye yemibhalo yakhe yeMalika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅମୃତ ଭକ୍ଷି ଯାଇପାରେ ପ୍ରାଣ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ନୋହିବ ଆନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Amṛita bhakṣhi jāipāre prāṇa,
Achyuta bachana nohiba āna.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngisho nokungenzeki okufana nokulahlekelwa impilo esikhundleni sokuthola ukungafi ngokuphuza i-nectar (Amṛta) kungenzeka, kodwa amazwi ka-Achyutananda Das ngeke abe amanga.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku-'Udaya Kahani' yakhe:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଆନ ନୋହିବ,
କହିଲେ ଅପ୍ରାଧ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପାଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Achyuta bachana āna nohiba,
kahile aparādha nishche pāiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, amazwi ka-Achyutananda Das ngeke abe amanga. Noma ubani obiza la mazwi ngamanga ngokuqinisekile uzoba umhlanganyeli walelo cala.

UMahapurush ubhala ku-'Chakada Madana':

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୋ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗାଇଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହଇ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mo shrīmukha bāṇī anyathā nohiba parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
Nemāla baṭaku laya lagāiṇa dīna Achyutī kahai.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, amazwi ami ngeke abe ngenye indlela, ngisho noma i-lotus ingaqhakaza entabeni! La mazwi abhalwe esesimweni sokuzindla ngaphansi komuthi we-Nemal banyan (Nemal Vaṭa).

Encwadini yeMalika ethi 'Chakada Madana' uqobo, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala futhi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମହୀଫାଟି ଜଳୁପଛେ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଅନଳ,
ଯାହା କହିଛନ୍ତି ତାହା ସର୍ବଦା ଅଚଳ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mahīphāṭi jalupache pralaya anala,
jāhā kahichaṃti tāhā sarbadā achala.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, umlilo oqhuma ngemuva kokuhlukana komhlaba ungadala ukubhujiswa (Pralaya) kuwo wonke umhlaba, kodwa amazwi ami ngeke abe amanga. Amazwi ami awaphikiseki futhi awashintsheki.

2 Inhloso YeMalika – Usindiso Lwabazinikele (bhaktas)

Ngokuvamile, abantu abaningi bayesaba ngemuva kokufunda iziprofetho zeMalika futhi bazichithe njengezinto “zokwesabisa.” Nokho, lokhu kucabanga akulungile neze. Inhloso eyinhloko ukuvikela ngokomoya nokuqondisa abazinikele ngesikhathi sokushintsha kwezikhathi.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ହେତୁ ରସାଇବା ପାଇଁକି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶାହାସ୍ତ୍ର ପୁରାଣ କଲା,
କଳିକାଳଠାରୁ ବଳି କାଳ ଯହିଁ ହକ କଥାଟା ଲେଖିଲା।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Hetu rasāibā pāiṃki Achyuta shāhāstra purāṇa kalā,
kalikālaṭhāru bali kāla jahiṃ haka kathāṭā lekhilā.

Incazelo:
Ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, ukuze avuse ukuqonda okulele kwabazinikele, uqamba le mibhalo yeMalika. Inhloso ukuthi eKaliyuga ejulile, lapho ubumnyama bokungazi bugcwele yonke indawo, abazinikele beNkosi bakwazi ukufinyelela kuMahaprabhu Kalki ngomlomo weMalika, okuvumela ukuhlangana komzinikele noNkulunkulu. Ngenxa yalesi sizathu, ubhala iziprofetho ezinkulu emibhalweni yeMalika kusukela eKaliyuga (Kali Yuga) kuya eSangam Yuga (Isikhathi Sogucuko) naku-Ananta Yuga ezayo (Adi Satyayuga (Ādi Satyayuga)).

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala futhi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅବନୀକୁ ଚେତେଇବା ପାଇଁ ଚେତାବନୀ,
ଅବନୀ ହିତେ ଲେଖା ଏ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Abanīku cheteibā pāiñ chetābanī,
Abanī hite lekhā e bhabishya bāṇī.

Incazelo:
Inhloso yeziprofetho ezibhalwe yimi emibhalweni yeMalika ukunikeza isixwayiso emhlabeni. Uyakucacisa ukuthi lezi ziprofetho zibhalelwe inhlalakahle yomhlaba.

UMahapurush ubhala futhi ku-‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ପୂର୍ବର ବାସନା ମୂଳେ ଜ୍ଞାନ ଉଦୟ ହେଲା,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହିତରେ ମାଳିକା ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଲେଖି ରଖିଲା।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Pūrbara bāsanā mūḷe jñāna udaya helā,
Bhaktanka hitare Māḷikā grantha lekhi rakhilā.

Incazelo:
Ngenxa ye-'vasana' (imikhondo) edlule, okusho ukuzinikela eNkosini kusukela ekuzalweni okwedlule, ulwazi lwafika kimi kulokhu kuzalwa, bese ngiqamba imibhalo yeMalika ukuze kuzuze futhi kube yinhlalakahle yabazinikele.

UMahapurush ubhala futhi ku-‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ସତର୍କ ଘଣ୍ଟି ଯେ ଚେତାବନୀ ଏହି ଜାଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa satarka ghaṇṭi je chetābanī ehi jāṇa.

Incazelo:
Amagama emibhalo ye-Malika enzelwe ukuxwayisa; wazi ukuthi asesimweni sezixwayiso.

U-Mahapurush wabuye wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଏହି ଚେତାବନୀ ପୁଣ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ଭକ୍ତ ହିତେ ପୁଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Sāraswata grantha ehi chetābanī puṇa,
bāraṇa beḷe prakāsha bhakta hite puṇa.

Incazelo:
Amavesi e-Malika asesimweni sezixwayiso kubantu. Abhalwe ngenhlalakahle yabazinikele ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga. Ngakho-ke, kufanele kucace kithi ukuthi i-Malika ayisakazi ukwesaba, kodwa kunalokho ivula indlela yensindiso. Inhloso eyodwa ye-Bhavishya Malika yinhlalakahle yomhlaba.

2.1 I-Malika akuyona eyabo bonke abantu

Kufanele sazi kahle ukuthi i-Malika akuyona eyabo bonke abantu. Ama-Panchasakha Mahapurushas awenqabele abafundi bawo emibhalweni ye-Malika ukuba basakaze la magama phambi kweziwula. U-Mahapurush wathi lokhu akuyona eyabo bonke abantu; akubona bonke abantu abazokwazi ukuwaqonda. Abazinikele beNkosi kuphela abazokwazi ukukholelwa kuwo.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe we-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ କଳିପୁଣି ଆଗତ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ,
ଭକତ ହିତରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲି ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଭଣି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Lakshe pañchāashī grantha kaḷi puṇi āgata bhabishyabāṇī,
Bhakata hitare prakāsha karili dīna Achyuta je bhaṇi.

Incazelo:
Ngaqamba imibhalo engu-1,85,000 isiyonke futhi ngaphandle kwalokho, ngabhala iziprofetho eziningi ezizayo. Ngishicilela konke lokhu ukuze kuzuze abazinikele.

U-Achyutananda Das bese etshela umfundi wakhe u-Ramadas embhalweni wakhe we-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଏ ପରମତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ମୂର୍ଖକୁ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ତତେ ସପ୍ତବାର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି କହଇ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ତିନି ଅଠର ମାଳିକା କଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ରଖିଛି ଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଜନଙ୍କ ପୀୟୂଷ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shuṇa Rāmachandra e paramatattwa mūrkhaku na kara prakāsha,
tate saptabāra niyama deuchi kahai dīna Achyuta.
Lakshe pañchāashī tini aṭhara mālikā kali prakāsha,
bāraṇa beḷaku e grantha rakhichi jñānī janaṅka pīyūṣa.

Incazelo:
Lalela, mfundi Ramadas, la ngamagama ayimfihlo. Ungawatsheli phambi kweziwula, ngaphandle kwalokho uzokwenza isono. Le mibhalo isesimweni sezixwayiso kubantu ezizoba njengoju lwabazinikele ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala ku-‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ହୋଇବ ଡାକିନୀ ଗୋଳ ପଡ଼ିବ ଚହଳ,
ଅବନୀ କମ୍ପିବ ସଦା ହୋଇ ଟଳଟଳ।
ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ମାନ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Hoiba ḍākinī goḷa paḍiba chahaḷa,
Abanī kampiba sadā hoi taḷataḷa.
Nānā utpāta māna antarikṣe heba,
Bhakta binā anya kehi jāṇi na pāriba.

Incazelo:
Ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga, ngesikhathi kusungulwa i-Dharma, kuzokwenzeka impi esabekayo, futhi kuzoba nesiphithiphithi eMhlabeni. Umhlaba uzothuthumela. Kuzokwenzeka iziphazamiso eziningi emkhathini. Kodwa ngaphandle kwabazinikele, akekho omunye ozokwazi ukuqonda ukuthi lezi yimisebenzi engcwele yokucekela phansi yeNkosi.

U-Mahapurush utshela umfundi Ramadas embhalweni wakhe we-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାବୁରାମ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ କହିବୁ ନାହିଁ,
ଘୋର କଳିକାଳ ଟାହି ଯେ ଟାପରା ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା ହେବ ତହିଁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shuṇa Bāburāma Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhanku kahibu nāhiñ,
Ghora Kaḷikāḷa ṭāhi je ṭāparā bānā nindā heba tahiñ.

Incazelo:
O mfundi Ramadas, ungawatsheli amagama ale mbhalo ‘Chakada Madana’ phambi kweziwula. E-Kaliyuga ejulile, iziwula zizowahleka usulu, okuzoholela ekugxekeni kweNkosi.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabuye wabhala embhalweni wakhe we-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇଟି ବିଷ ଜ୍ଞାନୀଙ୍କୁ ଅଟେ ପିୟୂଷ,
ଅଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲେ ମୋ ଅମୃତେ ହେବ ବିଷ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mūrkhanku e grantha aṭaiṭi biṣa jñānīnku aṭe piyūṣa,
Ajñānī āgare prakāsha karile mo amṛute heba biṣa.

Incazelo:
Eziwuleni, la magama e-Malika anjengobuthi, kanti kwabahlakaniphile anjenge-'Piyush' noma uju. Ukutshela la magama kwabangazi kufana nokuxuba ubuthi oju.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala futhi ku-‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କହୁଛି ଏହି ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଅଟଇ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଏହା ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ଖେଳ ହୋଇବ ତୋତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଗଣ୍ଠିଆ ଭକତ ଅଟଇ ଗଣ୍ଠି କଥାରେ ମନ,
ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ ପୁରାଣ।
ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ହୋଇବ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକତ,
ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଦାସରେ ମୋର ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shuṇa Rāmachandra kahuchi ehi niṣṭhā bachana,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa aṭai bhaktaṅka gaṇṭhidhana.
Bāraṇa beḷaku rakhichi ehā bhaktaṅka pāiṃ,
Gupatare kheḷa hoiba tote deli chetei.
Gaṇṭhiā bhakata aṭai gaṇṭhi kathāre mana,
Sātasha bāstarī parārddha bhabishyata purāṇa.
Dharma saṃsthāpana hoiba bhakta hebe mukata,
Shuṇa Rāmachandra dāsare mora bachana satya.

Incazelo:
Lalela mfundi Ramadas, la magama ami e-Malika anjenge-'Ganthidhana' noma ingcebo eyigugu kubazinikele. INkosi izosungula i-Dharma futhi abazinikele bazokhululwa. Le misebenzi engcwele izokwenzeka ngendlela eyimfihlo kakhulu. Ngenxa yensindiso yabazinikele kuphela lapho ngiqamba khona le mibhalo ye-Malika. Izobasiza e-‘Barana Bela’ noma ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabuye wabhala embhalweni wakhe we-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗେଇ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଖେଦା ଲେଖଇ।
ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇ ଏହି,
ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ।
ଉତ୍ତମ ଶିଷ୍ୟରେ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Nemāḷa baṭaku laya lagei,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa khedā lekhai.
Sāraswata grantha aṭai ehi,
Beḷaku rakhichi bhaktaṅka pāiñ.
Uttama shiṣyare kara prakāsha,
Bānā nindā kahe Achyuta Dāsa.

Incazelo:
Ngaphansi komuthi we-Banyan wase-Nemalo, ngibhala le mbhalo ye-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’. Izosiza abazinikele ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga. Yembule kuphela phakathi kwabazinikele abahamba phambili; ngaphandle kwalokho, kuzoba nokugxekwa kwala magama neNkosi.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala futhi ku-‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗି ଅଲଗାରେ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଭକ୍ତ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ ଜାଣ,
ଭକତ ଜାଣିବେ ଏହାର ଯେ ଭେଦ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଅଛି ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ପ୍ରକାଶ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟ ଯେ ସାଧୁ ପଦାଶ୍ରିତ ଭଣେ ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Lāgi alagāre Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa bhakta gaṇṭhidhana jāṇa,
bhakata jāṇibe ehāra je bheda shūnyare achi pramāṇa.
Bāraṇa beḷare e grantha prakāsha mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
Nemāḷa baṭa je sādhu padāshrita bhaṇe pāmara Achyuta.

Incazelo:
I-Chakada Madana iyigugu kubazinikele. Ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga, abazinikele kuphela abazokwazi ukuqonda izakhi zayo nezimfihlakalo zayo. Ungayembuli phambi kweziwula.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala ku-‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚେତୁଆ ଭକତମାନଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ତିନି ଅଠର ଖଣ୍ଡ,
ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଲେଖିଲା ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଦୁରୁଦଣ୍ଡ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chetuā bhaktamānaṅka nimante tini aṭhara khaṇḍa,
Bhabishya lekhilā pāmara Achyuta na jāṇanti durudaṇḍa.

Incazelo:
U-Mahapurush uqambe imiqulu engu-318 yeziprofetho ze-Malika ngenhlalakahle yabazinikele abavukile kuphela. Iziwula nezoni ngeke zikwazi ukwazi imfihlakalo yale mibhalo.

Wabuye wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ବିନାଶି ପ୍ରଭୁ ମେଦିନୀ ରଖିବେ,
ସନାତନ ଧର୍ମ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପ୍ରଚାର କରିବେ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନରହରି,
ନାମ ଭଜଏ ଘୋର କଳିରେ ଯିବ ତରି।
ବିପଦ କାଳରେ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ରକ୍ଷା ପାଇଁ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ରଚଇ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mlechha bināshi Prabhu medinī rakhibe,
Sanātana Dharma nishche prachāra karibe.
Kaḷiyuga sheshare prakāsha Narahari,
Nāma bhajae ghora kaḷire jiba tari.
Bipada kāḷare bhaktaṅka rakṣā pāiñ,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa grantha Achyuta rachai.

Incazelo:
Ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga, iNkosi izozalwa. Izocekela phansi ababi nezoni ukuze ivikele uMhlaba futhi izophinde isungule i-Sanatana Dharma. U-Mahapurush wabhala ukuthi ngaleso sikhathi, ukuze aqondise abazinikele futhi abavikela, uqamba imibhalo ye-Malika. Kuleli vesi, u-Mahapurush wabhala ngokucacile ukuthi inhloso yokuqanjwa kwe-Malika ukuvikela abazinikele ngezikhathi zenhlekelele nokuvula indlela yensindiso yabo.

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ngokuqhubekayo embhalweni wakhe we-Malika, 'Chakada Madana':

I-Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ପାପ ନାଶନ,
ଖେଦା ଚେତାବନୀ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଜ୍ଞାନ।
ସବୁ ଲେଖିଗଲି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଭକ୍ତ ମୋହର ଯେ ସୋଦର ଭାଇ।
ଭକତ ମୋ ପଞ୍ଚ ପ୍ରାଣଟି ଜାଣ,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ଖେଦା ଲିଖନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dharma saṃsthāpana pāpa nāshana,
khedā chetābanī bhabishya jñāna.
Sabu lekhigali bhaktanka pāiṃ,
bhakta mohara je sodara bhāī.
Bhakata mo pañcha prāṇaṭi jāṇa,
bhaktanka nimaṃte khedā likhana.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iziprofetho engizibhalile emibhalweni yami ye-Malika mayelana nokuphela kwezono nokusungulwa kwe-Dharma ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga, zonke ezenzelwe abazinikele ngoba abazinikele banjengabafowethu. Abazinikele bayimiphefumulo yami emihlanu (Pancha-prana (Pancha-prana)), futhi ngibhale yonke le mibhalo ye-Malika ngenxa yabo kuphela.

U-Mahapurush wabuye wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଅଟଇ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଜାଣ,
ଘୋର କଳିଯୁଗ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନ ଛନ୍ତି ଡର ଲାଗୁଅଛି ପୁଣ।
ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନ କର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି ମୁହିଁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Sātasha bāstarī parārddha aṭai Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa jāṇa,
ghora kaliyuga duṣhṭajana chaṃti ḍara lāguachi puṇa.
Mūrkhanka āgare prakāsha na kara niyama deuchi muṃhi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, mfundi Rama, kukhona ama-Parardhas angu-772 noma izahluko kule mbhalo ye-Malika ebizwa ngokuthi 'Chakada Madana'. Ungazembuli phambi kweziwula. Kukhona abantu abaningi ababi e-Kaliyuga esabekayo; yigcine kude nabo bonke.

Ku-'Chakada Madana' uqobo, u-Mahapurush ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନୋହିଲା ନୋହିଲା ପଡୁଥିବ ହୁରି ଭକ୍ତ ଜାଣିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shuṇa Rāmadāsa Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
nohilā nohilā paḍuthiba huri bhakta jāṇiba abashya.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, O mfundi Ramadasa, ungazembuli lezi ziprofetho ze-Malika phambi kweziwula. Bazothi la magama angamanga, ukuthi lezi zigameko ngeke zenzeke. Ngakho-ke, batshele abazinikele kuphela; bona kuphela abazokwazi ukukholelwa kulokhu.

3 Iziprofetho Ze-Malika Ezifezekile

I-Bhavishya Malika iqukethe amagama ka-Mahaprabhu uqobo, ngakho-ke akumangalisi ukuthi iziprofetho ezingenakubalwa ku-Malika zifezekile kuze kube manje. Sizoxoxa nawe ngezinye zeziprofetho ezinjalo ezibonakale ziyi-100% ziqiniso.

3.1 Isiprofetho Esiphathelene No-Atal Bihari Vajpayee Ji

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala emibhalweni yakhe ye-Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ kanye ‘Achyuta Thara’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ ଯେବେ, କଳ୍କୀଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା ଲାଗିବ ତେବେ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ରାଜା, ଫେରିବ କିଛିତାହାର ତରିଜା।
କିଛି ଦିନ ଭୋଗ କରିବ ମହୀ, ତେର ଦିନ ସେହି ଛତ୍ର ଉଡ଼ାଇ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇଲେ ଯେବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଯେ ରାଜା, ତାଙ୍କ ସମୟରେ କିଛି ହୋଇଲା ତରିଜା।
ତେରଦିନ ଛତ୍ର ସେହି ଉଡ଼େଇଣ ଦେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ମହୀ ଭୋଗ କଲେ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
ପ୍ରଥମେ ତେର ଦିନ ପ୍ରଧାନ ମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ ହେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ଶାସନ କରିଲେ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ ଶକତି କାହାର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Tera dina pakṣa hoiba jebe, Kaḷkīṅka līḷā lāgiba tebe.
Khetratre hoiba Brāhmaṇa rājā, pheriba kichhitāhāra tarijā.
Kichhi dina bhoga kariba mahī, tera dina sehi chhatra uḍāi.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Khetratre hoile jebe Brāhmaṇa je rājā, tāṅka samayare kichhi hoilā tarijā.
Teradina chhatra sehi uḍeiṇa dele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa mahī bhoga kale.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Prathame tera dina pradhāna mantrī hele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa shāsana karile.
Achyuta bachana aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ shakati kāhāra.

Incazelo:
Emakhulwini eminyaka adlule, u-Mahapurush wabhala ngosopolitiki obalulekile wase-India ezimele. Wathi lapho um-Brahmin ebusa i-India, izinguquko eziningi zizobonakala emhlabeni. Okokuqala, uzobusa izinsuku ezingu-13, bese kamuva uzobamba amandla iminyaka emihlanu. U-Mahapurush wabhala ukuthi igama lalo muntu obusa e-Delhi lizoba u-"Atal Bihari Vajpayee". Njengoba umlando ukhombisa, u-Shri Vajpayee wasebenza njengoNdunankulu izinsuku ezingu-13 ngo-1996 bese kuba isikhathi esigcwele seminyaka emi-5 kusukela ngo-1999 kuya ku-2004.

3.2 Isiprofetho Esiphathelene No-Mahatma Gandhi

U-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, eminyakeni engu-600 edlule embhalweni wakhe odumile ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’, wenza isiprofetho esibanzi mayelana no-Mahatma Gandhi.

I-Odia Sloka:

ଜନମ ହୋଇ ଗୁଜରାଟରେ, ମୋହନ ମୋହିବ ରାମ ନାମରେ।
ଅହିଂସାକୁ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର କରିବ ଜାଣ, ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ ହଟିବେ ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣ।
ତା ଅନ୍ତେ ଧର୍ମ ହୋଇବ ହାଣି, ମୋହନ ରାଜୁତି ହୋଇବ ହାଣି।
ଗୁଳି ଚୋଟେ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଯିବ, ଗଡସେ କଳିମଞ୍ଜି ପୋତିବ।
ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବେ ସଂସାର ପ୍ରାଣୀ, ଟେକି କହୁଥିବେ କଥା ନ ଜାଣି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Janama hoi Gujarāṭare, Mohana mohiba Rāma nāmare.
Ahinsāku astra kariba jāṇa, phiriṅgī haṭibe se kāḷe jāṇa.
Tā ante dharma hoiba hāṇi, Mohana rājuti hoiba hāṇi.
Guḷi choṭe tāra prāṇa jiba, Gaḍse kaḷimañji potiba.
Jāṇi na pāribe saṃsāra prāṇī, ṭeki kahuthibe kathā na jāṇi.

Incazelo:
"Uzozalelwa esifundazweni sase-Gujarat. Igama lakhe lizoba u-'Mohan' futhi uzohlala ehlabelela igama lika-Rama. 'Ahimsa' (Ukungabi nodlame) kuzoba isikhali sakhe, futhi ngenxa yemizamo yakhe, ama-British (Firingis (Firingis)) azophoqeleka ukuba ashiye i-India."

UMahapurush waphinde wabhala ukuthi uMohan ngeke abambe amandla yena uqobo, futhi ngemuva kokufa kwakhe, kuzoba nokwehla kweDharma. Waphawula ngokukhethekile ukuthi umuntu ogama lakhe lingu-"Godse" uzombulala nge-"inhlamvu" (Guli), futhi ngokwenza kanjalo, uzohlwanyela imbewu yeKali esizweni. Waze wabikezela ukuthi abantu abaningi bazohluleka ukuqonda uGandhi kahle futhi bazothethelela isenzo sombulali ngenxa yokungazi. Njengoba sazi, wonke amazwi alesi siprofetho mayelana noBaba Wesizwe agcwaliseka.

3.3 Isiprofetho Sesiphepho Esikhulu sika-1999 kanye Nokuphuka kweKalpavata

Kumbhalo wakhe 'Jai Birachit Chautisha', uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabikezela isiphepho sika-1999 eminyakeni engu-600 ngaphambi kwesikhathi.

I-Odia Sloka:

ସମୁଦ୍ର ଉଠି ଆସିବ ବତାସ ବହିବ, ଶ୍ରୀ କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଭାଙ୍ଗି ପକାଇବ।
ସତ୍ୟଭାବ ହେ, ସାଲ ଅନେଶତ କର ଠାବ ହେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Samudra uṭhi āsiba batāsa bahiba, Shrī Kaḷpabaṭa mora bhāṅgi pakāiba.
Satyabhāba he, sāla aneshata kara ṭhāba he.

Incazelo:
"Ulwandle luzokhuphuka futhi umoya onamandla uzovunguza. Igatsha leKalpavata elingcwele (umuthi omdala weBanyan (Banyan) ngaphakathi kwesakhiwo sethempeli laseJagannath) lizophuka." UMahapurush wathi lesi sigameko sizokwenzeka ngonyaka "Anesata" (99).

Ngo-Okthoba 29, 1999, Isiphepho Esikhulu sashaya i-Odisha ngemimoya engu-250 km/h. Sadala ukubhujiswa okukhulu, sathatha izimpilo ezingaphezu kwezi-10,000. Ngokwesiprofetho, igatsha elikhulu lomuthi omdala weKalpavata ngaphakathi kwethempeli lasePuri (Puri) laphuka ngesikhathi salesi siphepho.

3.4 Isiprofetho Sokuzimela KweNdiya (1947)

Kumbhalo 'Garuda Kalpa', Usanta Achyutananda wabikezela ukuthi iNdiya izokhululeka ekubusweni kwamaNgisi ngo-1947 esebenzisa ikhodi yezinombolo.

I-Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ଭକ୍ତି ବେଦ ବାରକୁ ଥୋଇ, ଏ ଠାରେ ବିଦେଶୀ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Brahma bhakti beda bāraku thoi, e ṭhāre bideshī jibe paḷāi.

Ukuchazwa Kwezinombolo:
UMahapurush wasebenzisa amagama angokomfanekiso ezinombolo:
  • Brahma: Kumelelwa u-1 (Njengaku-"Ekam Brahma" (Ekam Brahma)).
  • Bhakti: Kumelelwa u-9 (Izinhlobo eziyisishiyagalolunye zeNavadha Bhakti (Navadha Bhakti)).
  • Veda: Kumelelwa u-4 (AmaVeda (Veda) amane).
  • Baar: Kumelelwa u-7 (Izinsuku eziyisikhombisa zesonto).

Uma zibekwe ndawonye (1-9-4-7), zakha u-1947. Isiprofetho sithi ngalo nyaka, abafokazi (amaNgisi) bazobaleka kuleli zwe.

3.5 Isiprofetho Mayelana Nokubulawa Kwezingane Zesifazane Esibelethweni

Ku-'Jaiphula Malika', uMahapurush wabikezela ububi bomphakathi bokuphela kweKaliyuga:

I-Odia Sloka:

କନ୍ୟା ରତ୍ନକୁ ଗର୍ଭର, ନଷ୍ଟ କରିବ ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ହୃଦୟେ ନ କରି ପାପ ବିଚାର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kanyā ratnaku garbhara, naṣṭa kariba tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
hṛudaye na kari pāpa bichāra.

Incazelo:
"Abantu bazobhubhisa ingane yentombazane (Kanya Ratna) esibelethweni (Garbha) ngokuphindaphindiwe ngaphandle komuzwa wesono ezinhliziyweni zabo." Lokhu kubonisa ngokunembile isikhathi esimnyama sokubulawa kwezingane zesifazane esibelethweni esihlukumeze umphakathi emashumini eminyaka adlule.

3.6 Isiprofetho Sobhubhane Lwango-2020 (COVID-19)

UMahapurush Hadidas, ukuzalwa kabusha kwamaPanchasakhas owazalwa ngekhulu le-18, wabhala ku-'Agata Bhavishyat Chautisha':

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ବିଂଶ ଯେବେ ବର୍ଷ ବୋଲାଇବ, ବହୁ ବିତିପାତ ହେବ।
ଅଜଣା ବ୍ୟାଧିରେ ବହୁ ନାଶ ଯିବେ, ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dui biṃsha jebe barsha bolāiba, bahu bitipāta heba.
Ajaṇā byādhire bahu nāsha jibe, hāhākāra paḍijiba.

Incazelo:
"Lapho kufika unyaka 'Dui-Binsha' (Okhishumi-Nambili / 2020), kuzoba neziphazamiso ezinkulu. Abantu bazofa ngenxa yesifo esingaziwa (Ajana Vyadhi) futhi kuzoba nokukhala kokuphelelwa ithemba (Haahakaar) yonke indawo." Lokhu kuyincazelo enembile ngokumangalisayo yobhubhane lwe-COVID-19 olwabamba umhlaba ngo-2020.

3.7 Isiprofetho Sedamu laseHirakud

Kumbhalo 'Chakada Madana' kanye 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa', Osanta (Saints) Achyutananda no-Arkhit Das babikezela ukwakhiwa kwedamu elikhulu e-Odisha.

I-Odia Sloka:

ଛବିଶ ଠାବରେ ହୀରାକୁଦ ଉଛୁଳିବ, ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଅର୍ଦ୍ଧଭାଗ ଜଳମଗ୍ନ ହେବ।
ଧନଜନ ନଷ୍ଟ ହେବ କହିଲି ବୁଝାଇ, ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ବେଶୀ ବେଳ ନାହିଁ।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲିଣଟି ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକାକାର ହେବ।
ସେହି ବେଳେ ଗଙ୍ଗା ପୁଣି ଆସିବେଟି ମାଡ଼ି, ବୈତରଣୀ, ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣୀ ଯେ ଦେବେ ଘୋର ରଡ଼ି।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ବଡ଼ ବନ୍ଧ ତିଆରି ଯେ ଥିବ, ସେଇବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁଦ୍ୱାରା ଭାଙ୍ଗିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ଛଅ ଜିଲ୍ଲା ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇ, ଯିବାପାଇଁ ବାଟ ନ ପାଇବେ ପୁଣି କେହି।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲି ଗ୍ରାମର ଯେ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chabiṣha ṭhābare Hīrākuda uchuḷiba, e Oḍiśhā arddhabhāga jaḷamagna heba.
Dhanajana naṣhṭa heba kahili bujhāi, dīna Achyuta kahai beśhī beḷa nāhiñ.
Hīrākuda boliṇaṭi grāma nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu ekākāra heba.
Sehi beḷe Gaṅgā puṇi āsibeṭi māḍi, Baitaraṇī, Brāhmaṇī je debe ghora raḍi.
Oḍiśhāre baḍa bandha tiāri je thiba, seibeḷe śhatrudwārā bhāṅgiṇa je jiba.
Oḍiśhāre chha-a jillā jaḷārṇnaba hoi, jibāpāiñ bāṭa na pāibe puṇi kehi.
Hīrākuda boli grāmara je nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu eka je hoiba.

Incazelo:
UAchyutananda Das ji embhalweni wakhe weMalika 'Chakada Madana' kanye nosanta (saint) weBrahma Avadhut Arakhita Das, owazalwa engxenyeni yesibili yekhulu leshumi nesishiyagalombili futhi wenza i-sadhana ephelele emhumeni wase-Olashuni, embhalweni wakhe weMalika 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa' wabhala ukuthi kuzokwakhiwa idamu elikhulu esifundazweni sase-Odisha elizobizwa ngokuthi "Hirakud". Waphinde wathi ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, ngokuhlasela kwezitha, lelo damu lizophuka, okuzokwenza izifunda eziyisithupha zesifundazwe sase-Odisha zigcwale amanzi, ezokuthutha zizoma, futhi kuzoba nokulahlekelwa okukhulu kwengcebo nabantu. AmaMahapurusha athi ngaleso sikhathi iGanga izobonisa isimo sayo esesabekayo futhi nemifula iBaitarani, iBrahmani, njll. nayo izodala umonakalo omkhulu.

Njengoba kwaziwa, ngonyaka ka-1948 esifundazweni sase-Odisha eNdiya, kwaqala umsebenzi wokwakha idamu elibizwa ngeHirakud, elaqedwa ngonyaka ka-1953. Ngenyanga kaJanuwari yonyaka ka-1957, nguNdunankulu wangaleso sikhathi uPandit Jawaharlal Nehru, kwenziwa ukuvulwa okusemthethweni kwaleli damu. Ngobude obuphelele obungu-26 km, elakhiwe phezu komfula iMahanadi esifundeni saseSambalpur e-Odisha, leli damu namuhla liyidamu elide kunawo wonke emhlabeni elakhiwe ngabantu. Ngale ndlela, ukunemba kwengxenye yokuqala yalesi siprofetho kwafakazelwa.

Ngokushesha umhlaba uzobona ingxenye yesibili yaleso siprofetho nayo igcwaliseka lapho, ngesikhathi sempi yaseNdiya neChina, ngokuhlasela ngemicibisholo kweChina, leli damu lizophuka futhi isifundazwe sase-Odisha sizohlushwa isikhukhula esesabekayo.

3.8 Isiprofetho Sokuzalwa KwePakistan Emakhulwini Eminyaka Ngaphambi Kwesikhathi

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das nabanye bakhuluma ngokudalwa negama lePakistan eminyakeni engu-600 edlule emibhalweni efana ne-'Gupta Kheda Malika' kanye ne-'Bhavishyat Malika'. Ngokufanayo, usanta wekhulu le-18 u-Arkhit Das wakukhuluma ku-'Mahagupta Padmakalpa'.

I-Odia Sloka:

ପାକିସ୍ତାନର ଦଶା ଯାହା ହେବ ସହସା, ମନ କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦଇ ଥରେ ଶୁଣ ସେ ଭାଷା।
ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ତାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ, ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପ୍ରାଚ୍ୟ ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ଗଣ ଏହିପରି ମିଳିଣ, ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ଲାଗି ସର୍ବେ କରିବେ ରଣ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟଗୋଟି ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ହାହାକାର ଶୁଭୁଣ ଯେ ଥିବ।
ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ, ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟ ଗୋଟା ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ପଛ ବେଳକୁ ତ ପୁଣି ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଯେ ସ୍ଫୁରିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Pākistānara dashā jāhā heba sahasā, mana karṇṇa dei thare shuṇa se bhāṣā.
Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākistānaṭi raṅge, mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Prāchya pāshchātya gaṇa ehipari miḷiṇa, Pākistāna lāgi sarbe karibe raṇa.
Pākistāna rājyagoṭi chhārakhāra heba, chāriāḍe hāhākāra shubhuṇa je thiba.
Chīna Pākistāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa, Ruṣiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmana Jāpāna.
Pākistāna rājya goṭā chhārakhāra heba, pachha beḷaku ta puṇi buddhi je sphuriba.

Incazelo:
"Izwe elibizwa ngePakistan lizovela ngokungazelelwe. Ezikhathini zokuphela, iNdiya izobhekana nempi enamandla nePakistan. Amandla aseMpumalanga Ephakathi naseNtshonalanga azohlangana; Izizwe zamaSulumane, amazwe aseNtshonalanga, iChina, neMelika zizosekela iPakistan, kanti iRussia, iJalimane, neJapane zizosiza iNdiya. I-Pakistan izobhujiswa ngokuphelele (Chara-khara) kule mpi, futhi phakathi kokuphelelwa ithemba komhlaba wonke, ekugcineni izobona amaphutha ayo."

Kuyafaneleka ukuqaphela ukuthi igama elithi 'Pakistan' laqanjwa ngo-1933 kuphela, kodwa laba santa balibiza emakhulwini eminyaka ngaphambili.

3.9 Iziprofetho Mayelana NedoLobha laseBhubaneswar

Isisekelo seBhubaneswar yanamuhla sabekwa ngo-1948 nguNdunankulu uNehru. Nokho, ukukhulunywa ngayo njengenhloko-dolobha kuvela emibhalweni yeMalika efana ne-'Chakada Madana' kanye ne-'Kali Agata Bhabishyant'.

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭୈରବୀ ଡାକିବ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର, ଚୈତ୍ର ମାସ ଯେ ମଙ୍ଗଳବାର।
ପୃଥିବୀରେ ଗୋଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଆରମ୍ଭିବ ଯେବେ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସୈନ୍ୟ ମାଡ଼ି ଯେ ଆସିବେ।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ, ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ତ ପୁଣି ରାଜଧାନୀ ଜାଣ, ବିଦେଶୀ ଶତ୍ରୁଙ୍କ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି ପଡ଼ିବଟି ପୁଣ।
ଭୈରବ ଡାକ ହେବ ଜାଣ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରଟି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhairabī ḍākiba Bhubaneshwar, Chaitra māsa je Maṅgaḷabāra.
Pṛuthibīre goḷa yuddha ārambhiba jebe, Bhubaneshwaraku sainya māḍi je āsibe.
Bhubaneshwarare lāgiba goḷa, Ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Bhubaneshwar ta puṇi rājadhānī jāṇa, bideshī shatruṅka dṛuṣṭi paḍibaṭi puṇa.
Bhairaba ḍāka heba jāṇa, Bhubaneshwarati pramāṇa.

Incazelo:
Osanta babikezela ukuthi ngoLwesibili ngenyanga kaChaitra, kuzozwakala umsindo omkhulu (ukubhonga kukaBhairavi (Bhairavi)) eBhubaneswar. Ngesikhathi seMpi Yezwe Yesithathu, amabutho ezitha azofika eBhubaneswar (Ekamra Bana). Imibhalo yakhuluma ngokusobala ukuthi iBhubaneswar izoba inhloko-dolobha kanye nendawo okuhloswe ngayo izitha zangaphandle.

3.10 Isiprofetho Mayelana Nomholi Ongokomoya Odumile uShri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, emibhalweni yakhe yeMalika eyabhalwa eminyakeni engaba ngu-600 edlule, wabikezela ukuzalwa kwe-"Yugapurusha" (Umuntu Wesikhathi) ozokwenza izenzo eziningi zaphezulu futhi udumo lwakhe luzosakazeka emhlabeni wonke.

I-Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳାମାନ ହେବ କୀର୍ତ୍ତି ରହି ଯିବ, ଅନୁକୂଳ ନାମ ଧରି।
ଦେଶ ବିଦେଶରୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିବେ, ସତସଙ୍ଗ ଆଶ୍ରା କରି।
ସତନାମ ଗୋଟି ଉଚ୍ଚାରି ବୋଲିବେ, ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହେବ ମେଳ।
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ମାର୍ଗେ ରହି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦେଖିବ, ଯୁଗପୁରୁଷଙ୍କ ଖେଳ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Līḷāmāna heba kīrtti rahi jiba, Anukūḷa nāma dhari.
Desha bidesharu bhakata āsibe, sat-saṅga āshrā kari.
Satanāma goṭi uchchāri bolibe, bhaktaṅka heba meḷa.
Shūnya mārge rahi Achyuta dekhiba, Yugapuruṣaṅka kheḷa.

Incazelo:
UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi usanta ogama lakhe lingu-"Anukul" uzovela, futhi abazinikele abavela eNdiya nakwamanye amazwe bazofika bezofuna indawo yokukhosela ku-"Satsanga" yakhe. Igama elingcwele (Satnaam) lizohlabelelwa, futhi kuzoba nomhlangano omkhulu wabazinikele (devotees). UAchyutananda Das waphinde wathi yena uqobo, ehlala endaweni ecashile/engenalutho (Shunya Maarga), uzofakaza izenzo zaphezulu zale Yugapurusha.

Egcwalisa lesi siprofetho, u-Anukulchandra Chakravarty wazalwa ngoSepthemba 14, 1888, esifundeni sasePabna (Pabna) saseBengal esingahlukanisiwe (manje esiyiBangladesh (Bangladesh)). Owaziwa emhlabeni wonke njengoShri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra, wasungula i- 'Satsang Ashram' eDeoghar, eJharkhand, ngo-1946. Izigidi zabazinikele emhlabeni wonke zajoyina umsebenzi wakhe, zimkhuluma njengo-'Yuga Purushottam'.

3.11 Ukukhuluma ngeBhomu Lenuzi (Paramanu Bomba) Emibhalweni yeMalika

Ngenkathi ibhomu lenuzi lalithuthukiswa maphakathi nekhulu lama-20, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das (ku-'Gupta Kheda Malika' (Gupta Kheda Malika) naku- 'Bhavishyat Malika') noBalarama Das (ku-'Kali Tandaba Gita' (Kali Tandaba Gita)) balikhuluma emakhulwini eminyaka adlule.

I-Odia Sloka:

ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା କରିଦେବେଟି ଜମା, ପୋଡ଼ିଜାଳି ଦେବା ପାଇଁ ଭାରତ ସୀମା।
ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା ଯାହା ଲାଗି ଗାରିମା, ଦେଖାଇ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ଆଜି ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ସେନା।
ତାହା ଫୁଟିବ ନାହିଁ କେଣେ ଯିବ ମିଳାଇ, ଏହା ଦେଖି ବିଦେଶୀଏ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।
ଅଗ୍ନିର ଦାହିକା ଶକ୍ତି ଲୋ ବଉଳ ପ୍ରଭୁ କରିବେ ହରଣ, ପରମାଣୁ ବୋମା ଯାହାଲାଗି ଗାରିମା ଶୀତଳ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Paramāṇu je bomā karidebeṭi jamā, poḍijāḷi debā pāiñ Bhārata sīmā.
Paramāṇu je bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā, dekhāi huanti āji pāshchātya senā.
Tāhā phuṭiba nāhiñ keṇe jiba miḷāi, ehā dekhi bideshīe jibe paḷāi.
Agnira dāhikā shakti lo bauḷa Prabhu karibe haraṇa, paramāṇu bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā shītaḷa hoiba jāṇa.

Incazelo:
Abangcwele babhala ukuthi ngesikhathi seMpi Yesithathu Yomhlaba, izitha zaseNdiya zizokwakha amasu okubhubhisa imingcele yaseNdiya zisebenzisa Amabhomu Enuzi. Nokho, uhlelo lwazo luzohluleka ngoba iNkosi Kalki izomunca amandla avuthayo/abhubhisa (Dahika Shakti) alawa mabhomu. Izikhali kanye namabutho aseNtshonalanga nawesitha aziqhenya ngawo azoba "banda" (angasebenzi) futhi angabi namthelela emhlabathini waseNdiya (India). Zibona lesi senzo esingenakwenzeka, izitha zangaphandle zizobaleka ngesaba. Imibhalo iyaxwayisa ukuthi impi yenuzi esabekayo (Anu-Paramanu Yuddha) izogcina yenzekile, yenze umhlaba ungahlaleki, okuzobonwa abazinikele.

3.12 Ukukhuluma ngoMadan Mohan Malaviya, uMahatma Gandhi, noMotilal Nehru

Wazalwa ngo-1850, umngcwele omkhulu uBhima Bhoi (umlandeli wesiko lamaPanchasakhas (Panchasakhas)) wanikeza imininingwane ngabaholi bomzabalazo wenkululeko waseNdiya embhalweni wakhe 'Padmakalpa'.

I-Odia Sloka:

ମଦନମାଲବ୍ୟ ନାମ ବୋଲିକରି ଏକ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବ ଜନ୍ମି,
ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ସହିତରେ ଅହିଂସା ଧର୍ମରେ ନିରନ୍ତରେ ଥିବେ ପୁଣି।
ଚୁଡ଼ଙ୍ଗ ବଂଶରେ ଉତପତ୍ତି ହୋଇ ମୋତିଲାଲ ଯେ ନେହେରୁ,
ମହାପ୍ରତାପୀ ଅଟଇ ସେହୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିଛନ୍ତି ଶୂନ୍ୟପୁରୁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Madanamālabya nāma bolikari eka bhakta thiba janmi,
Gāndhī sahitare ahinsā dharmare nirantare thibe puṇi.
Chuḍaṅga baṃshare utpatti hoi Motilāl je Neheru,
mahāpratāpī aṭai sehu bhakta āsichanti Shūnyapuru.

Incazelo:
Umbhalo wabikezela ukuzalwa kozinikele ogama lakhe lingu-"Madan Mohan Malaviya" ozohola, kanye no-"Mahatma Gandhi", umzabalazo osuselwe enkolweni yokungabi nodlame (Ahimsa). Ngaphezu kwalokho, wakhuluma ngokuzalwa kozinikele onethonya elikhulu futhi onamandla u-"Motilal Nehru". Bobathathu baba abaholi ababalulekile be-Indian National Congress nomzabalazo wenkululeko.

3.13 Isiprofetho Sokubulawa kukaMahatma Gandhi nguNathuram Godse

Ku-'Jaiphula Malika', uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabikezela ukufa kukaMahatma.

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୋହନ ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ମରଣ, ନାଥୁରାମ ହସ୍ତେ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏ କଥା ସିଦ୍ଧ ଫଳ ରେ ନିପୁଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mohana Gāndhī maraṇa, Nāthurāma haste hoiba jāṇa lo jāiphula,
e kathā siddha phaḷa re nipuṇa.

Incazelo:
"Ukufa kukaMohan Gandhi (Mahatma Gandhi) kuzokwenzeka ezandleni zikaNathuram." Njengoba umlando uqopha, ngoJanuwari 30, 1948, uNathuram Vinayak Godse wabulala uMahatma Gandhi.

3.14 Ukuzimela kweNdiya Ngaphansi Kobuholi bukaMahatma Gandhi

Ku-'Kali Bhagabata', uMahapurush Abhiram Paramahansa (umngcwele wekhulu lama-20 esikweni leMalika (Malika)) wachaza ushintsho lwezepolitiki.

I-Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟାଗ୍ରହୀ ହୋଇଲେ ମେଳି ।
ଅନ୍ତର ହୋଇଯିବ କଳି ।।
ଇଉରୋପିଆନ ଯିବେ ଛାଡ଼ି ।
ମୋହନ ସେନା ଦେବେ କାଢ଼ି ।।
ପ୍ରଥମ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ସେନାପତି ।
ହେବେ ମୋହନ ସୈନ୍ୟ ଘେତି ।।
ମୋହନ ବୋଲି ଯାଙ୍କୁ କହି ।
ମହତ ଆତ୍ମା ଅଟେ ସେହି ।।
ସେହି ନାରଦ ମହାମୁନ୍ୟ ।
ତାଙ୍କୁ କରନ୍ତି ସର୍ବେ ମାନ୍ୟ ।।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Satyāgrahī hoile meḷi,
antara hoijiba kaḷi.
Iuropiāna jibe chhāḍi,
Mohana senā debe kāḍhi.
Prathama yuddhe senāpati,
hebe Mohana sainya gheti.
Mohana boli jāṅku kahi,
mahata ātmā aṭe sehi.
Sehi Nārada mahāmunya,
tāṅku karanti sarbe mānya.

Incazelo:
Umngcwele wabikezela ukuthi ngokuqoqana kwamaSatyagrahis, abaseYurophu bazophoqeleka ukuhamba. Wakhomba u-"Mohan" (Gandhi) njengomkhuzi/umholi walo mzabalazo omkhulu wokuqala wenkululeko. Waphinde wachaza uMahatma Gandhi njengomphefumulo omkhulu (Mahatma) nokuzalwa kabusha kukaNarada, amazwi akhe ayeyohlonyishwa yibo bonke. Lokhu kwagcwaliseka njengoba ubuholi bukaGandhi baba amandla abalulekile aholela amaNgisi ukuba asuke emhlabathini waseNdiya.

4 Iminyaka Ye-Kaliyuga

Ngokwemibhalo yeSanatan, isikhathi sonke seKaliyuga yiminyaka engu-432,000. IBhavishya Malika nayo iqinisekisa lesi sikhathi sonke, kodwa yengeza isiprofetho esibalulekile: ngenxa yezono eziningi zesintu, iKaliyuga ngeke isiqede isikhathi sayo esigcwele.

4.1 Umyalo Wombhalo weminyaka engu-432,000

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku-'Adi Samhita':

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳିରେ ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯାଏ, ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନିର୍ଣ୍ଣୟ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷire chāri lakṣa jāe, batiśa sahasra nirṇaya.

Incazelo:
"EKaliyuga, iminyaka eyizinkulungwane ezingamakhulu amane namashumi amathathu nambili (432,000) inqunywe njengesikhathi sayo sokuphila."

Bese uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe, 'Dashapatala Malika':

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଭୋଗ ପ୍ରମାଣ ସାର,
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ହଜାର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaliyuga bhoga pramāṇa sāra,
chārilakṣha je batisha hajāra.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, isikhathi sokuphila esinqunyiwe seKaliyuga yiminyaka eyizinkulungwane ezingamakhulu amane namashumi amathathu nambili.

Ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika, 'Parardha Jataka':

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶି ସହସ୍ର କଳି,
କରିଲି ତିନି ଯୁଗଠାରୁ ବଳି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chārilakṣha batishi sahasra kali,
karili tini yugaṭhāru bali.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, isikhathi sokuphila seKaliyuga yiminyaka eyizinkulungwane ezingamakhulu amane namashumi amathathu nambili.

Nokho, amaMahapurushas abazange bayeke ngemuva kokusho lokhu nje; baphinde basho emibhalweni yabo yeMalika ukuthi ngenxa yezono ezahlukahlukene ezenziwa abantu, iKaliyuga ngeke ikwazi ukuqeda isikhathi sayo esigcwele.

4.2 Kungani iKaliyuga izophela ngaphambi kwesikhathi

AmaPanchasakhas ayacacisa ukuthi nakuba iminyaka yethiyori ingu-432,000, i-"Bhoga" (isikhathi sangempela esizobhekana naso) siyoba sifushane kakhulu ngenxa yesisindo sezono. Ku-'Dashapatala Malika', kubhalwe ukuthi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର, ଭୋଗ ନୋହିବ କହ ପୀତବାସ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra, bhoga nohiba kaha Pītabāsa.

Incazelo:
"O Nkosi Pitabasa (Jagannath (Jagannath))! Sitshele ukuthi iKaliyuga ngeke ikwazi kanjani ukubhekana nesikhathi sayo esigcwele seminyaka engu-432,000 ."

UMahapurush ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika, 'Kalikalpa Gita':

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ବର୍ଷ ଆଉ,
ଭୋଗ ନହେବ ପାପଭାରା ଯହୁଁ।
ଆପଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଉଦିତ,
ପାପୀମାନଙ୍କୁ କରିବେ ମୁକତ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chāri lakṣha batisha sasra barṣha āu,
bhoga naheba pāpabhārā jahuṃ.
Āpaṇe prabhu hoibe udita,
pāpīmānaṃku karibe mukata.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngenxa yomthwalo omkhulu wezono eKaliyuga, iKaliyuga ngeke ikwazi ukubhekana nesikhathi sayo sokuphila seminyaka engu-4,32,000. Ngisho nangaphambi kwalesi sikhathi, iNkosi izozalwa kabusha, izoni zizobhujiswa, neDharma izosungulwa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala futhi kweminye yemibhalo yakhe yeMalika:

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ,
ପାପ ଯୋଗୁଁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kali chāri lakṣha batisha sasra nohiba bhoga,
pāpa joguṃ kṣhaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iKaliyuga ngeke ikwazi ukubhekana nesikhathi sayo esinqunyiwe seminyaka engu-4,32,000. Ngenxa yezono, isikhathi sayo sokuphila sizobola, okusho ukuthi, ngeke ikwazi ukubhekana naso ngokuphelele.

4.3 Uma kungewona iminyaka engu-4,32,000, pho siyini isikhathi sokuphila esibhekana naso seKaliyuga?

Ezinsukwini zanamuhla, abanye abantu bazibandakanya ekusakazeni, bekhomba iMalika, ukuthi isikhathi sokuphila esibhekana naso seKaliyuga yiminyaka engu-10,000. Makwaziwe ukuthi lokhu akulungile neze.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦଶ ସହସ୍ରରେ କଳିନାଶ ହେବ ଏ ଅଟେ ବିଷମ ବାଣୀ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dasha sahasrare kalināsha heba e aṭe biṣhama bāṇī.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iKaliyuga izophela eminyakeni engu-10,000 – lokhu kuyisitatimende esingahambisani noma esingamanga.

AmaPanchasakha Mahapurushas abhale ezindaweni eziningi emibhalweni yabo yeMalika ukuthi isikhathi sokuphila esibhekana naso seKaliyuga siyoba iminyaka engu-5,000, emva kwalokho iNkosi Kalki izozalwa kabusha bese kufika iSatya-Kali noma i-Ananta Yuga noma i-Adya Satya Yuga.

Woza, asithole ulwazi olwengeziwe ngalesi sihloko ngasinye ngasinye ngama-slokas eMalika.

Okokuqala, asazi ukuthi iNkosi Shri Krishna Yona uqobo yashoni mayelana nesikhathi sokuphila seKaliyuga.

4.4 Isikhathi Sangempela: Iminyaka engu-5,000

Ngokuphambene nolwazi olungelona iqiniso olusatshalaliswa abanye lokuthi iKaliyuga ihlala iminyaka eyi-10,000, imibhalo yeMalika iphinda iphinde ithi ukubusa kwangempela kukaKali (Kali) kuphela ngemuva kweminyaka engu-5,000, kulandele isikhathi soguquko.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ucaphuna amazwi eNkosi uKrishna kuGaruda encwadini ethi 'Agata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika':

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ, ପାପ ଯୋଗୁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେ ପଚାଶ ଭୋଗ ହେବ ନିକର, ଏଥୁ କିଛି ଉଣା ହୋଇବ ଶୁଣ ଗରୁଡ଼ ବୀର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷi chāri lakṣa batiśa sahasra nohiba bhoga, pāpa jogu kṣaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.
Pāñcha sahasra je pachāsha bhoga heba nikara, ethu kichi uṇā hoiba shuṇa Garuḍa bīra.

Incazelo:
"O Garuda onesibindi! Lalela, iKaliyuga ngeke ihlale iminyaka engu-432,000. Ngenxa yesono, ubude bayo buzokwehla. Izophela cishe ngemuva kweminyaka engu-5,050.

4.5 Umfanekiso kaRavana

I-Odia Sloka:

ରାମଦାସ ବୋଲଇ ହୋ ଶୁଣ ଗୁରୁଦେବ, କଳିଯୁଗ କେତେ ପୁଣ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ଯେ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ, କେମନ୍ତେ ଯେ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ହୋଇବଟି ଶେଷ।
ଏଡ଼େ ବଡ଼ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ସଂଶୟ ଫେଡ଼ିଣ ମୋତେ କହ ଗୁରୁଦେବ।
ରାମଦାସ ବାଣୀ ଶୁଣି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହନ୍ତି, ତୋତେ କହୁଅଛି ରାମ ଶୁଣ ଦେଇ ମତି।
ରାବଣ ଯେ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ ତପ କଲା, ଛପନ ଗଣ୍ଡା ଯୁଗକୁ ବର ସେ ପାଇଲା।
ପାପେ ଗଲା ତାର ତିନି ଭାଗ ଯେ ବିନାଶ, ଏକଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସ୍ୱର୍ଗେ ପଶି ଅପସରୀ ମାନଙ୍କୁ ହରିଲା, ପର ଦ୍ରବ୍ୟ ହରି ଯେବେ ପାଦେ ଖଟାଇଲା।
ଆପେ ଜଗତମାତା ଯେ ଜନକ ନନ୍ଦିନୀ, ତାଙ୍କୁ ହରି ନେଲା ଗର୍ବେ ମୂଢ଼ ବିଂଶପାଣି।
ସୁରାପାନ କଲା ସେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିଙ୍କୁ ବଧିଲା, ବ୍ରହ୍ମହତ୍ୟା ଆଦିକରି କେତେ ପାପ କଲା।
ପାପରୁ ଯେ ତିନି ଭାଗ ହୋଇଲା ବିନାଶ, ଏକ ଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସେହିପରି କଳିଯୁଗ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ପାଞ୍ଚସସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅନ୍ୟ ପାପେ ଯିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Rāmadāsa bolai ho shuṇa gurudeba, kaliyuga kete puṇa bhoga je hoiba.
Chārilakṣha batisha je sahasra baraṣha, kemaṃte je yuga puṇa hoibaṭi sheṣha.
Eḍe baḍa yuga puṇa bināsha hoiba, saṃshaya pheriṇa mote kaha gurudeba.
Rāmadāsa bāṇī shuṇi Achyuta kahaṃti, tote kahuachi Rāma shuṇa dei mati.
Rābaṇa je tretayā yugare tapa kalā, chapana gaṇḍā yugaku bara se pāilā.
Pāpe galā tāra tini bhāga je vināsha, ekabhāga bhoga kalā Bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Swarge pashi apasarī mānaṃku harilā, para drabya hari jebe pāde khaṭāilā.
Āpe jagatamātā je Janaka naṃdinī, tāṃku hari nelā garve mūḍha biṃshapāṇi.
Surāpāna kalā se kṣhatriṃṃku badhilā, brahmahatyā ādi kari kete pāpa kalā.
Pāparu je tini bhāga hoilā bināsha, eka bhāga bhoga kalā bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Sehipari kaliyuga bināsha hoiba, pāṃchasasra bhoga heba anya pāpe jiba.

Encwadini ethi 'Kalpatika', uAchyutananda Das uchaza lesi simo esebenzisa isibonelo sikaRavana. URavana wayenze ukuzila okukhulu futhi wathola ubude besikhathi esingama-"56 Ganda" (isikhathi esikhulu sasemkhathini). Nokho, ngenxa yokuthi wathumba uMama uSita, wahlambalaza izintombi zasezulwini, futhi wenza izono ezimbi njengeBrahman-hatya, wajabulela ingxenye eyodwa kuphela yesibusiso sakhe, kanti ezinye izingxenye ezintathu zabhujiswa izono zakhe.

Ngokufanayo, ubude beKaliyuga obuyiminyaka engu-432,000 "budliwa" ukwanda okusheshayo kwesono somhlaba wonke, okuholela ekupheleni kwayo ngaphambi kwesikhathi ngemuva kweminyaka engu-5,000 yesikhathi esihlangenwe naso, kuvula indlela ye-Ananta Yuga noma iSatya Yuga.

4.6 Ubudala beKaliyuga: Ukuhlaziya Okuningiliziwe

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala komunye wemibhalo yakhe yeMalika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗର ଅଟଇ ଆୟୁଷ।
ପାପଭାରରେ ଆୟୁ କଟିଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra,
Kaḷiyugara aṭai āyuṣa.
Pāpabhārare āyu kaṭijiba,
Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi bhoga hoiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ubudala beKaliyuga buyiminyaka engamakhulu amane namashumi amathathu nambili (432,000), kodwa ngenxa yethonya lezono, lobu budala buzofushaniswa futhi buzophela eminyakeni eyizinkulungwane ezinhlanu (5,000) kuphela.

Ukuzalwa kabusha kukaMahapurush Achyutananda Das, owazalwa ngekhulu le-18 esifundeni saseJajpur e-Odisha, uNgcwele uMahapurush Hadi Das waxoxa ngobudala beKaliyuga embhalweni wakhe weMalika ogama lakhe lingu-‘Kali Chautisha’. Wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ସଂବତ୍ସର ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗ ଆଦ୍ୟ ହୋଇବ ଶୁଭଯୋଗେ ପ୍ରବେଶ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Saṃbatsara pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hoiba sheṣa,
Satya yuga ādya hoiba shubhajoge prabesha.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ku-"Sambatsara Paancha Sahasra" (iminyaka engu-5,000), iKaliyuga izophela, futhi ngemuva kwalokho, i-Adya Satya Yuga izoqala ngesikhathi esihle.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala komunye umbhalo weMalika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ଯେ ହଜାର ଥାରୁ ମହାଭୟ ହେବ,
ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସମ୍ରାଟକୁ ଆସି ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଗାନ୍ଧାର ସେନାଟି ଗୋଳ କରିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର ପଚାଶ କଳିର ଆୟୁଷ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Pāñcha je hajāra thāru mahābhaya heba,
Dillī samrāṭaku āsi bipada paḍiba.
Gāndhāra senāṭi goḷa kariba abashya,
Pāñcha hajāra pachāsha Kaḷira āyuṣa.

Incazelo:
UMahapurush ubhala lapha futhi ukuthi ngemuva kweminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga, kuzoba nomoya wokwesaba okukhulu; izinhlekelele zizowela phezu koMbusi waseDelhi (Delhi) noma uNdunankulu wezwe. Ibutho laseGandhara (Taliban) ngokuqinisekile lizolwa ne-India. UMahapurush uphinde abhale ukuthi konke lokhu kuzokwenzeka ngemuva kokuphela kweKaliyuga, okungukuthi, ngemuva kokuba sekudlule iminyaka engu-5,050 yeKaliyuga.

UAchyutananda Das ji (Achyutananda Das) naye wabhala ivesi embhalweni wakhe weMalika lapho engazange asho ubudala beKaliyuga ngokuqondile njengeminyaka engu-5,000 noma engu-5,050, kodwa wakusho ngokungaqondile ngamacebo:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶେ ମସିହା ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କରିବେ କଳି ବିନାଶ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Biṃshe masiha hoiba sheṣa,
Ananta karibe Kaḷi bināsha.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kuma-'20s'—kusukela ngonyaka ka-2000 kuya ku-2099—ngesikhathi esithile phakathi nalesi sikhathi, iKaliyuga izophela. INkosi u-Ananta izobhubhisa iKaliyuga eMhlabeni.

Omunye wamaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, wachaza ubudala beKaliyuga ngokuningiliziwe embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Aagata Bhavishya Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବୋଲଇ ଶୁଣିମା ଗୋସାଇଁ କୁହ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବିଚାର,
କେତେବେଳେ କଳ୍କୀ ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ଶୁଣଇଁ ମୁଖୁ ତୁମ୍ଭର।
ଶିଶୁ ବୋଲନ୍ତି ହେ ଶୁଣିମା ବାରଙ୍ଗ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ତୁହି,
ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଯେବେ ଯିବ ଭୋଗ ହୋଇ।
ଜେସେନେକେ ନିଶି ପାହିଲେ ପ୍ରଭାତ ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ଏହା ଜାଣ,
ଯେମନ୍ତ ସମୟେ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ସେ ନାରାୟଣ।
ସମକ୍ଷର ବତା ଶୁଣି ଆଣିକରି ପ୍ରମାଣ ଏହାକୁ କର,
ସବୁ ଏକଠାବେ ମିଶାଇ କରିଣ କରିବୁ ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର।
ଏହି ସମୟକୁ ତଳେ କରିଥିବୁ କହଇ ହୋ ବାବୁ ତୋତେ,
ଠିକ ରେ ଏ କଥା ଦେଖାଇ କହିଲୁ ରଖିଥିବୁ ହୃଦଗତେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bāraṅga bolai shuṇimā gosāiṃ kuha bhabiṣya bichāra,
ketebeḷe Kalkī swarūpa hoibe shuṇaiṃ mukhu tumbhara.
Shishu bolanti he shuṇimā Bāraṅga Kaḷaṅki swarūpa tuhi,
yugashandhi pāñcha sahasra baraṣa jebe jiba bhoga hoi.
Jeseneke nishi pāhile prabhāta yugashandhi ehā jāṇa,
jemanta samaye Kaḷaṅki swarūpa hoibe se Nārāyaṇa.
Samakṣara batā shuṇi āṇikari pramāṇa ehāku kara,
sabu ekaṭhābe mishāi kariṇa karibu pāñcha hajāra.
Ehi samayaku taḷe karithibu kahai ho bābu tote,
Ṭhika re e kathā dekhāi kahilu rakhithibu hṛudagate.

Incazelo:
Umfundi uBaranga Das uthi kuMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das—"O Guru Dev, ngitshele ngekusasa. Ngifuna ukuzwa emlonyeni wakho ukuthi iNkosi izovela nini ngefomu layo likaKalki (Kalki)."

Lapho ezwa lokhu, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das utshela umfundi wakhe omkhulu uBaranga ukuthi lapho kwenzeka i-'Yuga-Sandhi' (uguquko phakathi kwezinkathi), kuphela lapho iNkosi izothatha ukuzalwa kabusha kwayo njengoKalki. Echaza kabanzi, uthi le Yuga-Sandhi izokwenzeka lapho iKaliyuga isihlangabezwe iminyaka engu-5,000. Njengoba nje ekuseni kuza ngemuva kobusuku, enye inkathi iza ngemuva kwenkathi eyodwa. Ingxenye ephakathi kwezinkathi ezimbili ibizwa ngeYuga-Sandhi. Futhi kule Yuga-Sandhi uNarayana uzothatha ifomu lakhe likaKalki. Le Yuga-Sandhi izofika ngemuva kokuba sekudlule iminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga.

UAchyutananda ji (Achyutananda), ngenkathi esho ubudala beKaliyuga embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Kaliyuga Malika’, uthi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚହଟିବ ଲୀଳା ତୁ ଚାରିରେ ମିଶା ଏକ,
ଚଢ଼ା ତିନି ଶୂନ ତହିଁ ଯେତେ ହେଲା ଠିକ।
ଚଳିଯିବ ଘୋର କଳି ଦଳି ଦେବେ ମିଳି,
ଚେତାଇ ଗୀତ ଛନ୍ଦଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ସେ ଭାଳି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

chahaṭiba līḷā tu chārire mishā eka,
chaḍhā tini shūna tahiñ jete helā ṭhika.
Chalijiba ghora Kaḷi daḷi debe miḷi,
chetāi gīta chhandai Achyuta se bhāḷi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, engeza okukodwa enombolweni yesine (4+1=5) bese ubeka ama-zero amathathu eduze kwesamba; inombolo etholakele imelela isikhathi lapho isikhathi seKaliyuga esesabekayo sizophela khona. Evesini elingenhla, uMahapurush usho ubudala beKaliyuga ngomfanekiso. Uthi 4+1=5, bese ebeka ama-zero amathathu eduze kuka-5 kusho ukuthi ngemuva kokuba sekudlule iminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga, lobo budala buzophela.

Kwenye incwadi yeMalika ethi ‘Bhavishya Chautisha’, uthi kumfundi wakhe uRamachandra:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅମରପୁର,
ଠାକୁର ତହିଁରୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ, ଠାରି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଧର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ṭhikaṇā Amarapura,
Ṭhākura tahiñru hebe bāhāra, Rāmachandra he,
ṭhāri pāñcha sahasra dhara.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, O mfundi uRamachandra, ukuzalwa kabusha kweNkosi uKalki kuzokwenzeka e-Amarapur (isifunda esingafi—esikhona ngisho nangaphambi kweSatya Yuga (Satya Yuga), okungukuthi, iJajpur), futhi lokhu kuzokwenzeka ngemuva kokuba sekudlule iminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga.

Bese, embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Kali Bayalisha’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ukhuluma ngobudala beKaliyuga ngezimpawu. UMahapurush uthi: Okusho ukuthi, bhala inombolo 3 phezu kwenombolo yamadodana iNkosi uDasharatha eyayinayo (okungukuthi, 4^3 = 64). UMahapurush bese ethi yiguqule bese ukhipha u-11 kuleyo nombolo (Ukuguqula u-64 kunikeza u-46, no-46 - 11 = 35). UMahapurush uthi phindaphinda idijithi esohlangothini lwesokudla sale nombolo etholakele ngo-'Gandiva' (okungukuthi, 1000) bese ebhala ukuthi iKaliyuga izophela enombolweni etholakele noma eminyakeni engu-5,000.
Qaphela: Lapha, ngo-'Gandiva', uMahapurush ubhekisela ebangeni layo lokushaya, elalingama-1,000 Yojanas.

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦଶରଥ ସୁତ ମୁଣ୍ଡରେ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କ ଚଢ଼ିବ,
ବଜ୍ର ଅଙ୍କ ଦେଇ ଏଗାର ଅଙ୍କ ତହୁଁ ଫେରିବ।
ଶେଷ ଡାହାଣ ଭାଗ ଯାହା ହେଲା ଗାଣ୍ଡିବ ଗୁଣ,
କଳି ଶେଷ ଭୋଗ ଏହିଟି କହିଲି ଅରଜୁନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dasharatha suta muṇḍare tini aṅka chaḍhiba,
bajra aṅka dei egāra aṅka tahuṃ pheriba.
Sheṣa ḍāhāṇa bhāga jāhā helā gāṇḍiba guṇa,
kaḷi sheṣa bhoga ehiṭi kahili Arajuna.

Incazelo:
Incazelo, bhala inombolo 3 phezu kwenani lamadodana iNkosi uDasharatha ayenayo (okungukuthi, 4^3 = 64). UMahapurush bese ethi yiguqule bese ususa u-11 kuleyo nombolo (Ukuguqula u-64 kunikeza u-46, no-46 - 11 = 35). UMahapurush uthi phindaphinda idijithi engakwesokudla yale nombolo etholakele ngo-'Gandiva' (okungukuthi, 1000) futhi ubhala ukuthi iKaliyuga izophela kule nombolo etholakele noma eminyakeni engu-5,000.
Qaphela: Lapha, ngo-'Gandiva', uMahapurush ubhekisela ebanganini layo lokushaya, elalingu-1,000 Yojanas.

UAchyutananda Das ji ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Adi Sanhita’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଏ ଯେ ସୁବାହୁ ଯୁଗ କଳି,
କ୍ଷୀଣ ଆୟୁଷ ମହାବଳି।
ପାପେ ସକଳ କ୍ଷୟ ଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

E je subāhu yuga kaḷi,
kṣīṇa āyuṣa mahābaḷi.
pāpe sakaḷa kṣaya jiba,
pāñcha sahasra bhoga heba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ubudala beminyaka engu-432,000 yeKaliyuga buzofinyela bube iminyaka engu-5,000 kuphela ngenxa yezono.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଭୋଗ ହେବ,
ସେହି ଦିନୁ ସତ୍ୟକଳି ପ୍ରକାଶ ପାଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷi pāñchasahasra baraṣa bhoga heba,
sehi dinu Satyakali prakāsha pāiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, isipiliyoni seKaliyuga sizoba iminyaka engu-5,000. Ngemuva kwalokho, i-'Satyakali'—ukukhanya kwe-Adya Satya Yuga ngaphakathi kweKaliyuga—kuzovela.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାରରେ ଗଙ୍ଗାଦେବୀ ମେଳ କଟକ ଠାରେ,
ଭକ୍ତ ଦେଖିବେ ନେତ୍ରରେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷi pāñchahajārare Gaṅgādebī meḷa Kaṭaka ṭhāre,
bhakta dekhibe netrare.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngemuva kokuba sekudlule iminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga, uNkulunkulukazi uGanga uzodlula eCuttack; okusho ukuthi, yonke into kusukela eKolkata kuya eCuttack izocwila; kuzoba nesikhukhula esesabekayo.

UMahapurush wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହଟେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ,
ମୋର ତଥ୍ୟମାନ ସେତେବେଳେଟି ହୋଇବ।
ପୂର୍ବ ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଆବର ଦକ୍ଷିଣ,
ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେବେ କଳଙ୍କି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hāte satya upujiba,
mora tathyamāna setebeḷeṭi hoiba.
pūrba pashchima uttara ābara dakṣiṇa,
prakāsha hebe Kaḷaṅki Achyuta bachana.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngemuva kokuba sekudlule iminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga, iKaliyuga izophela futhi iQiniso (Satya) lizofika. Bese, ukukhanya kweNkosi uKalki kuzovela kuzo zonke izinhlangothi—Empumalanga, ENtshonalanga, ENyakatho, naseNingizimu.

UNgcwele uAchyutananda Das ubhala futhi embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ସପ୍ତ ବିଂଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଆୟୁଷ ହେବ ବିନାଶ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରଟି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ,
ସତ୍ୟଯୁଗ ପୁଣି ଉଦୟା ହେବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chāri lakṣa sapta biṃsha sahasra,
Kaḷiyuga āyuṣa heba bināsha.
pāñcha sahasraṭi bhoga hoiba,
Satyayuga puṇi udayā heba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iminyaka engu-427,000 izobhujiswa eminyakeni engu-432,000 yobudala beKaliyuga; iminyaka engu-5,000 kuphela ezohlangabezwa, ngemuva kwalokho iSatya Yuga izoqala.

Akukhona nje uMahapurush Achyutananda Das kuphela, kodwa nomunye wamaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, naye waxoxa ngobudala beKaliyuga emibhalweni yakhe ehlukahlukene yeMalika. Ngenkathi echazela umfundi wakhe uBaranga embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Chumbaka Malika’, wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଏବେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ଠିକ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବିଚାର ଚିତ୍ତରେ ଘେନ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ebe pāñcha ṭhika kahibā shuṇa,
Bāraṅga bichāra chittare ghena.
pāñcha sahasra jetebeḷe heba,
sampūrṇa līḷā prakāsha hoiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lalela ngokucophelela mfundi uBaranga, bamba inombolo 5. Lapho sekudlule iminyaka engu-5,000, kuphela lapho izenzo ezingcwele (Leelas) ephelele yeNkosi izovela.

UMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wabhala futhi embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Chumbaka Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଗଲେ,
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଜନମ ଯେ ହୋଇବେ ଭଲେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷiyuga pāñcha sahasra gale,
Viṣṇu janama je hoibe bhale.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngemuva kokuba sekudlule iminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga, ukuzalwa kabusha kweNkosi uVishnu kuzokwenzeka.

Echaza ukuthi iKaliyuga izophela ngemuva kokuba sekudlule iminyaka eyizinkulungwane ezinhlanu futhi imidlalo yeNkosi izokwenzeka, uMahapurush Balarama Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ହସ୍ତିନା କଟକ ଯେବଣ ଭୂଇଁ,
ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସହର ସେହି।
ପୂର୍ବର ଚରିତ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ଦେବାପି ଅଛନ୍ତି ତପସ୍ୟାରେଣ।
ଉତ୍ତର ଖଣ୍ଡେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରନ୍ତି,
ତପସ୍ୟା ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ହେ ହୋଇ ନାହାନ୍ତି।
ପଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରେ ବରଷ ପୁଣ,
ତପସ୍ୟା ତାଙ୍କର ହୋଇବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ।
ବାରୁଣା କୁଳେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଭେଟିବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଶ୍ରୀହସ୍ତେ ସିରୀପା ବାନ୍ଧିବେ।
ଶ୍ରୀପଦ୍ମ ହସ୍ତ ତା ମୁଣ୍ଡେ ଲଦିବେ,
ହସ୍ତିନା ରାଜ୍ୟ ନଗ୍ରରେ ସ୍ଥାପିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Hastinā kaṭaka jebaṇa bhūiṃ,
bartamāna Dillī sahara sehi.
Pūrbara charita kahibā shuṇa,
Debāpi achhanti tapasyāreṇa.
Uttara khaṇḍe tapasyā karanti,
tapasyā pūrṇa he hoi nāhānti.
Pañcha sahasre baraṣa puṇa,
tapasyā tāṅkara hoiba pūrṇa.
Bāruṇā kuḷe prabhuṅku bheṭibe,
Prabhu shrīhaste sirīpā bāndhibe.
Shrīpadma hasta tā muṇḍe ladibe,
Hastinā rājya nagrare sthāpibe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, umhlaba owawuyiHastinapur Cuttack yidolobha lanamuhla laseDelhi. Lalela indaba yakudala—uDevaapi umatasa ngokuzila. Wenza ukuzila emzaneni waseKalapa esifundeni sase-Uttarakhand e-India, okuzophela ngemuva kokuba sekudlule iminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga. Ngemuva kokuba ukuzila sekuphelile, uzohlangana neNkosi. INkosi izombeka umqhele ngezandla zayo ezinjengama-lotus futhi imenze iNkosi yaseHastinapur noma iDelhi.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ethi ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦାରୁ ଚଢ଼ି ଦାରୁରୂପ ତେଜିକରି କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହୋଇବ,
ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ ମୋହର ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳିରେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଘଟିବ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରରେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):
