Dāru chaḍhi dārurūpa tejikari Kalkī rūpa hoiba,
niṣṭhā bachana mohara Rāmachandra Kaḷire nishche ghaṭiba.
āhuri kathāe shuṇa Rāmadāsa Kaḷi pāñcha sahasrare.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngemuva kweminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga idlulile, iNkosi uJagannatha izoshiya i-'Daaru-rupa' yayo noma ifomu likaJagannatha bese ithatha ifomu likaKalki.

Kulawo mavesi angenhla, kucacile ukuthi iminyaka esidlulile yeKaliyuga ingamashumi amahlanu kuphela. Akuyona iminyaka engu-432,000 noma iminyaka engu-10,000.

Kodwa umbuzo obalulekile uvela lapha— Njengoba sazi, iminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga isivele yadlula futhi okwamanje kuqhubeka unyaka wama-5,127. Ngokwalokhu kubala, iKaliyuga bekufanele ukuba isiphelile manje futhi i-'Adya Satya Yuga' (Ananta Yuga (Ananta Yuga)) bekufanele ukuba isiqhubeka! Kodwa ithonya leKaliyuga lisabonakala yonke indawo. Isono nokungalungi kugcwele yonke indawo. Kungani lokhu kuphikisana?

Impendulo imi kanje— Okokuqala, iminyaka engu-5,000 isitatimende esijwayelekile, hhayi esinembile ngokuqinile. Iminyaka engu-5,000 isho cishe iminyaka engu-5,000. Lesi yisizathu sokuthi ezindaweni eziningi, kubhalwe neminyaka engu-5,050.

Okwesibili, ngokweMalika, isikhathi seKaliyuga sesiphelile futhi okwamanje, i-'Sandhikala' (isikhathi soguquko) seKaliyuga neSatya Yuga siyaqhubeka. NgokweMalika (Malika), kungalesi Sandhikala lapho kuzokwenzeka khona izigameko zokuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki nokusungulwa kweDharma yiYo—okungukuthi, ukubhujiswa kwababi nokusindiswa kwabazinikele. Yingakho isono nokungalungi kusezingeni eliphezulu naphezu kokuphela kwesikhathi seKaliyuga. Kufanele kwaziwe ukuthi lapho ukusungulwa kweDharma sekuqediwe, ithonya leKaliyuga liyophela ngokuphelele futhi iQiniso, iDharma, noBulungiswa kuzophinde kusungulwe.

U-Achyutananda Das uphinde wabhala emibhalweni yakhe yeMalika mayelana nokuthi lokhu kusungulwa kweDharma kuzokwenzeka nini ngaphansi kobuholi beNkosi uKalki.

4.7 Ukuhlangana kweMeena-Shani

UMahapurush wabhala encwadini yakhe ethi ‘Aagata Bhavishyat Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲେ,
ଠ’ ତିନି ବାମେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ରଖିଲେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ,
ଠକିଯିବ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭଲେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ṭhikaṇā Achyuta bole,
ṭha’ tini bāme pāñcha rakhile Rāmachandra he,
ṭhakijiba mīna shani bhale.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, uma ubeka oziro abathathu nenombolo 5 phambi kwabo (5,000), kusho ukuthi ngemuva kweminyaka engu-5,000 yeKaliyuga idlulile, kuzokwenzeka ukuhlangana kweMeena-Shani (uShani (Shani) eMeena), okuzoba okubaluleke kakhulu.

5 Meena-Shani Yoga; NgokweMalika (Malika)

5.1 Yini iMeena-Shani?

Ekufundweni kwezinkanyezi, uShani (uSaturn) uthathwa njengeplanethi ehamba kancane kakhulu, ehlala esibonakalweni esisodwa sezinkanyezi cishe iminyaka engu-2.5. Kunezibonakaliso zezinkanyezi eziyi-12 esiyingini sezinkanyezi, futhi uShani uhlala kusona ngasinye cishe iminyaka engu-2.5. Lokhu kusho ukuthi uShani uthatha cishe iminyaka engu-12 × 2.5 = 30 ukuze adlule kuso sonke isiyingi sezinkanyezi.

Ake sithi uShani ungena esibonakalweni sezinkanyezi namuhla; ubuyela kuleso sibonakaliso esifanayo ngemuva kweminyaka engu-30 ngemuva kokuqeda ukudlula okuphelele. IMeena-Shani isho ukudlula kukaShani (Shani) esibonakalweni sezinkanyezi iMeena (Pisces).

Okokugcina uShani edlula eMeena kwakungo-1995. Njengoba kuthatha cishe iminyaka engu-30 ukuthi uShani abuyele esibonakalweni esifanayo, 1995 + 30 = 2025. Ngakho-ke, ngonyaka ka-2025, uShani uphinde adlulele eMeena futhi. Ngemuva kuka-2025, ukudlula okulandelayo kuzoba ngu-2025 + 30, okungukuthi 2054–2055.

AmaPanchasakha Mahapurushas achaza ngokucacile emibhalweni yawo yeMalika ukuthi ukubhujiswa kwababi, ukuvikelwa kwabazinikele, nokusungulwa kweDharma yiNkosi uKalki kuzokwenzeka ikakhulukazi ngesikhathi seMeena-Shani.

Ukuze sikuqonde kangcono lokhu, ake sibheke amanye amavesi ashiwo eMalika nezincazelo zawo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ethi ‘Prachi Kalpa’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମହୀମଣ୍ଡଳରେ ଚମକ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ନ ଜାଣିବେ ହୀନ ଜନ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀରେ ସଂହାର ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ର ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mahīmaṇḍaḷare chamaka paḍiba,
na jāṇibe hīna jana.
Mlechcha māruṇīre saṃhāra hoiba,
mīna shani ra pramāṇa.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, kuzoba nokukhala okukhulu emhlabeni; kuzoba neziphazamiso yonke indawo. Akekho ngaphandle kwabazinikele ozokwazi ukuqonda lokhu. Kulezi zigameko, abantu abaningi ababi nezoni bazobhujiswa. UMahapurush wathi lezi zigameko zizokwenzeka ngesikhathi seminyaka engu-2.5 yeMeena-Shani Yoga.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦା ପ୍ରମାଣ, ଗୁପ୍ତରେ ଲାଗିବ ସେ ମାୟା ରଣ।
ଥୋକେ ଯିବେ କାମଚଣ୍ଡୀ ମୁଖରେ, ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଗୁପ୍ତରେ।
ତରକ ତେରୁଆ ଯେତେ ଭକତ, ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦାରେ ନାଶ।
ଛିନ୍ନମସ୍ତା ଦେବୀ ଖର୍ପର ପାତି, କାଠମାଳିଆଙ୍କୁ ଖାଇବ ବାଛି।
ଘୋର ଅନ୍ଧକାର ସେବେଳେ ହେବ, ସବୁ ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଜ୍ଞାନ ହଜିଣ ଯିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ନ ମିଳିବ ଶସ୍ୟ ନିଅଣ୍ଟି, ମୀନ ଶନିଆସି ଦେବଟି ଘାଣ୍ଟି।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ଚୁଲି ସେହିଦିନ ଫିଟିବ, ହାଡ଼ିଆ କମାର ପରଣୁ ଥିବ।
ସେଦିନ ଯେ ଗୁରୁବାରଟି ଜାଣ, କାଳିଆ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଅଟେ ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Jobarā Kāḷiābodā pramāṇa,
guptare lāgiba se māyā raṇa.
Thoke jibe Kāmachaṇḍī mukhare, Mlechcha māruṇī je heba guptare.
Taraka teruā jete bhakata, Jobarā Kāḷiābodāre nāsha.
Chhinnamastā debī kharpara pāti, kāṭhamāḷiāṅku khāiba bāchi.
Ghora andhakāra sebeḷe heba, sabu buddhi jñāna hajiṇa jiba.
Anna na miḷiba shasya niaṇṭi, mīna shaniāsi debaṭi ghāṇṭi.
Lakṣe chuli sehidina phiṭiba, hāḍiā kamāra paraṇu thiba.
Sedina je gurubāraṭi jāṇa, Kāḷiā parīkṣā aṭe pramāṇa.

Incazelo:
UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ukuthi lapho uShani eseMeena, ngaleso sikhathi, kuzokwenzeka izivivinyo kubo bonke abazenzisi nabangazinikele eJobra naseKaliaboda eCuttack. Omama Abangcwele bazobhubhisa bonke abangazinikele abanjalo. Ngaleso sikhathi, kuzoba nobumnyama izinsuku eziyisikhombisa nobusuku obuyisikhombisa. Ingqondo yawo wonke umuntu izoyeka ukusebenza. Ngesikhathi leso, izoni ngeke zikwazi nokuthola ukudla.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ethi ‘Kalikalpa Gita’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଅଙ୍କ,
ଭାରତଶ୍ୱରୀ ହେବ ଉତପାତ।
ହେତୁ ରଖିଥାଅ ବ୍ରଜକୁମର,
ସେକାଳେ ତେଜିବି ମୁଁ କଳେବର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna shani heba Mukunda aṅka,
Bhāratashwarī heba utapāta.
Hetu rakhithāa Brajakumara,
sekāḷe tejibi muñ kaḷebara.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, lapho uShani eseMeena, kuzoba neziphazamiso eNdiya. Ngaleso sikhathi, iNkosi Ephakeme izoshiya ifomu layo elijwayelekile lomuntu bese ithatha ifomu likaKalki (Kalki) elinamandla amakhulu.

Kwenye incwadi yeMalika, uMahapurush wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନିର ସଂଯୋଗ ହେବାର ସମୟ,
ପୃଥବୀରେ ହେବ ପୁଣି ଘୋର ଯେ ପ୍ରଳୟ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna shanira saṃyoga hebāra samaya,
pṛthabīre heba puṇi ghora je pralaya.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, ngesikhathi sokuhlangana kweMeena-Shani, kuzophinde kube nokubhujiswa okwesabekayo (Pralaya (Pralaya)) eMhlabeni.

U-Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ୋଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧୃବ।
ଅଢ଼ୋଇ ବରଷ ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ ଛବିଶ ଅଙ୍କ କୁଅନା,
ଶ୍ମଶାନ ପରାୟ ଏ ସାରା ସଂସାର ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ସିନା।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna shani āsi deuthiba ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhōi heba,
dasha aṇā jiba chha' aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.
aḍhāi baraṣa ghora utpāta chhabbisha aṅka kuanā,
shmashāna parāya e sārā saṃsāra uchhanna hoiba sinā.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, ngesikhathi uShani edlula eMeena, iMpi Yesithathu Yomhlaba izoqala eMhlabeni, kuhlanganise neNdiya. Iminyaka emibili nengxenye, kuzobonakala ukubhujiswa okwesabekayo eMhlabeni. Umhlaba wonke uzobhujiswa bese uthatha isimo sendawo yokushiswa kwezidumbu. Ngesikhathi leso, abantu abaningi bazofa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala kwenye incwadi yeMalika ethi ‘Bhabishyata Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ସାବଧାନ ହୁଅ ତୁହି,
ଠା ଠିକଣା କିଛି ରହିବ ନାହିଁ।
ହେ ଭକ୍ତଗଣ! ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସିଗଲେ ଧାଇଁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Sābadhāna hua tuhi,
ṭhā ṭhikaṇā kichhi rahiba nāhiñ.
He bhaktagaṇa! Mīna shani āsigale dhāiñ.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, O bantu, qaphelani. IMeena-Shani Yoga iyeza. Ngaleso sikhathi, kuzofika izinhlekelele eziningi, futhi ukubhujiswa kuzothinta cishe wonke umuntu; akekho ozosindiswa. Ingqondo yabantu izoyeka ukusebenza. Isiphithiphithi sizogcwele yonke indawo.

Ozala ngekhulu le-19, imbongi engcwele uBhima Bhoi wabhala ngalesi sihloko encwadini yakhe iMalika ethi ‘Padmakalpa’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଖିବ ଜଳ ଅବିଳମ୍ବେ,
ମୀନ ଶନିର ମଧ୍ୟଭାଗେ।
ମୋଗଲ ପଠାଣର ଧାଡ଼ି,
ମଧ୍ୟ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଥିବ ମାଡ଼ି।
ସିଂହ ମଙ୍ଗଳ ଗ୍ରହ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି,
କିଛି ମେଦିନୀ ଯିବ ଫାଟି।
ଗିରି କନ୍ଦର ଗୁମ୍ଫାମାନ,
ମୋ ଭକ୍ତେ ରହିବେଟି ପୁଣ।
ଆସିବେ ଚଣ୍ଡୀକାଇଗଣ,
ଭକ୍ଷିବେ ନଗ୍ରରେ ପଶିଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shukhiba jaḷa abiḷambe,
mīna shanira madhyabhāge.
Mogala paṭhāṇara dhāḍi,
madhya Oḍishā thiba māḍi.
Siṃha maṅgaḷa graha dṛṣṭi,
kichhi medinī jiba phāṭi.
Giri kandara gumphāmāna,
mo bhakte rahibeṭi puṇa.
Āsibe chaṇḍīkāigaṇa,
bhakṣibe nagrare pashiṇa.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, ngemuva kwengxenye ephakathi yeMeena-Shani Yoga, imifula nemifudlana izoqala ukoma ngokuphazima kweso. Amabutho amazwe anobutha azongena e-Odisha alwe. Phakathi kwalowo Meena-Shani Yoga ofanayo, lapho uMangala (Mars) eseSimha (Leo), kuzokwenzeka nokuzamazama komhlaba. INkosi izogcina abazinikele bayo bephephile ngokucophelela emihumeni yezintaba. UChandi namaYoginis bazobhubhisa ababi nezoni.

Imbongi ezinikele uNarada Das uphinde wakhuluma ngeziphazamiso ezenzeka ngesikhathi seMeena-Shani Yoga encwadini yakhe iMalika. Wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀଏ ନାଶ ଯିବ ରଙ୍କରେ।
ବତିଶ ତେତିଶ ପୁଣି ଚଉତିରିଶ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ।
ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ ଉଲ୍ଲୋଳ,
ଯୋଗିନୀ ହେବେ ପ୍ରବଳ।
ପଡ଼ି ହାଡ଼ କଙ୍କାଳ,
ଗଡ଼ିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ ମାଳ।
କମ୍ପିବ ଭୈରବ ଡାକରେ,
ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare,
prāṇīe nāsha jiba raṅkare.
Batisha tetisha puṇi chautirisha,
mīna shani bhoga heba aḍhāi baraṣa.
Pṛthabī heba ulloḷa,
yoginī hebe prabaḷa.
Paḍi hāḍa kaṅkāḷa,
gaḍiba muṇḍa māḷa.
Kampiba bhairaba ḍākare,
mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, iMeena-Shani Yoga izohlala isikhathi esiphelele seminyaka emibili nengxenye futhi ngalesi sikhathi, abantu abaningi bazofa. Kuzoba nokukhala okukhulu eMhlabeni. AmaYoginis azoqhuba i-leela yokubhujiswa. Ngalesi sikhathi, izidumbu zizohlakazeka emhlabeni wonke. Umhlaba uzothuthumela ngokubhonga kukaBhairavi (UMama Bhairavi (Bhairavi)).

Ecaphuna ingxoxo phakathi kweNkosi uKrishna noMama Rukmini ngesikhathi seDvapara Yuga, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ethi ‘Rukmini Gita’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ମେଳ ଗୋ ହୋଇବ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ସେବେଳେ ସମର ହେବ ଭାରତ ମଣ୍ଡଳେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna shani meḷa go hoiba jetebeḷe,
sebeḷe samara heba Bhārata maṇḍaḷe.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, ngesikhathi uShani edlula eMeena, kuzokwenzeka impi enkulu eNdiya.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das uphinde wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ପଡ଼ିଲେଟି ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ମନେ ରଖିଥିବୁ ରାମ ତୁହି ବ୍ରଜସୁତ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna shani paḍileṭi ghora utpāta,
mane rakhithibu Rāma tuhi Brajasuta.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, lapho kwenzeka iMeena-Shani Yoga, kuzobonakala iziphazamiso ezinkulu kulo Mhlaba.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala encwadini yeMalika ebizwa ngokuthi ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ତେରଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ ରାଜନ,
ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଗଗନ।
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ କଷଣ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ଚଉଦ୍ୱାରେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ।
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନିକ ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଚଳ,
ନିକଟରେ ଗୋଳ ଚହଳ।
ତପ ସଙ୍ଗେ ତୋପ ପରୀକ୍ଷା,
ସତ୍ୟ ଅନନ୍ତଟି ଦେଖା।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ହୋଇବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Teradina pakṣa hoiba,
mīna shani yoga paḍiba.
Aḍhāi baraṣa rājana,
dibasare tārā gagana.
Bhaktaṅku kaṣaṇa paḍiba,
Chaudwāre yuddha hoiba.
Baijñānika yantra achaḷa,
nikaṭare goḷa chahaḷa.
Tapa saṅge topa parīkṣā,
satya Anantaṭi dekhā.
Ananta yugaṭi hoiba,
Achyutī kāmanā pūriba.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, ngesikhathi sokusungula iDharma, kuzokwenzeka isikhathi esiyizinsuku eziyishumi nantathu (Paksha (Paksha)). Kuzoba neMeena-Shani Yoga ezohlala iminyaka emibili nengxenye. Ngesikhathi leso, izinkanyezi zizobonakala emini. Ngesikhathi leso, abazinikele bazobhekana nobunzima obuningi. Kuzokwenzeka impi eChoudwar eCuttack; imishini yesayensi neyagesi izoyeka ukusebenza. Kuzokwenzeka impi enkulu. Imicibisholo yesimanje nezibhamu zamazwe anobutha zizophendulwa ngamandla okuzila (Tapa (Tapa)) kwabangcwele nabahlakaniphile baseNdiya abaphelele. INkosi uKalki izovela; abazinikele bazothola umbono (Darshan (Darshan)) weNkosi. Iminyaka Engunaphakade (Ananta Yuga (Ananta Yuga)) izofika. Isifiso sika-Achyutananda sizofezeka.

Uphinde wabhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଆକାଶୁ ଖସିବ ମୀନ ଶନିର ରାଜତ୍ୱ,
ବାଡ଼ି ବେମାରିରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dibasare tārā ākāśu khasiba mīna shanira rājatva,
bāḍi bemārire aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, izinkanyezi zizowa esibhakabhakeni emini; abantu abaningi abangenakubalwa bazofa ngenxa yomthelela wezifo nobhubhane. Ngesikhathi leso, i-Meena-Shani Yoga izobe iqhubeka.

UMahapurush u-Achyutananda Das wabhalwa encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି କରିବ ରାଜତ୍ୱ ମାନବ ମାରୁଣୀ ହେବ,
ପରାଧୀନ ହେବ ଏ ଭାରତବର୍ଷ ଶାସନ କଳ ଲାଗିବ।
ଅରାଜକ ହେବ ଏହି ଜମ୍ବୁଦ୍ବୀପ ଭକ୍ତ ହୋଇବେ ଛାନିଆଁ,
ଅନହୁତି ଅଗ୍ନି ପ୍ରଜ୍ୱଳିତ ହେବ ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ସାରା ଦୁନିଆ।
ଛିନିଛତ୍ର ହେବ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳ ଥୟ ନ ପଡ଼ିବେ କେହି,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କୁହଇ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna shani āsi kariba rājatwa mānaba māruṇī heba,
parādhīna heba e Bhāratabarṣa shāsana kaḷa lāgiba.
Arājaka heba ehi Jambudwīpa bhakta hoibe chhāniāñ,
anahuti agni prajwaḷita heba uchhanna sārā duniā.
Chhinichhatra heba Oḍishā maṇḍaḷa thaya na paḍibe kehi,
Chakaḍā maḍāṇa prakāsha kariṇa dīna Achyuta kuhai.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho i-Saturn ingena e-Pisces, isikhathi sokubhujiswa kwesintu sizoqala. Uhlelo lwentando yeningi luzowa, futhi i-India izolahlekelwa ubukhosi bayo—ingase ize ingene ngaphansi kokubusa komunye umuntu. Isiphithiphithi sizogubuzela uhlelo lokuphatha, futhi izwe lizoshona ebushokobhini. Abazinikele (bhaktas) bazokwesaba lapho bebona lezi zenzakalo. Umlilo wokubhujiswa ngeke ugwinye i-Odisha ne-India kuphela, kodwa umhlaba wonke.

U-Achyutananda ubhala encwadini yakhe i-Kalikalpa Gita:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକୁ ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଗମନ ହେଲେ ଏମନ୍ତ ଭିଆଣ,
ଭାରା ନିବାରଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଦେବରାୟ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେ ଉହ୍ଲେଇବେ ଜାଣ।
କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna rāshiku Shanishchara gamana hele emanta bhiāṇa,
bhārā nibāraṇe prabhu debarāya martye uhleibe jāṇa.
Kalkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣatrīmānaṅku nikṣatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ukuze kukhululwe uMhlaba esonweni, iNkosi uMahavishnu izothatha ukuzalwa emhlabeni. Ngesikhathi se-Meena-Shani Yoga, ethatha isimo se-Kalki, iNkosi Ephakeme izohamba ezweni futhi ngokubhujiswa kwababi nezoni, Izokhulula uMama uMhlaba.

UMahapurush ubhalwa encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଭାବ ବିନୋଦିଆ ମୂର୍ତ୍ତିଙ୍କୁ ଭାବ,
ଭାବରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ ଚୁମ୍ବିବ।
ବାଇଆ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଗୀର,
ବାରହାତ ଖଣ୍ଡା ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବିରାଟ ଗାଦୀ ସଂହାର।
ଭକତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ,
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହୋଇ ପୃଥବୀ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ ବିଶେଷ।
ମାୟାରେ ବୁଲେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଣାକାର ହୃଦରେ ଘୋଷ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ଦୃଶ୍ୟ।
ଯୋବରା ଘାଟର ଲୀଳା,
ଜଗିଛି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ କରି ହେଳା,
କଟିବ ପାପୀଙ୍କ ଗଳା।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
bhāba binodiā mūrttiṅku bhāba,
bhābare Achyutī dāsa chumbiba.
Bāiā Achyutī gīra,
bārahāta khaṇḍā heba bāhara,
birāṭa gādī saṃhāra.
Bhakata Achyutī dāsa,
bhūmikampa hoi pṛthabī ushwāsa,
bhakta rahibe bisheṣa.
Māyāre bule Achyuta,
mantra aṇākāra hṛdare ghoṣa,
mīna shani heba dṛshya.
Jobarā ghāṭara līḷā,
jagichhi Achyuta na kari heḷā,
kaṭiba pāpīṅka gaḷā.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, impi esele ye-Mahabharata izokwenzeka. Impi enkulu izokwenzeka e-Virat Gadi noma e-Choudwar yase-Cuttack, futhi inkemba enezandla eziyishumi nambili izobulala izoni. Ukuzamazama komhlaba okukhulu kuzokwenzeka okuzobhubhisa ababi. Izivivinyo zabo bonke abazenzisi zizokwenzeka e-Jobra Ghat yase-Cuttack, futhi uMama Ongcwele uzophuza igazi labo. Ngesikhathi salokhu, abazinikele bazovikelwa. UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi lezi zenzakalo zizokwenzeka e-Meena-Shani Yoga.

U-Achyutananda Das ubhalwa encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ‘Tattvabodhini’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକି ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଯିବ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ନଷ୍ଟ ଭ୍ରଷ୍ଟ କରିଦେବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna rāshiki Shanishchara jiba,
Oḍishā naṣṭa bhraṣṭa karideba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngemuva kokungena kwe-Saturn e-Pisces, isifundazwe sase-Odisha sizolimala.

UMahapurush u-Achyutananda Das ubhalwa futhi encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ଦେଶକୁ ପଡ଼ିବ ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହେବ,
ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ସେଠାବରେ ହେବ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ମନେ ଭାବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna shani yoga deshaku paḍiba tera dina pakṣa heba,
gupata māruṇī seṭhābare heba Rāmachandra mane bhāba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, i-Meena-Shani Yoga izokwenzeka, futhi kuzoba nesonto elinezinsuku eziyishumi nantathu. Ngesikhathi esifanayo, abantu bazofa ngasese, futhi i-Dharma izosungulwa.

Ngokuqhubekayo ku-‘Kalikalpa Gita’, ubhalwa:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚଳିଯିବ କଳି ଗୋଳ ଉପୁଜିବ ବାବୁ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହୋଇଥିବ ଗୁପତ ରଖିବୁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chaḷi jiba kaḷi goḷa upujiba bābu,
mīna shani hoithiba gupata rakhibu.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngesikhathi se-Meena-Shani Yoga, i-Kaliyuga izophela futhi i-Dharma izosungulwa.

UMahapurush u-Achyutananda Das ubhalwa futhi encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଛାରଖାର ହେବ ଏ ସୁନା ସଂସାର ଭଇରବୀ ଦେବ ଡାକ,
ଅକାଳ ମରଣ ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ମୀନ ଶନିଯୋଗ ହେବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chārakhāra heba e sunā saṃsāra bhairabī deba ḍāka,
akāḷa maraṇa gupata māruṇī mīna shaniyoga heba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lomhlaba uzobe "uchithwe" noma ubhujiswe ngokuphelele. UMama uBhairavi uzokhipha ukubhonga okukhulu; izoni eziningi zizobhekana nokufa okungafanele, futhi abaningi bazofa ngasese. Zonke lezi zenzo zokubhujiswa zizokwenzeka ngesikhathi se-Meena-Shani Yoga, okungukuthi lapho i-Saturn ikhona esibonakalweni se-zodiac se-Pisces.

Kusukela emavesini e-Malika anikezwe ngenhla, kuyacaca ukuthi ngesikhathi se-Meena-Shani Yoga, izenzo zokubhujiswa zeNkosi uKalki zizosebenza kakhulu. Ngesikhathi lesi, uMhlaba wonke uzobona izenzakalo ezinkulu nezingakaze zibonwe zenhlekelele. Kuzobonakala kuphela ukukhala, impi, iziyaluyalu, ukuphazamiseka, nezinhlekelele zemvelo yonke indawo. Ngesikhathi lesi, ababi nabangalungile bazobhujiswa kuwo wonke uMhlaba.

5.2 Ku-Meena-Shani iphi izenzo (Leelas) zeNkosi uKalki zizokwenzeka?

Ngemuva kokwazi kusukela emavesini angenhla ukuthi izenzo zokubhujiswa ze-Kalki zizokwenzeka ngesikhathi se-Meena-Shani, kuvela umbuzo omkhulu: ngesikhathi esithile se-Meena-Shani zizokwenzeka nini lezi zenzakalo?

I-Meena-Shani Yoga yenzeka kanye njalo eminyakeni engama-30. Ngakho-ke, kubalulekile ukwazi ukuthi iyiphi i-Meena-Shani, ngokwe-Malika, izobona izenzakalo zokusungulwa kwe-Dharma.

Lapho sibheka eduze, sithola ukuthi i-Saturn yayise-Pisces ngo-1995; njengamanje, ngo-2025, i-Saturn isiphinde yangena e-Pisces futhi; bese ngo-2054, futhi cishe eminyakeni engama-30 ngemuva kwalokho, ngo-2084, ukudlula kwe-Saturn e-Pisces kuzokwenzeka futhi.

Iyiphi i-Meena-Shani?

Kubhalwe ngokucacile ku-Bhavishya Malika ukuthi lapho lezi zenzakalo zenzeka phakathi kweminyaka emibili nengxenye ye-Meena-Shani, igama leNkosi yase-Puri, e-Odisha, ngaleso sikhathi lizoba u-Divyasingha Deva.

UMahapurush u-Achyutananda Das wabhalwa encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ‘Chandra Kalpa’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଥିବ ସେହି ବେଳର,
ପୃଥବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ଘନ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତର ଏ ପ୍ରଳୟ ରାଶି,
ଭୂଭାରାକୁ ରାମ ଯିବ ରେ ଗ୍ରାସୀ।
ସେକାଳେ ମୀନେ ଶନି ଥିବ ଚଲି,
ନାଶିବ କାଳ ହେବ ଜଳ ଖେଳି।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ଗୁରୁ ସ୍ମରିଣ,
ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳ୍ପରେ ମୁଁ କଲି ବଖାଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dibyasiṃha thiba sehi beḷara,
pṛthabī hoijiba ghana andhāra.
pañchabhūtara e pralaya rāśi,
bhūbhārāku rāma jiba re grāsī.
sekāḷe mīne śani thiba chali,
nāśiba kāḷa heba jaḷa kheḷi.
Achyuta kahai guru smariṇa,
chandra kalpare muñ kali bakhāṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho i-Saturn iku-Pisces, ngaleso sikhathi, ubumnyama obukhulu buzogubuzela uMhlaba izinsuku eziyisi-7 nobusuku obuyisi-7. Ukuchithwa okubangelwa izakhi ezinhlanu (uMhlaba, Amanzi, Umlilo, Umoya, ne-Ether (Ether)) kuzogwinya uMhlaba wonke. UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi ngaleso sikhathi, igama leNkosi yase-Puri lizoba ‘u-Divyasingha’.

Manje amanye amaphuzu afanele ukuphawulwa lapha. INkosi yamanje yase-Puri, uShri Divyasingha Deva (IV), njengamanje ineminyaka engama-72 ubudala. Ku-Meena-Shani elandelayo—okungukuthi, eminyakeni engama-30 kusukela manje—iminyaka yakhe izoba ngu-100+ futhi kungenzeka kancane kakhulu ukuthi usazobe ehlezi esihlalweni sobukhosi sase-Puri ngaleso sikhathi!

Manje abanye abantu, abathi isikhathi sezenzakalo yi-Meena-Shani elandelayo (okungukuthi, unyaka ka-2054–2057), basikisela ukuthi kungenzeka ukuthi lezi zenzakalo zenzeka ngesikhathi sokubusa kwenye inkosi egama layo lingu-Divyasingha! Kodwa ake ngikutshele ukuthi lokhu akunakwenzeka. INkosi yamanje yase-Puri iyiNkosi ye-19, futhi ngokwe-Malika, iNkosi ye-19 izoba iNkosi yokugcina ye-Kaliyuga. Okungukuthi, ngokwe-Malika, ngemuva kweNkosi yamanje uShri Divyasingha Deva, ngeke kusaba khona amakhosi ase-Puri.

Ingxoxo engenhla ifakazela ukuthi zonke izenzakalo ezinhlekelele zizokwenzeka ngesikhathi se-Meena-Shani Yoga phakathi kuka-2025–2028, ngesikhathi sokubusa kukaShri Divyasingha Deva IV.

UMahapurush u-Achyutananda Das ubhalwa futhi encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବହୁତ ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ପାପର ଭାରା,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ହୋଇବେ ଧରା।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରେ ପାଣି ପଶିବ,
ସିଂହ ଦ୍ୱାରେ ରକ୍ତଧାର ବହିବ।
ଟିକରା ଗୋହିରୀ ହୋଇବ ରଡ଼ି,
ଧାନ ଥିବ ଯିବ କଣ୍ଠା ଅଗାଡ଼ି।
ସେବେଳକୁ ଏକା ଅକ୍ଷର ସାର,
ସପତ ଦିନ ଯେ ହେବ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ଗାଲୁଆ ମାଲୁଆ ଭକତ ଛନ୍ତି,
ବଗୁଲିଆ କର୍ମେ ପେଟ ପୋଷନ୍ତି।
ଏମାନେ ଯେ ଦେବୀ ଖପରରେ ଯିବେ,
ଭକତମାନେ ଆନନ୍ଦରେ ରହିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bahuta anyāya pāpara bhārā,
Dibyasiṃha deba hoibe dharā.
Baḍa deuḷare pāṇi pashiba,
siṃhadvāre raktadhāra bahiba.
ṭikarā gohirī hoiba raḍi,
dhāna thiba jiba kaṇṭhā agāḍi.
sebeḷaku ekā akṣara sāra,
sapata dina je heba andhāra.
gāluā māluā bhakata chanti,
baguliā karme peṭa poṣanti.
emāne je debī khaparare jibe,
bhaktamāne ānandare rahibe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, esigabeni sokugcina se-Kaliyuga, kuzoba nokungalungi okuningi nomthwalo omkhulu wesono. Ngesikhathi leso, amanzi olwandle azongena e-Jagannath Temple. Impi izoqhuma ngaphakathi ethempeleni. Umfudlana wegazi uzogeleza nge-Lion's Gate (Singhadwara). Ngesikhathi leso, ubumnyama buzogubuzela uMhlaba wonke izinsuku eziyisikhombisa nobusuku obuyisikhombisa. Bonke labo abangabazinikele abazenzisayo, abaziphilisa ngezenzo ezingalungile, bazobhujiswa oMama Ongcwele. Abazinikele bazochitha isikhathi sabo enjabulweni. Ngesikhathi leso, iNkosi yase-Puri izoba uShri Divyasingha Deva, ozobanjwa noma athunjwe abacindezeli.

Kucacile ngokuphelele kusukela evesini elingenhla ukuthi lapho izenzakalo zokusungulwa kwe-Dharma zenzeka, futhi lapho kunobumnyama izinsuku eziyisikhombisa nobusuku obuyisikhombisa eMhlabeni, iNkosi yase-Puri ngaleso sikhathi izoba uShri Divyasingha Deva.

Omunye uMahapurush wase-Odisha owazalwa ngekhulu lama-20 wabhalwa kwenye yezincwadi zakhe ukuthi ngemuva konyaka ka-2033, ukukhanya kwenkathi entsha (Satyayuga) kuzovela; okungukuthi, ngokukaMahapurush, zonke izenzo zokushintsha kwezinkathi zizophela ngaphambi konyaka ka-2033. Sizokwethula amavesi ahlobene nalokhu phambi kwakho kuSahluko 12. Iphuzu ukuthi zonke izenzakalo ezinhlekelele zizokwenzeka ngaphambi kuka-2033.

UMahapurush uBhima Bhoi (imbongi eyizinyonga eyazalelwa e-Sambalpur (Sambalpur), e-Odisha ngekhulu le-19) wabhalwa ngalolu daba encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ‘Padma Kalpa’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ନିଶ୍ଚୟ କଳଙ୍କି ହୋଇବ, ଯେ ଦିନ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ।
ପଞ୍ଚଗ୍ରହ ଯେ ଯୁକ୍ତ ହେବେ, ଆଗାମୀ ଅଢ଼ାଇର ଯୋଗେ।
ତଦନ୍ତେ ଷଡ଼ ଗ୍ରହ ଆସି, ଶନି କୁମ୍ଭରୁ ଯିବେ ଖସି।
ପ୍ରବେଶ ମୀନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ, ଟାକିଥାଅ ହେ ସୁଜ୍ଞ ନରେ।
ହୋଇବ ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ ସମର, ଦମନ ହୋଇବ ବାହାର।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ନାଶିବ ଅବହେଳେ, ଜଗତେ ରଖି ବାହୁବଳେ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛଙ୍କୁ ଦମନ ନାଶିବ, ଦୁର୍ଗା ଖପର ପକାଇବ।
ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ କଳି ସେ ଦମନ, ଶୁଣ ହେ ସାଧୁ ସୁଜ୍ଞଜନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Nishchaya Kaḷaṅki hoiba, je dina mīna shani bhoga.
Pañchagraha je yukta hebe, āgāmī aḍhāira joge.
Tadante ṣaḍa graha āsi, shani kumbharu jibe khasi.
Prabesha mīna saṅgatare, ṭākithāa he sujña nare.
Hoiba asaṃkhya samara, damana hoiba bāhāra.
Biṣṇu nāshiba abaheḷe, jagate rakhi bāhubaḷe.
Mlechchaṅku damana nāshiba, Durgā khapara pakāiba.
Asaṃkhya kaḷi se damana, shuṇa he sādhu sujñajana.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho i-Saturn ingena e-Pisces, izenzo zokubhujiswa zeNkosi uKalki zizokwenzeka. Lesi sikhathi sizohlala iminyaka emibili nengxenye. Ngesikhathi lesi, izimpi ezingenakubalwa zizolwa phakathi kwamazwe ahlukahlukene. INkosi uKalki izovikela abazinikele, futhi uMama uDurga uzobhubhisa ababi nezoni.

Evesini elingenhla, uMahapurush wacacisa ukuthi izenzo zokubhujiswa ze-Kalki zizokwenzeka ku-Meena-Shani iphi. Echaza ubunikazi bale Meena-Shani, wathi ngosuku i-Saturn ingena ngalo e-Pisces, amaplanethi ama-5 azobe esekhona e-Pisces. Okungukuthi, ngalolo suku, ingqikithi yamaplanethi ayisi-6 izobe ikhona e-Pisces—amaplanethi ama-5 asekhona kanye ne-Saturn eyodwa, okwenza amaplanethi ayisi-6.

Uma sibheka i-Meena-Shani ka-2025, sithola ukuthi isitatimende sikaMahapurush siphuma sisona ngokuphelele. NgoMashi 29, 2025, usuku i-Saturn idlulela ngalo e-Pisces, amaplanethi amahlanu (uRahu (Rahu), i-Venus, i-Mercury, iLanga, neNyanga) ayesekhona e-Pisces.

Manje, uma sibheka i-Meena-Shani ka-2054, sithola ukuthi ngoMeyi 14, 2054, usuku i-Saturn idlulela ngalo e-Pisces, amaplanethi ama-2 kuphela (i-Venus (Venus) ne-Mars) azobe ekhona e-Pisces ngaphambili, futhi nge-Saturn, kwenza amaplanethi ama-3. Kanti ngokukaSaint (Saint) uBhima Bhoi, kufanele kube nengqikithi yamaplanethi ayisi-6 e-Pisces, hhayi ama-3.

Uma sibheka i-Meena-Shani Yoga yonyaka ka-2084, sithola ukuthi ngoMashi 20, 2084, usuku i-Saturn idlulela ngalo e-Pisces, amaplanethi ama-2 kuphela (i-Mercury (Mercury) neLanga) azobe ekhona e-Pisces ngaphambili. Ngisho nokufaka i-Saturn, amaplanethi ama-3. Lokhu kusho ukuthi ukudlula kwamaplanethi ayisi-6 akwenzeki lapha futhi.

Kucacile ukuthi uMahapurush akakhulumi nge-Meena-Shani Yoga ka-2054 noma ka-2084. I-Meena-Shani akhuluma ngayo encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ‘Padma Kalpa’ iyi-Meena-Shani ka-2025.

UMahapurush u-Achyutananda Das waphinde wathi encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ukuthi ubani ozoba uMahant (Inhloko) ye-Padmaban Peeth yakhe etholakala e-Nemalo, e-Cuttack, lapho lezi zenzo zokubhujiswa zenzeka. Wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳରେ,
ନବ୍ୟକ୍ତ କଥା କହିଲି ରାମ ହେତୁ ଚିତ୍ତରେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta kaḷi śeṣa beḷare,
Nabyakta kathā kahili rāma hetu chittare.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, O mfundi uRamadas, lapho i-Kaliyuga iphela, ngaleso sikhathi umuntu ogama lakhe lingu-Gaganānanda uzoba Inhloko ye-Peeth yami.

I-Mahapurush yabhalwa kwenye incwadi ye-Malika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ ଗଗନେ ଉଡ଼ିବ ଧୂଳି,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶ ଯେ ଚମକି ପଡ଼ିବ ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ହାଲହୁଳି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta hoiba gagane uḍiba dhūḷi,
Oḍishā desha je chamaki paḍiba paḍijiba hālahuḷi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, izenzo ezingcwele izokwenzeka ngesikhathi sikaMahant Gaganānanda. Ngaleso sikhathi, kuzoba nokukhala okukhulu esifundazweni sase-Odisha.

5.3 UMahant Gaganānanda Goswami

Kuyaphawuleka ukuthi esizukulwaneni se-14 se-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, inzalo yakhe futhi okwamanje oneminyaka engu-85–90 ubudala uShri Gaganānanda Goswamiji uyiNhloko yesihlalo somoya se-Mahapurush, i-Padmaban, e-Nemalo. Lokhu futhi kwenza kucace ukuthi i-Mahapurush ikhuluma ngesikhathi samanje.

Kusukela ezindaweni ezine ezingenhla, kucacile ukuthi ngokwe-Malika, i-Meena-Shani lapho kwenzeka khona izenzo ezingcwele yokubhujiswa kukaKalki izofika ngaphambi konyaka ka-2033, futhi ngaleso sikhathi se-Meena-Shani, uShri Divyasingha Deva uzoba yiNkosi yase-Puri kanti uShri Gaganānanda uzoba yi-Mahant ye-Padmaban Peeth, e-Nemalo. Akukhona lokhu kuphela, kodwa ngosuku uSaturn engena ngalo ku-Pisces, i-Shad-graha (amaplanethi angu-6) i-Yoga izobe yakheka ku-Pisces.

Kucacile ukuthi i-Mahapurush ikhomba ku-Meena-Shani efika phakathi kuka-2025–2028 ngoba zonke izindawo ezine ezingenhla zisebenza kuphela kule-Meena-Shani Yoga futhi akukho okunye.

Akudingekile ukusho ukuthi ngokwe-Malika, izigameko ezinkulu zenhlekelele ezizokwenziwa yiNkosi uKalki (Lord Kalki) zizokwenzeka phakathi kuka-2025–2028.

Qaphela: Lezi zinsuku abanye abantu bathi i-Meena-Shani eshiwo ku-Malika akuyona i-Meena-Shani yamanje, kodwa kunalokho elandelayo efika ngo-2054! Kungafanele ukuthi bonke abazinikele (devotees) abakholwa ku-Malika bangadideki ngalokhu, ngoba izitatimende ezinjalo azilungile neze.

6 INkosi yasePuri (Puri) Ekupheleni kweKaliyuga

Esahlukweni esedlule, sabona ukuthi zonke izigameko ezinkulu zokusungulwa kwe-Dharma zizokwenzeka ngesikhathi sokubusa kweNkosi yase-Puri, uShri Divyasingha Deva. Kulesi sahluko, sizokuqonda lokhu ngamanye amavesi e-Malika.

Omunye wama-Panchasakhas, i-Mahapurush Jagannath Das, wabhala encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ethi ‘Kali Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ପୁରୁଷୋତ୍ତମ ଦେବ ରାଜା ଠାରୁ,
ଉନ୍ନବିଂଶ ରାଜା ହେବ ସେଠାରୁ।
ସେଠାରୁ ପୁଣ ରାଜା ନାହିଁ ଆଉ,
ଅକୁଳି ହୋଇବେ କୁଳକୁ ବହୁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Puruṣottama Deba rājā ṭhāru,
unnabiṃsha rājā heba seṭhāru.
Seṭhāru puṇa rājā nāhiṃ āu,
akuḷi hoibe kuḷaku bahu.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kusukela eNkosini uPurushottama Deva emva kweNkosi u-Indradyumna, inani eliphelele lamakhosi angu-19 azoba Amakhosi ase-Jagannath Puri ngezikhathi ezahlukene. I-Mahapurush yabhalwa ukuthi iNkosi ye-19 izoba ngaphandle kwendodana futhi izoba yinkosi yokugcina; okusho ukuthi, akekho omunye umuntu ozoba yinkosi emva kwakhe.

Uma sibheka isimo samanje, sithola ukuthi iNkosi yamanje yase-Puri iyiNkosi ye-19 futhi nayo ayinayo indodana. Inkomba ecacile ye-Mahapurush ukuthi yonke imidlalo engcwele kaKalki yokusungulwa kwe-Dharma izokwenzeka ngesikhathi sokubusa kwenkosi yamanje.

I-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhalwa encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ethi ‘Adi Samhita’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନାମେ ରାୟେ,
ଏ ଯୁଗ ପ୍ରାନ୍ତେ ସେ ଉଦୟେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shri Dibyasiṃha nāme rāye,
e juga prānte se udaye.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga, uShri Divyasingha uzoba yiNkosi yase-Puri.

Kwenye incwadi ye-Malika, wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଅଙ୍କେ ବାବୁ ସରବ ଦେଖିବୁ,
ଛାଡ଼ି ଚକା ଗଲୁ ବୋଲି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣିବୁ,
ନର ବାଳୁତ ରୂପରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମିବୁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dibyasiṃha aṅke bābu saraba dekhibu,
chhāḍi chakā galu boli nishchaya jānibu,
nara bāḷuta rūpare āmbhe janamibu.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho ukubusa kweNkosi egama layo linguShri Divyasingha Deva kuqhubeka e-Puri, ngaleso sikhathi yonke imidlalo engcwele yeNkosi uKalki (Lord Kalki) izokwenzeka. Ngaleso sikhathi, iNkosi (Lord) izoshiya iThempeli layo lase-Jagannath (Jagannath Temple) futhi izozalwa emzimbeni womuntu njenge-Kalki Avatar.

Bese encwadini ye-Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, u-Achyutanandaji ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବୃଦ୍ଧ ବାଳକ ରାଜା ହୋଇଥିବ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସଣ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବ।
ବାଳବୃଦ୍ଧ ଅଙ୍କେ ବତାସ ହେବ,
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ।
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୀନମାନ ବହୁ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bruddha bāḷaka rājā hoithiba,
gupatare saṇa sāla chāliba.
bāḷabruddha aṅke batāsha heba,
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba.
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba,
pṛthibī hīnamāna bahu hoiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, i-“Baalak-Briddha” izoba yiNkosi yase-Puri; okusho ukuthi, umuntu uzoba yinkosi ekhuphuka esihlalweni sobukhosi ebuntwaneni futhi ahlale kuleso sikhundla kuze kube yibudala. Ngesikhathi sokubusa kwakhe, izigameko ezifana nezinhlekelele zamanzi nezinhlekelele zomoya zizokwenzeka.

Kuyaziwa ukuthi phakathi kwamakhosi enziwe kuze kube manje, akekho ngaphandle kukaShri Divyasingha Deva oye waba yinkosi kusukela ebuntwaneni kuze kube yibudala. INkosi yamanje uShri Divyasingha Deva yakhwela esihlalweni sobukhosi ngoJulayi 7, 1970, eneminyaka engu-17 kuphela. Ngenkathi ekulesi sikhundla, usefinyelele ebudaleni futhi okwamanje uneminyaka engaba ngu-72 ubudala. Ngale vesi, ngolimi olungokomfanekiso, i-Mahapurush yembula ukuthi uDivyasingha Deva uzoba yinkosi ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga.

U-Achyutananda Dasji wabhala ngokuqhubekayo ku-‘Kalikalpa Gita’ yakhe:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ଅଙ୍କରେ ଜାଣ,
ଧୂମକେତୁ ହେବ ପୂର୍ବଭାଗେଣ।
ଉଲ୍‌କାପାତ ହେବ ହାଲହୁଳି,
ଶ୍ୱାନ ଶୃଗାଳ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ବୋବାଳି।
ମାତିବେ କାଳୀ ଯେ କରାଳୀଗଣ,
ଲୋକେ ମରିବେ ହୋଇ ରଣଭଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dibyasiṃha Deba aṅkare jāṇa,
dhūmaketu heba pūrbabhāgeṇa.
ulkāpāta heba hālahuḷi,
shwāna shṛgāḷa chhāḍibe bobāḷi.
mātibe kāḷī je karāḷīgaṇa,
loke maribe hoi raṇabhaṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho uShri Divyasingha Deva eyiNkosi yase-Puri, ngaleso sikhathi kuzobonakala ikometi ngasempumalanga. Kuzoba nezimvula zezinkanyezi emhlabeni wonke. Izilwane ezidla inyama njengezinja namakhanka zizoshiya amahlathi zibheke ezindaweni zokuhlala. UMama Kali (Mother Kali) kanye nama-Yoginis akanye naye bazobhubhisa izoni emhlabeni, okuholela ekufeni kwabantu abaningi kakhulu.

I-Mahapurush yazisa kafushane ngale vesi elingenhla ukuthi izigameko zokusungulwa kwe-Dharma zizokwenzeka ngesikhathi sokubusa kweNkosi yase-Puri egama layo linguShri Divyasingha Deva ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga.

I-Mahapurush yatshela umfundi wayo uRamadas kwenye incwadi ye-Malika:

I-Odia Sloka:

କେଉଁ କାଳ ବୋଲି ବୋଇଲୁ ଶୁଣ ରାମ ରତନ,
ଥିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ରାଜନ କୋଳେ କନ୍ୟା ରତନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Keuñ kāḷa boli boilu śuṇa Rāma ratana,
thiba Dibyasiṃha rājana koḷe kanyā ratana.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, O mfundi Ramadas, lalela ukuthi ngikhuluma ngasiphi isikhathi—ngesikhathi lezi zigameko zenzeka, ukubusa kweNkosi egama layo linguDivyasingha kuzobe kuqhubeka, engenayo indodana; amadodakazi kuphela.

I-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhalwa kwenye yezincwadi zakhe ze-Malika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ କରିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପତି,
ତାଙ୍କ କୁଳେ ପୁତ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭଣେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bāra bāra beḷaku kariba Dibyasiṃha nṛpati,
tāṅka kuḷe putra nohiba bhaṇe dīna Achyutī.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga, kuzoba khona ukubusa kweNkosi egama layo linguDivyasingha engenayo indodana.

Lesi siprofetho se-Mahapurush naso sihambisana ngokuphelele neNkosi yamanje yase-Puri, uShri Divyasingha Deva. INkosi yamanje ayinayo indodana; inamadodakazi amane kuphela.

Kwenye yezincwadi zakhe ze-Malika texts, ‘Kaliyuga Gita’, i-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das yabhalwa amazwi akhulunywe yiNkosi uKrishna ku-Arjuna e-Dwaparayuga, lapho iNkosi uKrishna (Lord Krishna) yathi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shrikshetra rājana mora na sebi payara.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga, iNkosi yase-Puri ngeke yenze inkonzo yami yansuku zonke (ngesimo sikaJagannath (Jagannath)).

U-Achyutananda Dasji wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ନ ଥିବ ରାଜନ ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ମଉଡ଼ ମଣି,
ରାଜ୍ୟ ରାଜକୁଳ କନ୍ୟାକୁ ନ କରିବ ବର୍ଣ୍ଣି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Na thiba rājana Shrīkṣetre kṣetra mauḍa maṇi,
rājya rājakuḷa kanyāku na kariba barṇṇi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi yase-Puri ngeke ihlale e-Shrikshetra noma e-Puri, kodwa izohlala kwenye indawo. Ngeke ishade nenkosazana yomndeni wasebukhosini base-Odisha kodwa izoshada nenkosazana evela kwesinye isifundazwe.

Usanta omkhulu wase-Odisha owazalwa ngekhulu le-18, i-Mahapurush Arakshita Das, wabhala encwadini yakhe ye-Malika ethi ‘Dwadasa Chandrika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଆସିବେ ଫେରି,
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରକୁ ନ ଯାଇ ରହିବେ ଜାଣ ସେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି।
ଭେଦ ମୁଁ କାହୁଁ ଜାଣିଥାନ୍ତି ଆଦିମାତା କହିଲେ,
ଭଣେ ଅରକ୍ଷିତ ଅଧମ ତହୁଁ ଶୁଣିଲି ଭଲେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati nishchaya āsibe pheri,
kṣhetraku na jāi rahibe jāṇa se Khaṇḍagiri.
bheda muñ kāhuñ jāṇithānti Ādimātā kahile,
bhaṇe Arakṣhita adhama tahuñ śuṇili bhale.

Incazelo:
I-Mahapurush ibhala ukuthi ekupheleni kwe-Kaliyuga, uDivyasingha Deva uzoba yiNkosi yase-Puri futhi ngeke ahlale e-Shrikshetra noma e-Puri, kodwa uzohlala endaweni yase-Khandagiri noma e-Bhubaneswar. I-Mahapurush ibhala ngokuqhubekayo ukuthi yena uqobo wayengawazi kanjani imfihlo yalezi zindaba; uMama Wokuqala (Adi Mata) wamtshela lezi zinto.

Ake ngazise abafundi ukuthi zonke izinkosi ngaphambi kweNkosi yamanje zazihlala e-Puri, e-Odisha, futhi zenza inkonzo yansuku zonke yeNkosi uJagannath, kodwa iNkosi yamanje uShri Divyasingha Deva yenza inkonzo yeNkosi uJagannath (Lord Jagannath) kuphela ngezikhathi ezithile ezikhethekile. Akahlali e-Puri kodwa uhlala e-Shahid Nagar, e-Bhubaneswar. Ngaphezu kwalokho, uShri Divyasingha Deva akazange ashade nenkosazana yase-Odisha kodwa washada nendodakazi kaLieutenant Colonel Anant Singh Charak wase-Jammu.

Lezi ziprofetho ezintathu ezingenhla zihambisana ngokuphelele neNkosi yamanje yase-Puri, uShri Divyasingha Deva. Lokhu futhi kuwubufakazi bokuthi i-Kaliyuga izophela ngesikhathi sokubusa kweNkosi uShri Divyasingha Deva.

I-Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhalwa kwenye yezincwadi zakhe ze-Malika ethi ‘Tera Janma Sharana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Māgha Saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājattwa karibe ehi Ekāmra Nagara.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho uDivyasingha Deva eyiNkosi yase-Puri, khona-ke ukubusa kuka-Ananta Keshari, okungukuthi, iNkosi uKalki, kuzokwenzeka. Kuzoba usuku lwe-Magha Saptami; i-Bhubaneswar (i-Ekambra Nagar (Ekambra Nagar)) izoba yindawo.

Le vesi futhi ifakazela ukuthi iNkosi yase-Puri ngesikhathi semidlalo engcwele kaKalki ngeke ibe ngomunye ngaphandle kukaShri Divyasingha Deva.

Umbhalo wakhe weMalika ‘Mohakalpa’, uAchyutanandaji wanikeza esinye isibonakaliso esenza kucace ukuthi uShri Divyasingha Deva uzoba yinkosi yokugcina yasePuri. UMahapurush ubhala kanje:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଧନୁଶର ଧରି ଆଗେ ସେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ଯେ ଜାଣ ବୈଷ୍ଣବ ଧର୍ମ ଛାଡିବେ।
କ୍ଷତ୍ରି ବୃତ୍ତି ଧରି ପଦାତିଙ୍କ ସହ କରିବେ ବହୁ ସମର,
ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କ ଦେଖି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଡିବେ ପଦ୍ମପାଦର।
ନବଲକ୍ଷ ଧର୍ମ-ହୀନ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ତହିଁ ତୁରିତେ ହେବେ ଛେଦନ,
ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଘାତେ ତହିଁ ମେଦିନୀ ଯେ କମ୍ପମାନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଠାରୁ ଆଦି ଓଡିଶା ରାଜ୍ୟରେ ଯେତେକ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଥିବେ,
ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେଇ ସମସ୍ତେ ତହିଁ ମରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ର ପୁଛିବେ,
ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ହତ୍ୟା ବୋଲି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ କହି ବଧ୍ୟ ନ କରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପରୀକ୍ଷା କରିବେ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ଜାଣ,
ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣଟି ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେବ ନାଶିବ ଯେ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ ଚକ୍ର ଶୂନ୍ୟ ଗଗନେ ରଖିବେ,
ଚଉଷଠି ଅକ୍ଷର ପ୍ରମାଣ ନ ପାଇ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଥାଇ ଛେଦିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Lalāṭa Kesharī dhanushara dhari āge se juddha karibe,
Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati je jāṇa baiṣṇaba dharma chhāḍibe.
Kṣatri bṛutti dhari padātiṅka saha karibe bahu samara,
Prabhuṅka dekhi Dibyasiṃha Deba paḍibe padmapādara.
Nabalakṣa dharma-hīna byakti tahiṃ turite hebe chhedana,
Lalāṭa Kesharī juddha ghāte tahiṃ medinī je kampamāna.
Kṣetra ṭhāru ādi Oḍishā rājyare jeteka brāhmaṇa thibe,
Gāyatrī mantraku parīkṣā na dei samaste tahiṃ maribe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba brāhmaṇamānaṅku Gāyatrī mantra puchhibe,
Brāhmaṇa hatyā boli brāhmaṇamānaṅku kahi badhya na karibe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba parīkṣā karibe Gāyatrī mantraku jāṇa,
Jeuṃ brāhmaṇaṭi parīkṣā na deba nāshiba je Sudarshana.
Ananta Kesharī Sudarshana chakra shūnya gagane rakhibe,
Chauṣaṭhi akṣara pramāṇa na pāi shūnyare thāi chhedibe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi yasePuri, uShri Divyasingha Deva, izoshiya iVaishnava Dharma yayo futhi yamukele umsebenzi weKshatriya futhi ilwe izimpi eziningi. Lapho ebona iNkosi Kalki, uzowela ezinyaweni zeNkosi, athole izibusiso zeNkosi, futhi abhubhise izigidi eziyisishiyagalolunye zabahlubuki. Endaweni yaseJagannath, ePuri, bonke abaBrahmins abakhona bazohlolwa ngeGayatri Mantra. Esesaba isono sokubulala abaBrahmins, uDivyasingha Deva ngeke ababulale labo baBrahmins abahluleka esivivinyweni. UMahapurush ubhala ukuthi bonke bazobhujiswa yiSudarshana Chakra yeNkosi ngokwayo. INkosi izobeka iSudarshana Chakra Yayo esibhakabhakeni esingenalutho futhi inqume amakhanda alabo baBrahmins ngaleli chakra elifanayo abangayisho iGayatri Mantra.

Lapha, uMahapurush unikeza ubufakazi bokuthi iNkosi yasePuri, uShri Divyasingha Deva, izohlangana neNkosi Kalki futhi ifake isandla ekusungulweni kweDharma. Kucacile ukuthi imidlalo yokubhubhisa yeNkosi Kalki izokwenzeka ngesikhathi sokubusa kukaShri Divyasingha Deva.

Bese uMahapurush ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Kalpa Tika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିୟ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ କରିଥିବେ ସେ ବନ୍ଧନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ନୃପତି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଯେ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଯେ ରଖିଥିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଗୋପନ।
ବନ୍ଧନର ଦୁଇ ଦିନରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି,
ରାତ୍ରିକାଳେ ରାଜା ପାଶେ ବିଜେ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ରାଜାର ବନ୍ଧନ ଫେଇ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବାସୁ ଦେବେ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ତଳ ପୁରେ ଗୋପନେ ରଖିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dibyasiṃha Deba se je Kṣhatriya rājana,
tāhānku Phiringī karithibe se bandhana.
Kṣhetrara nṛupati Dibyasiṃha je rājana,
tāhānku je rakhithibe Shrī Hari gopana.
Bandhanara dui dinare Prabhu je Shrī Hari,
rātrikāḷe rājā pāśe bije je Shrī Hari.
rājāra bandhana phei Prabhu bāśu debe,
Khaṇḍagiri taḷa pure gopane rakhibe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho iNkosi yasePuri, uShri Divyasingha Deva, ithunjwa amabutho angaphandle, khona-ke ezinsukwini ezimbili kamuva, iNkosi Kalki izofinyelela enkosini ebusuku, imkhulule ekuboshweni, futhi imfihle ezintabeni zaseKhandagiri.

Ngaphezu kwalokho, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Padmakalpa Tika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ମୋ ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ,
ସେହିବେଳେ ସରକାର ଖାଦ୍ୟ ପକାଇବେ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଆଦିକରି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ananta gumphā madhyare mo bhakta rahibe,
sehibeḷe sarakāra khādya pakāibe.
Ananta Keshari swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha Lalāṭa Keshari ādikari.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho kwenzeka inhlekelele enkulu yamanzi, abazinikele (bhaktas) abathembekile beNkosi Kalki bazohlala bephephile emhumeni wase-Ananta osentabeni yaseKhandagiri. Ngaleso sikhathi, uhulumeni uzohlinzeka ngokudla nezinto zokusiza izisulu. UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi ngaleso sikhathi, u-Ananta Madhava, okungukuthi, iNkosi Kalki, uzobe eseKhandagiri, e-Odisha. INkosi uDivyasingha Deva nayo izobe inaYe.

UMahapurush waphinda wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Kalpa Tika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାବରେ ସେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଦେଖିବେ,
ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହକୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Anaṃta kesharī swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha deba se je lalāṭakesharī.
Khaṇḍagiri ṭhābare se prabhuṃku dekhibe,
Lalāṭakesharī Dibyasiṃhaku chihnibe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho iNkosi Ananta Keshari, okungukuthi, iNkosi Kalki, iseKhandagiri, khona-ke ngaleso sikhathi uDivyasingha Deva naye uzobe eseKhandagiri. EKhandagiri, uDivyasingha Deva uzoba nombono (Darshan) weNkosi.

Kuzo zonke izindima ezintathu ezinikezwe ngenhla, kuyaziwa ukuthi ngesikhathi semidlalo engcwele yeNkosi Kalki, iNkosi yasePuri izoba uDivyasingha Deva.

6.1 Ukuhlangana kweNkosi Kalki noBaladeva noDivyasingha Deva kanye namaKhosi amaningi

IBhavishya Malika igcwele iziqephu eziningi eziyimfihlo neziprofetho. Phakathi kwalezi ziprofetho kukhona isiprofetho samakhosi amaningi ahlangana neNkosi.

UMahapurush ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ukuthi esikhathini esizayo, kuzofika isikhathi lapho amasosha angaphandle ezohlala kusukela eChhatia, eJajpur kuya eNilagiri noma eNilachal, ePuri. Ngisho nempi enkulu yesitha izongena ngaphakathi eTethempelini laseJagannath. Ebona konke lokhu ekucindezelekeni okukhulu, iNkosi, egxile ekuzileni, izokhumbula uSahadeva, uNakula, uAshwatthama, noShalya. Ngokushesha nje lapho iNkosi ibakhumbula, ukuzindla nokuzila kwalaba balwi abane abaqhawe, ochwepheshe bokucibishela, kuzophuka, futhi bonke abane bazofinyelela eChhatia futhi bawe ezinyaweni zeNkosi, becela umyalo Wayo. INkosi izobayala bonke abane ukuba babhubhise ibutho elikhulu lamaMughal futhi bavikele abazinikele. Ngokomyalo weNkosi, bonke abane bazobhubhisa amasosha amaMughal bese bebuyela eNkosini. Ngaleso sikhathi, abazinikele abaningi bazobe bekhona neNkosi eChhatia, futhi izinsuku ezingu-27, iChhatia izoba indawo yokuhlangana kwabazinikele. Ngalesi sikhathi, amakhosi aseRussia, eNepal, eManipur, e-Ali, eKujang, eKanika, njll., azohlangana neNkosi, athole iDarshan Yayo, futhi azinikele ezinyaweni Zayo. Ngaleso sikhathi, iNkosi izobathatha bonke futhi ifinyelele eSealdah ukuze icele iNkosi Baladeva ukuba ibhubhise ibutho langaphandle elisele. Ngale ndlela, wonke amakhosi aneNkosi azoqonda eSealdah futhi ahlangane noBaladeva, lapho u-Arjuna noBhima bazobe sebekhona.

Lapha uMahapurush ubhala ukuthi ngalesi sikhathi, phakathi kwamakhosi azohlangana neNkosi Ananta Keshari neNkosi Baladeva, iNkosi yasePuri, iShrikshetra, nayo izobe ikhona. Eveza ukuthi uzoba ngubani, uMahapurush ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika:

I-Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଯେ ରାଜା ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ତାହାଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗତେ ସ୍ଥିତି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kṣhetre je rājā Dibyasiṃha Deba tāhāṅka saṅgate sthiti.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, phakathi kwalabo makhosi athola iDarshan yeNkosi kuzoba iNkosi yaseShrikshetra Puri, uDivyasingha Deva.

Kusukela ezingxoxweni ezingenhla, kucacile futhi ukuthi iNkosi yasePuri ngesikhathi semidlalo engcwele kaKalki izoba uShri Divyasingha Deva.

Kusukela ezindimeni ezingu-17 ezingenhla, kufakazelwe ngokucacile ukuthi ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, lapho iNkosi Kalki isizalwe futhi lapho imidlalo Yayo yokubhubhisa yenzeka emhlabeni wonke, lapho ukubhujiswa kwababi, insindiso yabazinikele, nomsebenzi wokusungula iDharma kwenzeka, ngaleso sikhathi inkosi egama layo linguDivyasingha Deva izohlala esihlalweni sobukhosi saseJagannath Puri, futhi izoba yinkosi yokugcina ukuhlala kulesi sikhundla.

UShri Divyasingha Deva uzoba yinkosi yokugcina—lokhu kwabhalwa uMahapurush Jagannath Das, omunye wamaPanchasakhas, embhalweni wakhe weMalika, esiwushilo endimeni yokuqala yalesi sahluko.

Kodwa okufanele kuqashelwe ukuthi akukhona nje uJagannath Das, kodwa omunye uMahapurush phakathi kwamaPanchasakhas, uShishu Ananta Das, naye wabhala ngalesi sihloko embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Patta Madana’. UMahapurush ubhala kanje:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଆଉ ଶାସନ ନ ଥିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dibyasiṃha nṛupa rājattwa karibe āu śāsana na thiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi uDivyasingha Deva izobusa futhi ngemuva kwakhe, ngeke kube khona ukubusa kwanoma iyiphi enye inkosi. UDivyasingha Deva uzoba yinkosi yokugcina. Ngamanye amazwi, iKaliyuga izophela ngesikhathi sakhe, futhi imidlalo yokubhubhisa yeNkosi Kalki izokwenzeka.

6.2 UShri Divyasingha Deva

Iqiniso okufanele liqashelwe lapha ukuthi uMahapurush akazange agcine ngokuthi igama leNkosi yasePuri ekupheleni kweKaliyuga lizoba uDivyasingha Deva; kunalokho, wakhuluma izinto eziningi ngakho, njengokuthi uzoba yinkosi ye-19, uzoba uDivyasingha Deva wesine, uzoba ngaphandle kwendodana, uzoba namadodakazi kuphela, ngeke ahlale ePuri kodwa eBhubaneswar, ngeke akwazi ukwenza izinkonzo zansuku zonke zeNkosi Jagannath, uzothatha ubukhosi ebuntwaneni futhi ahlale eyinkosi kuze kube yibudala, njll.

Zonke lezi zinto zilingana neNkosi yamanje uShri Divyasingha Deva ngokuphelele.

7 Izimpawu Zokuphela kweKaliyuga eThempelini laseJagannath (Jagannath Temple)

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das kanye namanye amaPanchasakhas babhale ezindaweni eziningi emibhalweni yabo yeMalika ukuthi abazinikele (bhaktas) bazothola izimpawu eziningi ezivela eTethempelini laseJagannath lapho bazokwazi khona ukwazi ukuthi iKali Yuga isiphelile futhi iNkosi Kalki isivele. Lezi zimpawu zizobonakala hhayi kuphela eTethempelini laseJagannath kodwa nasezindaweni zokuhlala izindela namathempeli e-India, kodwa ikakhulukazi zizobonakala eTethempelini laseJagannath elisePuri (Puri).

I-Odia Sloka:

ବାରମ୍ବାର ଶ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ଦିରରେ ପୁଣି,
ନାନା ଅଘଟଣ ସତ୍ୟ ମୋ ବାଣୀ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bārambāra shrīmandirare puṇi,
nānā aghaṭana satya mo bāṇī.

Incazelo:
UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ukuthi ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, izinhlobo ezahlukene zezingozi (izimpawu ezimbi) zizobonakala ngokuphindaphindiwe eShrimandir (Itmpeli laseJagannath) elisePuri. Waphinde wathi leli zwi lami lizofakazela ukuthi liyiqiniso ngokuphelele.

7.1 Umlando wezimpawu ezimbi eTethempelini laseJagannath

Namuhla sithola ukuthi eminyakeni engu-15-20 edlule, izimpawu eziningi ezimbi ziye zabonakala eTethempelini laseJagannath, njengokuthi itshe elikhulu liwa ku-"Amla Bedha" yethempeli ngonyaka ka-1990; amatshe ewa phansi ezakhiweni ezingaphezulu zethempeli, kuhlanganise nendawo engcwele kakhulu, izikhathi eziningi eminyakeni yamuva; izinyoni ezimbi (ezidla izidumbu) zihlala esigxotsheni sefulegi sethempeli nase-Aruna Stambha phambi kweSinghadwara (Isango LeNgonyama); izinkozi (izinyoni ezidla inyama) ezingenakubalwa zizungeza phezu kwethempeli; umthunzi ogcwele amagugu (Ratnachandua) obekwe phezu kwezithixo ubamba umlilo; i-'Patitapabana Bana' (ifulegi) eliphakanyiswe phezulu ethempelini libamba umlilo; iNeelachakra engamathani angu-2.2, eyenziwe nge-Ashtadhatu (izinsimbi eziyisishiyagalombili), igoba ngemimoya enesivinini esikhulu; i-'Patitapabana Bana' iphephethwa futhi iwela olwandle ngesikhathi sesiphepho esikhulu; igatsha leKalpavata elikhulu (umuthi womkhiwane) elisezakhiweni zethempeli liphuka; iPatitapabana Bana ishisa ngenxa yokushaywa umbani; cishe izitofu zobumba ezingu-40 zonakaliswa izigebengu ekhishini elingcwele lethempeli; kuphazamiseka ku-Aruna Stambha yethempeli, ugongolo olungaphakathi kweNatamandapa, ihholo eliphambi kwendawo engcwele kakhulu (Jagamohana), naseduze neSinghadwara; itshe le-'Padma Pakhuda' (iqabunga lelotus) linyamalala kuRatnasinghasana (isihlalo sobukhosi esigcwele amagugu); ukushaywa umbani ekhishini lethempeli; ukwebiwa okwenzeka ethempelini ebusuku; ukwanda kwamagundane ethempelini ngenxa yokuncipha kwenani labazinikele ngesikhathi sobhubhane lweCorona, okudala ukuphazamiseka nokuphazamisa imikhosi yethempeli; ukuchitheka kwegazi okutholakala ezindaweni eziningi kuhlanganise neRatnasinghasana yethempeli, kulandelwa ukugeza okukhulu kwesiko (Mahasnana) kweChaturdha Murti (izithixo ezine); isibhakabhaka siba bomvu nxazonke zethempeli; ukunyamalala okungazelelwe kwamalulwane ayebonakala ngobuningi ethempelini; ukusetshenziswa komakhalekhukhwini naphezu kokuvinjelwa kwawo ethempelini; ukuqoshwa kwevidiyo okukhohlisayo ezakhiweni zethempeli kusetshenziswa amakhamera ayinhloli afihlwe ezibukweni; ukungena kwabangewona amaHindu nabangewona amaSanatanis ethempelini naphezu kokuvinjelwa; ukuzibulala noma ukufa ngenxa yezinye izizathu okwenzeka ngaphakathi ethempelini; ukufa kuBaisi Pahacha, okungukuthi, izitebhisi ezingu-22 zethempeli; ukuphazamiseka okutholakala kuGumuta (idome) yeHasti Dwara (Isango LaseNyakatho) yethempeli, njll.

Iziprofetho ezihlukene mayelana nezinye zalezi zigameko zitholakala emibhalweni ehlukahlukene yeMalika, lapho kushiwo khona ngokucacile ukuthi lezi zigameko zizokwenzeka kuphela ekupheleni kweKali Yuga futhi zizonikeza abazinikele isignali yokwehla kweNkosi Kalki emhlabeni. Ngakho-ke, labo abaveza ukungabaza kwabo nokungakholwa kwabo namuhla mayelana nokuphela kweKali Yuga nokuzalwa kweNkosi Kalki kufanele bacabange kabusha ngemibono yabo.




UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala ku-'Jaiphula Malika' yakhe:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁଁ ପଥର,
ଖସୁଥିବ ପୁଣି ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ତୁହି ଦେଖିବୁ ବେନି ନେତ୍ରର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Baḍa deuluṃ pathara,
khasuthiba puṇi tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
tuhi dekhibu beni netrara.

Incazelo:
UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi ngaleso sikhathi, izigameko zamatshe ewa ngokuphindaphindiwe eTethempelini laseJagannath elisePuri zizovela, okuzobonwa ngokucacile abazinikele ngamehlo abo.

Uma sicabangela iqiniso lalesi siprofetho, sizothola ukuthi ngemuva kokuba itshe elinesisindo esingamathani angu-5-6 liwe ku-"Amla Bedha" yethempeli ngoJuni 14, 1990, izigameko ezinjalo ziye zenzeka izikhathi eziningi. Ngo-Agasti 13, 1992, amatshe amabili amakhulu awela ngqo endaweni engcwele kakhulu yethempeli. Ngokufanayo, ngo-Okthoba 26, 1998, idwala elingu-15 kg lehlela phansi lisuka ethempelini elikhulu futhi lawela eduze nethempeli laseBimala ngaphakathi kwendawo yethempeli elikhulu. NgoNovemba 1, 2011, itshe elinesisindo esingaba ithani elilodwa lawela phansi eduze kweDadhinauti yesango lasentshonalanga leTethempeli laseJagannath.

Futhi ngonyaka ka-2015, ngoFebhuwari 2, itshe elinesisindo esingu-30-40 kg lawela phansi lisuka eJalakrida Mandapa eyakhiwe eduze kweThempeli laseBimala engxenyeni esentshonalanga yeThempeli laseJagannath. Uma sikhuluma ngezikhathi zakamuva, ngo-Agasti 3, 2022, ucezu lwe-plaster olunesisindo esingu-1.5 kg lwawa luvela ophahleni lwegumbi elingcwele kakhulu (garbhagṛha) laleli thempeli elakhiwe ngekhulu leshumi nambili. Ngo-Okthoba 23, 2022, itshe lawa lisuka ekhoneni eliseningizimu-mpumalanga yethempeli eduze kweMajana Mandapa neCharcha Ghara, futhi kungakapheli isonto kulokhu, ngo-Okthoba 28, 2022, itshe elingu-2-3 kg lawa lisuka esitezi sesithathu ngaphezu kwesithombe seSakhi eduze kwendawo yokuhlanganisa iNatamandapa neJagamohana. Ngisho nangonyaka ka-2024, ngoMashi 9, itshe elinesisindo esingamakhilogremu amane lawa lisuka ophahleni lweThempeli laseBaraha (Barāha Mandira) elisephambi kweThempeli laseBimala (Bimalā Mandira) ezindaweni zeThempeli laseJagannath (Jagannātha Mandira).

Ngale ndlela, isiprofetho esenziwa emakhulwini eminyaka adlule yiMahapurush Achyutananda Das mayelana namatshe awa "tharaku thara" (ngokuphindaphindiwe) eThempelini laseJagannath nakho kufakazelwe ukuthi kuyiqiniso eminyakeni embalwa edlule.

I-Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ସେହିଦିନଠାରୁ ସଖୀ ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ପଥର ଖସିବ ଯୁଗ ଶେଷ ହେବାପାଇଁ,
ଜାଣି ମୁଁ ଗରୁଡ଼ ଏ ଶକୁନମାନ ଗୁପତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ଏକ ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ତେଇଶି ବରଷେ କଳି ଦେଶ ଛାଡ଼ି ଯିବ।
ଚୁଳରୁ ପଥର ଯେବେ ଖସିବ ସୁତ,
ଖସିଲେ ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ହେବ ଏ କଳି ହତ।
ମୋର ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ତୋ ଦେଉଳ ଚୁଳେ,
ଖସିବ ପଥର ଖଣ୍ଡ ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ତଳେ।
ବତାସରେ ହେ। ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବକ୍ର ହେବ ବାରେ ହେ।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ଅରୁଣ ଖମ୍ବର।
ବତାସର ହେ। ବକ୍ର ହେବ ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ମୋର ହେ।
ତରକିବେ ତେରଜଣ ଜାଣିଥା ନିକର,
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁ ପଥର ଖସି ବାରମ୍ବାର।
ଗୃଧ୍ରପକ୍ଷୀ ମଥାପରେ ବସିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ,
ସେହିଦିନୁ ହତଶିରୀ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Baḍa deularu mora pathara khasiba,
sehidinaṭhāru sakhī bipada paḍiba.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru pathara khasiba yuga sheṣha hebāpāiṃ,
jāṇi mu Garuḍa e shakunamāna gupate deli chetei.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru eka pathara khasiba,
teishi baraṣhe kali desha chāḍi jiba.
Chularu pathara jebe khasiba suta,
khasile aaṃlā beḍhāru heba e kali hata.
Mora baḍa deularu khasiba pathara,
Shrīkṣhetra rājana mora na sebi payara.
Basiba je giddha pakṣhī to deula chule,
khasiba pathara khaṃḍa paḍiba je tale.
Batāsare he. Nīlachakra bakra heba bāre he.
Baḍa deularu mora khasiba pathara,
basiba je giddha pakṣhī Aruṇa khambara.
Batāsara he. Bakra heba Nīlachakra mora he.
Tarakibe terajaṇa jāṇithā nikara,
baḍa deulu pathara khasi bārambāra.
Giddhapakṣhī mathāpare basiṇa je jiba,
sehidinu hatashirī pṛithwī hoiba.

Incazelo:
IMahapurush Achyutananda Das ixoxe ngokuningiliziwe ngezimpawu eziningi ezimbi zokuphela kweKali Yuga ezitholakala eThempelini laseJagannath e-Puri emibhalweni eminingi yeMalika kuhlanganise ne-'Rukmini Gita', 'Garuda Samvada', 'Hari Arjuna Chautisha', 'Kaliyuga Gita', 'Jai Birachita Chautisha', kanye ne-'Chakada Madana'. Kulesi simo, ubhala ukuthi lapho itshe liwa lisuka ku-"Amla Bedha" yeThempeli laseJagannath, abazinikele (bhakta) kufanele baqonde ukuthi ukuphela kweKali Yuga sekuseduze kakhulu nokuthi izinhlekelele eziningi zizokwehlela umhlaba esikhathini esiseduze. Uthi ukuwa kwaleli tshe kuzoba uphawu olubalulekile noluyimfihlo lokuphela kweKali Yuga, futhi eminyakeni engu-23 ngemuva kokuwa kwalo, iKali Yuga izophela. Uphinde athi ngaleso sikhathi, inyoni embi izohlala phezu kweThempeli laseJagannath kanye ne-Aruna Stambha elisephambi kweSinghadwara (Siṁhadvāra) yethempeli. Akukhona lokhu kuphela, iNeelachakra enesisindo esingu-2.2-ton eyenziwe nge-Ashtadhatu esiqongweni sethempeli izogoba ngenxa yesivinini esikhulu somoya.

Abafundi bazomangala ukwazi ukuthi zonke iziprofetho ezishiwo ngenhla ezabhalwa emakhulwini eminyaka adlule sezenzekile eThempelini laseJagannath. Phakathi kwezulu elikhulu, okokuqala, ngoJuni 14, 1990, itshe elinesisindo esingu-5-6 amathani lawa lisuka ku-"Amla Bedha" yethempeli. Kwabe sekutholakala nenyoni embi ihlezi phezu kwe-Aruna Stambha.

Kuyaphawuleka ukuthi akukaze kubonwe nyoni indiza phezu kwethempeli laseJagannath Puri, futhi ososayensi nabacwaningi nabo bahluleka ukuhlaziya ukuthi kungani lokhu kwenzeka. Kodwa kulesi sikhathi sokuphela kweKali Yuga, kufakazela iziprofetho zeMalika ukuthi ziqinisile, izinyoni ezimbi njengamakhwela (ukhozi)/amavulture zibonwe zihlezi kuPatitapabana Bana (isigxobo sefulegi) phezu kwethempeli izikhathi eziningi. Eminyakeni yakamuva, okufanayo kwenzeka ngo-Agasti 11, 2018, Disemba 26, 2018, nangoJulayi 23, 2020, lapho kubonwa izinyoni ezimbi zihlezi esigxotsheni sefulegi sethempeli. NgoDisemba 7, 2021, izinyoni ezingenakubalwa ezinjalo zabonwa zizungeza ngomhlambi phezu kweThempeli laseJagannath. Isigameko esifanayo sabonwa futhi ngo-Agasti 17, 2022. Lokhu kulandelana akuzange kume lapha; ezinyangeni zikaMeyi, Juni, noSepthemba zonyaka ka-2023, izakhamuzi zase-Puri zabona futhi lesi sigameko esingajabulisi lapho kubonwa izinyoni ezifanayo zihlezi esigxotsheni sefulegi esifakwe phezu kwethempeli.

Muva nje ngonyaka ka-2025, inyoni yabukwa phezu kwe-Aruna Stambha ngoSepthemba 4, kanti amakhwela abukwa ehlezi phezu kwethempeli ngoSepthemba 2, Septhemba 16, nango-Okthoba 14. NgoDisemba 9, izinyoni ezingenakubalwa zatholakala zizungeza iShrimandir.

Ngaphezu kwakho konke lokhu, isiphepho esinamandla kakhulu, 'Fani', esashaya ugu lwase-Puri ngoMeyi 3, 2019, sagoba iNeelachakra phezu kwethempeli, ngaleyo ndlela sagcwalisa esinye isiprofetho esabhalwa emakhulwini eminyaka adlule kulokhu kulandelana.

Kuyaphawuleka ukuthi lezi ziprofetho ezabhalwa ngekhulu le-16 azizona nje izimpawu zokuphela kweKali Yuga, kodwa futhi ziyizimpawu ezinikezwe yiPanchasakhas zokufika kweKalki Avatar, ngoba iKalki Avatar izofika kuphela ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, njengoba kushiwo yiyo yonke imibhalo kuhlanganise neBhavishya Malika.





I-Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।
ହର ଉଦ୍ୟାନରେ ମାୟା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛକୁ ଗ୍ରାସୁଥିବେ,
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବାନା ଝଡ଼ିବ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଲୋଡ଼ିଯିବେ।
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ପରୁ ପତାକା ଛିଡ଼ିଣ ପଡ଼ିବ ସମୁଦ୍ର କୂଳେ,
କଳକୀ ରୂପକୁ ଚିନ୍ତା ପୁଣ ହେବ ଭାବିବେ କ୍ଷୀରାଧି କୂଳେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Nīlachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṃgā adhara,
Jājanagre janma hoibe Biṣhṇu Sharmā gṛihara.
Hara udyānare māyā prakāshiba mlechchaku grāsuthibe,
Nīlachakra bānā jhaḍiba nishchaya bhakataṃku loḍijibe.
Nīlachakra paru patākā chiḍiṇa paḍiba samudra kūle,
Kalki rūpaku chiṃtā puṇa heba bhābibe kṣhīrādhi kūle.

Incazelo:
IMahapurush Achyutananda Das yasho esinye isibonakaliso sokuphela kweKali Yuga nokuzalwa kweNkosi Kalki emibhalweni yayo yeMalika ethi 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' kanye ne-'Kalikalpa Gita'. Yabhala ukuthi kuzofika isikhathi lapho iPatitapabana Bana efakwe phezu kweNeelachakra (Nīlacakra) yeShrimandir izondiza isuke endaweni yayo. IMahapurush yaphinde yabhala ukuthi ifulegi lizondiza liwele olwandle.

Lesi siprofetho esenziwa yiMahapurush Achyutananda Das naso saphenduka saba yiqiniso. Ngosuku olulodwa ngaphambi kokuba isiphepho esinamandla kakhulu 'Fani' sishaye ugu lwase-Puri, ngoMeyi 2, 2019, ngenxa yemimoya enamandla, iPatitapabana Bana yandiza yawela olwandle. Isigameko esifanayo senzeka futhi ngoMeyi 18, 2020, ezinsukwini ezimbili ngaphambi kokuba isiphepho 'Amphan' sishaye ugu lwase-Odisha.



I-Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡ଼ି ଆମ୍ଭେ ଯିବୁ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ଲାଗିବ ରତ୍ନଚାନ୍ଦୁଆ ଅଗ୍ନି ସେତେବେଳେ।
ନିଶାକାଳେ ହେ। ନୀଳାଚଳୁ ଚୋରିଯିବ ହେଲେ ହେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Nīlāchala chāḍi āmbhe jibu jetebele,
lāgiba ratnachāṃduā agni setebele.
Nishākāle he. Nīlāchalu chorijiba hele he.

Incazelo:
Icaphuna amazwi eNkosi, iMahapurush Achyutananda Das yabhala embhalweni wayo weMalika othi 'Jai Birachita Chautisha' ukuthi lapho iNkosi Jagannath ishiya iNilachala, okungukuthi, iShrimandir, ukuze izalwe emzimbeni womuntu, ngaleso sikhathi umthunzi ogcwele amagugu (Ratnachandua (Ratnacandua)) obekwe phezu kobuthathu obungcwele eThempelini laseJagannath uzobamba umlilo.

Kuyaphawuleka ukuthi isigameko sokubamba umlilo kweRatnachandua senzeka ngonyaka ka-2004-05, ngaleyo ndlela lesi siprofetho seMahapurush naso sifakazelwe ukuthi siyiqiniso.

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍ପବଟ ଡ଼ାଳ ଛେଦନ ଭୋଇବ ଦେଖିବୁ ତୁ ନୟନରେ,
ଶିବକଳ୍ପ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ତୁରେ।
ଏକ ଦକ୍ଷିଣରେ ନବ ଯେ ଥିବ,
କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଛେଦନ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kalpabaṭa ḍāla chedana hoiba dekhibu tu nayanare,
Shibakalpa bāṇī anyathā nohiba shuṇa Rāmadāsa ture.
Eka dakṣhiṇare naba je thiba,
kalpabaṭa mora chedana hoiba.

Incazelo:
IMahapurush Achyutananda Das yabhala embhalweni wayo weMalika othi 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' eminyakeni engu-600 edlule ukuthi kuzofika isikhathi lapho igatsha leKalpavata elisezakhiweni zeThempeli laseJagannath lizophuka. Yaze yabhala kwenye yemibhalo yayo ukuthi lapho kukhona 'isishiyagalolunye' eduze 'koyedwa', okungukuthi, inombolo 19, yilapho kuphela lesi sigameko sizokwenzeka.

Ngokuvumelana ncamashi nesitatimende seMahapurush, lesi siprofetho saba yiqiniso ngonyaka ka-2019 lapho isiphepho esinamandla kakhulu 'Fani', esashaya ugu lwase-Puri ngoMeyi 3, salimaza iKalpavata enkulu, endala kakhulu elisezakhiweni zethempeli.

I-Odia Sloka:

ରକ୍ତଛିଟା ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ରତ୍ନସିଂହାସନେ,
ଏହି ଘଟଣା ଘଟିବ ମନେ ରଖ ଦିନେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Raktachīṃṭā paḍiba je ratnasiṃhāsane,
ehi ghaṭaṇā ghaṭiba mane rakha dine.

Incazelo:
Phakathi kwezimpawu eziningi ezishiwo yiMahapurush okufanele zitholakale eThempelini laseJagannath e-Puri ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, kwakukhona ukubona amachaphaza egazi kuRatnasinghasana sezithombe ezingcwele.

Lesi siprofetho esabhalwa yiMahapurush naso saphenduka saba yiqiniso. Eminyakeni embalwa edlule, amachaphaza egazi awele ezindaweni eziningi ethempelini, kuhlanganise neRatnasinghasana, ngemuva kwalokho, ngokwesiko lethempeli, iChaturdha Murti (onkulunkulu abane) bahlanjululwa ngesiko leMahasnana (ukuwasha okukhulu). Ngokwesibonelo, ngoMashi 6, 2021, igazi labonwa eduze kwe-'Bhitar Katha' elisebanga elithile ukusuka egumbini elingcwele kakhulu lethempeli. Ngo-Ephreli 18, 2021, ngenxa yokuxabana phakathi kwamaphoyisa amabili abekwe ukuvikela ithempeli, amachaphaza egazi awele ezindaweni zethempeli. NgoMashi 7, 2022, ngesikhathi se-'Abakasha Niti' (isiko), amachaphaza egazi atholakala eduze kwe-'Bhitar Katha' ngaphakathi kweJagamohana yethempeli (ihholo eliphambi kwegumbi elingcwele kakhulu). Kwabe sekuthi ngo-Ephreli 11, 2022, amachaphaza egazi abonwa eduze kweDhukudi Dwara yethempeli. NgoNovemba 3, 2022, ngemuva kwamasiko e-'Mangala Alati', 'Mailama', kanye ne-'Tadapa Lagi', amachaphaza egazi abonwa eduze kwe-'Bhitar Katha'. Isigameko esinjalo sabonwa futhi ngesonto lesithathu likaJanuwari ngonyaka ka-2023. Ngemuva kokuqedwa kwamasiko eNkosi Jagannath e-'Abakasha' kanye ne-'Besa Lagi', amabala egazi abonwa eShrimandir, ngenxa yalokho umnikelo (Bhoga (Bhoga)) kuGopalaballabha awukwazanga ukuqedwa. Kwabe sekuthi ngoJuni 4, 2023, ngemuva kokuqedwa kokuwasha okungcwele (Devasnana (Devasnāna)), amachaphaza egazi abonwa kuSnana Mandapa. Ngisho nanamuhla, ngo-Okthoba 13, 2023, ngenxa yengxabano enobudlova phakathi kwabakhonzi ababili mayelana nokusatshalaliswa kwePrasad ngokushesha ngemuva kwe-'Madhyahna Dhupa' eShrimandir, amachaphaza egazi awele egumbini elingcwele kakhulu lethempeli. Ngonyaka ka-2024 futhi, ekuseni kakhulu ngo-Ephreli 23, ngemuva kokuvulwa kweSinghadwara, owesifazane ovela emndenini wabakhonzi wabeka isandla sakhe emnyango wethempeli lapho umunwe wakhe wacindezelwa kuwo. Ngenxa yalokho, igazi laphuma emunweni wakhe eduze kweDhukudi Dwara eduze kweBedha yangaphakathi. Kulesi simo, iMahasnana yeNkosi Ephakeme yenziwa ngisho nangaphambi kweMangala Alati. Ngokufanayo, izigameko zokopha ezindaweni zethempeli zaqhubeka nangonyaka ka-2025.

7.2 Ukuwa kwesithombe esingcwele seNkosi Balabhadra ngesikhathi seRath Yatra ka-2024.

Ngesikhathi seRath Yatra yezinsuku ezingu-11 eShri Jagannath Puri, amasiko e-'Adapa Mandapa Bije' aqala ngo-7 PM kusihlwa ngoJulayi 9, 2024. Ngesikhathi salokhu, ngenkathi kuthathwa isithombe esingcwele seNkosi Balabhadra kusuka enqoleni kuya e-Adapa Mandapa yeGundicha Temple (Guṇḍicā Mandira), Yona yawa ngobuso kuCharamala, ngenxa yalokho abakhonzi abangu-12 nabo balimala. Isigameko esinjalo esingajabulisi senzeka okokuqala ngqa emlandweni. Ukuwa kweNkosi Balabhadra ngale ndlela ngokuqinisekile kuwuphawu lwezinhlekelele ezizayo, okukhulunywa ngazo kanje embhalweni 'Niladri' ohlobene nemithetho nezinqubomgomo zeShrikhetra, njengoba kuqondiswe yiNkosi Brahma neNkosi Vishnu.

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୁରବୈରିଣ ଏତସ୍ୟ ଜଗଦୀଶସ୍ୟ ଭୂପତେହଃ।
ଭଦ୍ରାୟାଶ୍ଚ ବଳସ୍ୟାପି ପ୍ରମାଦାତ୍ ପତନଂ ଯଦି।।
ମହାଭୟଂ ନୃପସ୍ୟାପି ରାଜ୍ୟସ୍ୟାପି ଭବେଦ୍ଧ୍ରୁବମ୍।
ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷଂ ମରଣଂ ଚୈବ ଜାୟତେ ଚ ପୁନଃ ପୁନଃ।।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Murabairiṇa etasya jagadīshasya bhūpatehaḥ.
Bhadrāyāshcha balasyāpi pramādāt patanaṃ yadi.
Mahābhayaṃ nṛipasyāpi rājyasyāpi bhabeddhrubam.
Durbhikṣhaṃ maraṇaṃ chaiba jāyate cha punaḥ punaḥ.

Incazelo:
Okungukuthi, "O Nkosi, uma ngephutha uJagannath, uBalabhadra, noma uSubhadra bewa, khona-ke inhlekelele enkulu izokwehlela inkosi nombuso. Indlala izokwenzeka, futhi izifo eziwumshayabhuqe zizoshaya ngokuphindaphindiwe."

Umlilo egumbini elingcwele kakhulu leThempeli laseMahakal (Mahākāl Mandira), e-Ujjain ngonyaka ka-2024

Ngosuku lwangoMsombuluko ekuseni, ngoMashi 25, 2024, ngesikhathi somkhosi weHoli, umlilo waqubuka egumbini elingcwele kakhulu leThempeli laseMahakaleshwar (Mahākāleśvara Mandira) e-Ujjain ngenxa yokuphonswa kwe-'gulal' (impushana enemibala) ngesikhathi seBhasma Aarti. Abantu ababalelwa ku-14, kuhlanganise nompristi omkhulu nabanye abakhonzi, balimala ngenxa yokusha kulesi sigameko.

7.3 Izimpawu ezimbi eziyinhloko ezitholakale eThempelini laseJagannath ngonyaka ka-2025

Izimpawu ezimbi ezitholakale eThempelini laseJagannath zaqhubeka nangonyaka ka-2025. Ngezimpawu eziningi, iNkosi Jagannath yanikeza abazinikele bayo isexwayiso sezinhlekelele ezizayo, ezinye zezimpawu eziyinhloko esizethula phambi kwenu.

(a) Ukuphuka kwe-Akshaya Danda ngoFebhuwari 14

Njengansuku zonke, ntambama ngoFebhuwari 14, 2025 (uLwesihlanu), lapho umkhonzi weChunara efika eNeelachakra phezu kwethempeli ukuze ashintshe iPatitapabana Bana noma ifulegi elikhulu, wathola i-Akshaya Danda (isigxobo soqalo esibamba iPatitapabana Bana (Patitapābana Bāṇa)) iphukile. Ngemuva kwalokhu, abakhonzi ngandlela thile babopha ifulegi kuNeelachakra uqobo.

Ukuphuka kwe-Akshaya Danda ngale ndlela kwalimaza imizwa yabazinikele abaningi, futhi bazwakalisa usizi olukhulu ngakho. Kanye nalokhu, abazinikele abaningi bakubiza njengophawu lwezinhlekelele ezizayo esikhathini esiseduze.

(b) Amachaphaza egazi atholakala ethempelini ngoFebhuwari 16; iMahasnana yezithombe ezingcwele yenziwa

NgoFebhuwari 16 (ngeSonto), ngemuva kokuqedwa kwamasiko asekuseni afana ne-'Dwara Phita Niti' kanye ne-'Mangala Alati', ngokushesha nje lapho i-ukubona okungcwele kweNkosi uJagannath kuqala, ngemuva kwesikhashana, izinceku zathola amabala egazi eduze kwendawo ye-'Bhitar Katha'. Ngokushesha ngemuva kwalesi sigameko, kwenziwa amalungiselelo e-'Bada Mahasnana Niti' yezithombe ezingcwele, futhi i-ukubona okungcwele (Darshan) nayo yamiswa isikhashana.

(c) Ukhozi (Garuda) lundiza luhamba nePatitapabana Bana ngo-Ephreli 13

Ngo-Ephreli 13, 2025, abazinikele beNkosi uJagannath abaseShrimandir yasePuri babona isibonakaliso esingakaze sibonwe. Cishe ngo-5 PM, kwabonakala ukhozi luzungeza ithempeli luphathe iPatitapabana Bana ngomlomo walo. Ngokwemibiko, ukhozi lwazungeza ithempeli izikhathi ezi-4-5 lwabe selundizela olwandle ngaphezu kwesango lasentshonalanga.

Ividiyo yalesi sigameko yasakazeka kakhulu ezinkundleni zokuxhumana. Abazinikele abaningi bazwakalisa usizi lwabo nokukhathazeka ngalokhu. Abazinikele abaningi bakuhlobanisa nezinhlekelele ezizayo, kanti abaningana bakubiza njengesibonakaliso esibi esingakaze sibonwe esinikezwe iNkosi uJagannath.

(d) Amabala egazi atholakala ethempelini ngo-Ephreli 21 kanye neMahasnana yezithombe ezingcwele eyenziwe

Ngo-Ephreli 21 (ngoMsombuluko) nakho, amabala egazi abonakala eduze kweBhitar Katha eShrimandir, ngemuva kwalokho iMahasnana yezithombe ezingcwele zeNkosi yenziwa ngokufanele ngokuya ngesiko. Phakathi naleso sikhathi, i-ukubona okungcwele yeNkosi Ephakeme yabazinikele yaphazamiseka cishe ihora eli-1. Ngokwemibiko, amabala egazi atholakala ngaphakathi ethempelini ngenxa yezingcezu zengilazi ezihlabele unyawo lwephoyisa.

(e) Ukhozi luhlezi phezu kwePatitapabana Bana ngoSepthemba 2

Ukhozi luhlezi phezu kwePatitapabana Bana phezulu kweShrimandir luye lwabonakala izikhathi eziningi eminyakeni embalwa edlule. Njalo lapho kubonakala isigameko esinjalo, siboniswa cishe kuzo zonke iziteshi zezindaba zasendaweni e-Odisha. NgoSepthemba 2, kwenzeka into efanayo futhi lapho ukhozi luqale lwabonakala lundiza eduze kweNeelachakra kwathi ngemuva kwesikhashana lwatholakala luhlezi phezu kwePatitapabana Bana olunamathiselwe phezulu kweNeelachakra.

(f) Ukhozi luhlezi phezu kweAruna Stambha futhi ngoSepthemba 4

Akukhona nje iThempeli likaJagannath, iBhavishya Malika nayo ichaza ukuhlala kokhozi noma enye inyoni embi phezu kweAruna Stambha elisephambi kweSinghadwara yethempeli njengesibonakaliso esibi. NgoSepthemba 4, okungukuthi, ezinsukwini ezimbili nje ngemuva kokuba ukhozi lubonakale phezu kweShrimandir ngoSepthemba 2, ukhozi nalo lwabonakala luhlezi phezu kweAruna Stambha. Abantu bakubona lokhu njengesibonakaliso esibi. Ngalesi sigameko, abaningi bachaza isiprofetho seMalika esabhalwa eminyakeni engu-600 edlule njengesigcwalisekayo.

(g) Ukhozi luhlezi phezu kwethempeli futhi ngoSepthemba 16

Ezinsukwini ezingu-15 nje kusukela ngoSepthemba 2, ekuseni kakhulu ngoSepthemba 16, ukhozi lwaphinde lwabonakala luhlezi phezu kwePatitapabana Bana yethempeli.

(h) Ukhozi lwatholakala luhlezi phezu kwePatitapabana Bana yeShrimandir futhi ngo-Okthoba 14

Ukhozi lwatholakala luhlezi phezu kwethempeli kabili ngenyanga kaSepthemba. Ukulandelana akuzange kume lapha; izakhamuzi zasePuri zabona ukuphindaphindwa kwalesi sigameko futhi ngo-Okthoba 14. Ukuhlala okuphindaphindiwe kokhozi phezu kwethempeli kwachazwa abazinikele abaningi njengesibonakaliso sezinhlekelele ezizayo.

(i) Izinkozi angenakubalwa azungeza phezu kweShrimandir ngoDisemba 9

Njengoba kwenzeka ngoDisemba 7, 2021, nango-Agasti 17, 2022, isibonakaliso esibi kakhulu sabonakala ngoDisemba 9, 2025, lapho izinkozi angenakubalwa atholakala ezungeza phezu kweThempeli likaJagannath. Lesi sigameko sadonsa ukunaka hhayi nje abazinikele e-Puri noma e-Odisha, kodwa nalabo abahlala ngaphandle kwe-Odisha. Abazinikele abaningi bakubiza lesi sigameko njengesibonakaliso somcimbi omkhulu ongalindelekile ozayo esikhathini esiseduze.

7.4 Izibonakaliso ezimbi ezitholwe kwezinye izindawo zokuhlala ezingcwele namathempeli abalulekile ngonyaka ka-2025

Kuqubuka umlilo ezindaweni zeThempeli laseMahakaleshwar e-Ujjain

NgoMeyi 5, 2025, ngo-12 PM, kwaqubuka umlilo egumbini lokulawula lebhodi lokulawula ukungcola eliseduze neShankh Dwara ezindaweni zeThempeli likaBaba Mahakaleshwar elidumile emhlabeni wonke e-Ujjain. Ngemuva kwalesi sigameko, kwaba nokwesaba ezindaweni zethempeli. Ngemuva kokuqubuka komlilo, ukungena kwabazinikele ethempelini kwamishwa isikhashana.

Kuqubuka umlilo ezindaweni zePeetha kaMahapurush Achyutananda Das

Ngosuku lweDevasnana Purnima, ngoJuni 11, 2025, umlilo oqubuka ezindaweni zeNemal Padmabana Peetha kaMahapurush Achyutananda Das eliseNischintakoili, eCuttack nawo wabonwa njengesibonakaliso esibi kakhulu abazinikele beMalika. Kwaba nomoya wokwesaba phakathi kwesixuku sezinkulungwane zabazinikele lapho isitendi sesibani esigcinwe ezindaweni sishaya umlilo ngokungazelelwe, futhi ngokushesha, kwaqala ukuqubuka amalangabi avuthayo.

Isihlahla somkhomo saseNemal (Nemal) sigawulwa uhulumeni

Ekuqaleni kuka-Agasti, iningi lezigodo zesihlahla somkhomo esidala kakhulu esiphambi kwethempeli eliseSadhana Peetha yombhali oyinhloko wemibhalo yeMalika, umuntu ongcwele omkhulu uShri Achyutananda Das, zagawulwa. Ngokwezinsolo, lesi sinyathelo sathathwa uhulumeni ngomsebenzi wokuthuthukisa iPeetha kaAchyutananda Das. Kwaba nokuphikiswa okukhulu ngalokhu ngabantu bendawo nabapristi, futhi ngemuva kokubhikisha kwabo, amagatsha ambalwa kuphela esihlahla somkhomo asele engagawuliwe. Kumele kuqashelwe ukuthi uMahapurush Achyutananda Das waqamba imibhalo yeMalika ehlezi ngaphansi kwe-'Nemal Banyan Tree' esesimweni sokuzindla. Ukugawulwa kwesihlahla somkhomo saseNemal kwabonwa njengesibonakaliso esibi kakhulu abazinikele beMalika.

Ifulegi legolide elifakwe esiqongweni seThempeli laseMahakaleshwar liwa

NgoJuni 1, 2025, isibonakaliso esibi saphinde sabonakala sisuka eThempelini laseMahakaleshwar elidumile emhlabeni wonke elise-Ujjain. Eqinisweni, ngenxa yemimoya enamandla, ifulegi legolide elifakwe esiqongweni seThempeli laseMahakaleshwar lawa ngokungazelelwe. Umpristi wethempeli wachaza lesi sigameko njengendaba ekhathazayo ngokombono wezenkolo.

Imaski yeNkosi uShiva eyenziwe eShivalinga eThempelini laseMahakaleshwar iyaphuka iwe

Isigameko esiyingqayizivele senzeka ngo-Agasti 18 eThempelini laseMahakal elise-Ujjain. Lapha, njengansuku zonke ngo-8:00 PM ngesikhathi sokuhlobisa (Shringara) kweShivalinga, imaski yeNkosi uShiva yenziwa ngeBhang (unama wensangu). Inani elikhulu leBhang lafakwa ngalesi sikhathi. Ngokushesha ngemuva kwalokhu, i-Aarti yayisizokuqala lapho iBhang Shringara iphuka iwa. Ikhala, izindebe, neso elilodwa kwaphuka emaskini weNkosi uShiva kwawa.

Umbono ongavamile obonwe eThempelini likaMaa Samaleswari

Kusuka ethempelini elidumile likaMaa Samaleswari eliseLakhanpur, eJharsuguda, e-Odisha, ngobusuku boLwesine, ngo-Agasti 21, 2025, abazinikele bathola isibonakaliso soNkulunkulukazi eshiya isithombe Sakhe esingcwele ephuma. Lapho umpristi evula iminyango yethempeli ekuseni kakhulu nge-'Mangala Alati', kwabonakala izinyawo eziya ngaphandle, ezipendwe nge-Alta (idayi elibomvu), ezingezikaNkulunkulukazi, futhi kanye nalokhu, inkemba Yakhe (Khanda) kanye nomkhonto (Trishul), okwakunamathiselwe esithombeni esingcwele sikaNkulunkulukazi, kwatholakala kuwile eceleni.

Ngokwe-'Bhavishya Malika', esigabeni sokugcina seKali Yuga, ngaphambi kokusungulwa kweDharma, bonke oNkulunkulukazi bazoshiya izindawo zabo baphume ukuze benze umsebenzi wokubhubhisa ababi nezoni. Lesi sigameko siwubufakazi bokuthi leso sikhathi sesifikile nokuthi leso siprofetho siyagcwaliseka.

Umlilo uqubuka ezithombeni ezingcwele eThempelini laseBrahmagiri Jagannath

Ngo-Agasti 28, 2025, izithombe zeNkosi uJagannath, umfowabo omdala uBalabhadra, nomama uSubhadra zashiswa umlilo eThempelini laseBrahmagiri Jagannath eliseKandagoda ngaphansi kwesifunda sasePuri e-Odisha. Empeleni, ngemuva kokuqedwa kokukhulekela kwakusihlwa, abapristi baya emakhaya abo. Ngo-8:30 PM, ngenxa yentuthu ephuma ethempelini, lapho umnyango wethempeli uphulwa, kwabonakala ukuthi izithombe ngaphakathi zazivutha. Ngemuva kokubonisana neMuktimandapa, iShaligrama yakhulekelwa kusukela ngalolo suku kuya phambili. Ngemuva kokuqoqwa kweDaru (ukhuni olungcwele) kuNabakalebara oluzayo, kwafakwa izithombe ezintsha.

Isibonakaliso sokubhujiswa okuzayo esitholwe eThempelini laseLingaraj, isigxobo sefulegi sethempeli siyaphuka

NgoMsombuluko, Septhemba 15, 2025, ngenxa yolaka lwezulu elikhulu nomoya, isigxobo sefulegi seThempeli laseLingaraj elakhiwa ngekhulu le-11 eliseBhubaneswar saphuka. NgoLwesibili ekuseni, ngemuva kokuba sibonwe izinceku, ifulegi laphakanyiswa esigxobeni esisha.

Ifulegi elisesiqongweni seThempeli laseLingaraj eliseBhubaneswar lidabuka ngenxa yemvula enkulu nesiphepho. Indawo engcwele kakhulu (Sanctum sanctorum) ithintwa ukushaywa umbani; ubumnyama bugubuzela ithempeli.

Phakathi kwemvula enkulu nesiphepho esinamandla ngoLwesithathu, Okthoba 8, 2025, iThempeli laseLingaraj eliseBhubaneswar, inhloko-dolobha yase-Odisha, laba yisisulu sokushaywa umbani. Lokhu nakho kwakuyisibonakaliso somcimbi ongemuhle. Ifulegi lethempeli ladabuka ngenxa yesiphepho, kanti amakhamera e-CCTV ethempeli kanye nesistimu yokukhanyisa konakaliswa ukushaywa umbani. Kwabikwa ukuthi abazinikele nezinceku ababekhona ethempelini nabo bathola ukushaqeka kukagesi.

(j) Okokuqala emlandweni walo weminyaka engu-500, iBal Bhog ayinikelwanga eThempelini laseBanke Bihari eVrindavan

UThakur Banke Bihari, owabonakala eminyakeni engaba amakhulu amahlanu edlule, unikelwa iBhog (umnikelo wokudla) ngezikhathi ezine zosuku. Ngaphambi kokubeka isihlalo seDarshan yasekuseni, kunikelwa iBal Bhog. Ngaphambi kokuba ithempeli livalwe ntambama, kunikelwa iRaj Bhog, futhi lapho iminyango ivulwa kusihlwa, kunikelwa iUtthapan Bhog. Lapho iminyango ivalwa, kunikelwa iShayan Bhog. Inkontileka yokulungisa iBhog inikezwa umenzi wamaswidi (Halwai). Umenzi wamaswidi ulungisa iBhog ayinikeze izinceku, bese zona zinikela iBhog.

Kodwa ngoDisemba 15 (uMsombuluko) ngo-8:45 AM, iminyango yethempeli yavuleka, kodwa umenzi wamaswidi akafikanga neBal Bhog. Lapho kuxhunyanwa naye, kwatholakala ukuthi umenzi wamaswidi akafikanga ngenxa yokungakhokhelwa kwezimali zenyanga. Okokuqala emlandweni walo weminyaka engu-500, kwenzeka into enjalo lapho iBal Bhog ingakwazanga ukunikelwa kuBanke Bihari Ji ngalolo suku.

7.5 Ukuhlelwa kweRath Yatra ngesikhathi esingafanele

Njengoba sonke sazi, iRath Yatra ihlelwa minyaka yonke ngeDwitiya Tithi yeShukla Paksha yenyanga ye-Ashadha. Lesi siko sihlale silandelwa kwamanye amathempeli aseJagannath eNdiya naphesheya, kuhlanganise neThempeli laseJagannath e-Puri. Kodwa eminyakeni embalwa edlule, kuye kwaba nezikhathi eziningi lapho i-ISKCON ibonakala iphula lesi siko.

Kulolu daba, ngemuva kokuba kuphakanyiswe iziphikiso yiMphathi Omkhulu weShree Jagannath Temple Administration kanye noSihlalo weShree Jagannath Temple Managing Committee, uGajapati Maharaj Divyasingha Deva, futhi kwenziwa isikhalazo esibhaliwe endlunkulu ye-ISKCON eseMayapur, ngempela kwenzeka intuthuko ethile, kodwa i-ISKCON isabonakala ihlela amaRath Yatra angahleliwe eNdiya ikakhulukazi phesheya.

Kulolu chungechunge, i-ISKCON muva nje yahlela iRath Yatra eTaiwan ngoNovemba 23, 2025, eyagxekwa umphakathi wase-Odia emhlabeni wonke kanye nabazinikele abaningi bakaJagannath base-Odisha. Abazinikele bakubheka njengokungagcini nje ngokwephula imithetho yendabuko kodwa futhi bakubheka njengokungawuhloniphi umyalo weNkosi uJagannath uqobo.


7.6 Iphutha ekhalendeni lesiNgisi lika-2026 elanyatheliswa yiShrimandir Administration

Muva nje, kuvele iphutha elikhulu ekhalendeni lesiNgisi lika-2026 elikhishwe yiJagannath Temple Administration. Empeleni, isithombe seRatnabedi sishicilelwe ekhalendeni lapho ukulandelana kweTrinity kungalungile. Ngokombono wombukeli, esithombeni sekhalenda, iNkosi uJagannath ibekwe ngakwesokunxele kakhulu, bese kuba uNkulunkulukazi uSubhadra, bese kuba umfowabo omdala uBalabhadra, kanti ekulandelaneni okulungile, iNkosi uBalabhadra kufanele ibe ngakwesokunxele kakhulu, bese kuba uNkulunkulukazi uSubhadra, bese kuba uShri Jagannath. Okusho ukuthi, iphutha ukuthi ekhalendeni, isithombe somfowabo omdala uBalabhadra sishicilelwe esikhundleni seNkosi uJagannath, nesithombe seNkosi uJagannath sisesikhundleni seNkosi uBalabhadra. Ngaphezu kwalokhu, uChakraraj Sudarshan akazange anikezwe indawo esithombeni seRatnasinghasana ekhalendeni. Akugcini lapho, isithombe seRath Yatra siphinde sishicilelwe ekhalendeni, esibonisa inqola kaMama uSubhadra kuqala, ilandelwe inqola yeNkosi Ephakeme uJagannath, bese kuba inqola yeNkosi uBalabhadra.

Ngenxa yala maphutha, kukhona ukunganeliseki nenzondo ezingqondweni zabazinikele. Kukhona nentukuthelo phakathi kwezinceku zeThempeli laseJagannath mayelana nalesi sigameko.
