Ngokusho kwabanye abazinikele, lokhu kuyisibonakaliso esinikezwe yiNkosi uJagannath uqobo sezinhlekelele ezizayo kanye noshintsho ohlelweni lomhlaba ngonyaka omusha.

8 Ukuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki – Nini futhi Kuphi?

Ngokusho kweBhavishya Malika, iNkosi uKalki isivele yazalwa. Akukhona nje iNkosi uKalki, kodwa nazo zonke izilwi eziqhawe zesikhathi seMahabharata njengoYudhishthira, uBhima, uArjuna, uNakula, uSahadeva, uBhishma, uDrona, uAshwatthama, uKarna, uShalya, uJayadratha, uBarbarika, uAbhimanyu, uBabruvahana, uEkalavya, uGhatotkacha, uDuryodhana, uShakuni, njll., bonke bazalwe kabusha kulo mhlaba. Ngokusho kweMalika, kulokhu zonke lezi zilwi zizolwa ndawonye ohlangothini lweDharma ngokumelene ne-Adharma.

Nokho, ngeke sixoxe ngezinye izilwi lapha futhi sizokhawulela amazwi ethu kuphela kuParahbrahma Narayana Mahavishnu iNkosi uKalki Ram.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯୋଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Nīḷachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṅgā adhara,
yojanagre janama hoibe Biṣṇusharmā gṛuhara.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho iPatitapabana Bana (ifulegi) yeThempeli lasePuri (Puri) Jagannath indiza isuke endaweni yayo iye kwenye indawo, ngaso leso sikhathi, uMahaprabhu Kalki uzalelwa eJajanagar noma eJajpur yase-Odisha endlini yeBrahmin ohlabelela izindumiso zeNkosi uVishnu.

Kufanele ngicacisele abafundi ukuthi lesi sigameko sibonwe izikhathi eziningi eThempelini laseJagannath, isibonelo ngonyaka ka-1990-91, ngonyaka ka-2005, bese kuba ngesikhathi seCyclone Fani neCyclone Amphan ngonyaka ka-2019 no-2020 ngokulandelana, njll.

UMahapurush wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ukuthi ukuzalwa kukaMahaprabhu Kalki kuzokwenzeka kuphela ngemuva kwalesi sigameko. Njengoba lesi sigameko senzekile, kungashiwo ngokuqiniseka ukuthi iNkosi uKalki isizalwe. Kodwa njengoba lesi sigameko senzeke eminyakeni eminingana ehlukene, akubonakali kungenzeka ukufinyelela unyaka othize wokuzalwa kukaMahaprabhu kulesi sigameko kuphela.

Ukuze sazi ukuthi uMahaprabhu wazalwa ngamuphi unyaka, kuzofanele sisebenzise amanye amavesi eMalika.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।
କେଉଁ ଦିନ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ କହେ ରାମଦାସ ଯହିଁ,
ବିଶାଖାପାଟଣା ନୃପ ନାଶ ହେଲେ ମୁହିଁ ବିକାଶ ହୁଅଇ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Māgha saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājatwa karibe ehi Ekāmra nagara.
Keuñ dina tumbhe prakāsha hoiba kahe Rāmadāsa yahiñ,
Viśākhāpāṭaṇā nṛpa nāśa hele muhiñ bikāsha huai.

Incazelo:
Ngamavesi angenhla, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das utshela umfundi wakhe uRamadas ukuthi lapho inkosi egama layo linguShri Dibyasingha Deb ibusa ePuri, ngaleso sikhathi uAnanta Keshari, okungukuthi, uMahaprabhu Kalki, uzobusa e-Ekamra Nagar noma eBhubaneswar. Kuzoba usuku lwesikhombisa (Saptami Tithi) lwenyanga kaMagha. UMahapurush uphinde athi ngaleso sikhathi, nami ngizohlala neNkosi ngikhule. Lokhu kuzokwenzeka lapho iNkosi yaseVisakhapatnam (okungukuthi, uNdunankulu wase-Andhra Pradesh (Andhra Pradesh)) ifa.

Uma sicabangisisa, sizothola ukuthi ngesibonakaliso, uMahapurush ukhuluma ngokufa kukaNdunankulu wase-Andhra Pradesh, uY.S. Rajasekhara Reddy, okwenzeka engozini yendiza enophephela emhlane ngonyaka ka-2009. Kuleli vesi elingenhla, uMahapurush akasho unyaka wokuzalwa kweNkosi, kodwa uqinisekisa ukuthi cishe ngonyaka ka-2009, izenzo zaphezulu (leelas) kaKalki izoqhubeka.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala ku-‘Jaiphula Malika’ yakhe –

I-Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଚୈତନ୍ୟ,
ଜନ୍ମିଛନ୍ତି ଆସି ଏହି ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସତଚାଳିଶକୁ ତୁହି ଘେନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Sehi Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Chaitanya,
janmichhanti āsi ehi martyeṇa lo jāiphula,
satachāḷishaku tuhi ghena.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi efanayo eyefika ngaphambili njengoShri Krishna noShri Chaitanya, izoza emhlabeni wabafayo ngesimo sikaKalki (Kalki) enombolweni 47.

Kuyaziwa kahle ukuthi le nombolo 47 ingeyeNkosi yasePuri, uShri Dibyasingha Deb, ephakathi kweminyaka ka-2005 no-2008.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Kalpatika’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଦ୍ରି ଛାଡି ପ୍ରଭୁ ପୀତବାସ,
ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟରେ ଜନ୍ମ ଲଭିବେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।
ଗୁଣରେ ଭେଦି ଭେଦ ଅଙ୍କେ ଜାଣ,
ଦେଲେ ଯେତେ ସାଲ ହେଲା ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ଏହି ସାଲରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିବେ,
ଅଙ୍କ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଯେ କହିଲୁ ଏବେ।
ବିଂଶ ରେବ ଅଙ୍କ ଯେବଣ ଦିନ,
ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଉଦେ ଗଗନ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ଲକ୍ଷଣ ଦେଖିବୁ ଯେବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇଥିବେ ତେବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Nīḷādri chhāḍi prabhu pītabāsa,
martyare janma labhibe abashya.
guṇare bhedi bheda aṅke jāṇa,
dele jete sāla helā pramāṇa.
ehi sālare prabhu janamibe,
aṅka pramāṇa je kahilu ebe.
biṃsha reba aṅka jebaṇa dina,
indu pare bindu ude gagana.
emanta lakṣaṇa dekhibu jebe,
prabhu janma hoithibe tebe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, uMahaprabhu ngokuqinisekile uzothatha ukuzalwa emhlabeni ngemuva kokushiya indawo yakhe yaseJagannath. Ngesikhathi esho unyaka wokuzalwa kukaMahaprabhu, uMahapurush uthi lapho ufaka amashumi amabili (20) enombolweni yeRevati Nakshatra (27), khona-ke inombolo ephumayo kufanele iqondwe njengonyaka wokuzalwa kweNkosi. Okusho ukuthi, 20+27 = 47.

Kuyaziwa kahle ukuthi le nombolo ingunyaka wokubusa (Anka) weNkosi yasePuri, uDibyasingha Deb, okukhomba komunye weminyaka ephakathi kuka-2005 no-2008.

UMahapurush waphinda wabhala komunye wemibhalo yakhe yeMalika –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଥାମାନ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଘଟିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ବାନା ଧରି ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଧରିଣ କଳକୀ ବେଶ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ehi kathāmāna nishchaya ghaṭiba Dibyasiṃha pañchachāḷisha,
shūnya bānā dhari āsibe Shrī Hari dhariṇa Kaḷakī besha.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho kungunyaka wama-45 kaMaharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb wasePuri, khona-ke iNkosi uShri Hari izothatha ukuzalwa kukaKalki yehle emhlabeni.

Kuphawuleka ukuthi unyaka wama-45 weNkosi uShri Dibyasingha Deb wasePuri ukhomba onyakeni ophakathi kuka-2005 no-2008.

U-Achyutananda Das Ji wabuye wabhala encwadini yakhe iMalika –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅତି ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ହୋଇବ ସଂସାର,
କାହାରି ନ ଥିବ କୁଳ ବିଚାର।
ବହୁତ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସଂସାରେ ହେବ,
ନିର୍ଭୟ ହୋଇ ଜଣେ କେ ନଥିବ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶେ,
ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କଳକୀ ବେଶେ।
ନାଶିବେ ଯାବତ୍ ଅବନୀଭାର,
ଏଇଠୁ ହୋଇବ ପୃଥବୀ ସଂହାର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ati anyāya hoiba saṃsāra,
kāhāri na thiba kuḷa bichāra.
bahuta utpāta saṃsāre heba,
nirbhaya hoi jaṇe ke nathiba.
Dibyasiṃha deba pañchachāliśe,
āsibe Śrī Hari Kaḷakī beśe.
nāśibe jābat abanībhāra,
eithu hoiba pṛuthabī saṃhāra.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, kuyoba nokungalungi okukhulu emhlabeni. Akekho oyoba nezinto zomndeni noma alandele iDharma. Kuzoba nesiphithiphithi yonke indawo; akekho oyochitha izinsuku zakhe ngokuthula nenjabulo. Ngaleso sikhathi, ngonyaka wama-45 wenkosi egama layo linguDibyasingha Deb (phakathi kuka-2005-2008), iNkosi uShri Hari iyokwehla kulo mhlaba ofayo ngesimo sikaKalki. Iyosusa umthwalo wesono emhlabeni, ibhubhise ababi, futhi iqede ama-'Mlechhas'.

AmaMahapurusha asho ukuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki ezindaweni eziningana emibhalweni eminingi.

Kwenye yezincwadi zakhe zeMalika, ‘iKalikalpa Gita’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala –

I-Odia Sloka:

ବିରଜା ପୀଠ ବୈତରଣୀ ନଦୀ,
ଦଶାଶନୀ ଘାଟ ଉଦୟ ଭେଦି।
ସପତ ମାତୃକା ଟେକିଅଛି ଶିର,
ତାଙ୍କ ଅଧରୁ ବହିବ ରୁଧିର।
ସେହି ସଂକେତକୁ ଜାଣିଥାଅ ରାମ,
କଳଙ୍କି ଉଦୟ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Birajā pīṭha Baitaraṇī nadī,
Dashāshanī ghāṭa udaya bhedi.
Sapata mātṛkā ṭekiachhi shira,
tāṅka adharu bahiba rudhira.
Sehi saṅketaku jāṇithāa Rāma,
Kaḷaṅki udaya nishchaya jāṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, uMfula iBaitarani ugelezela eduze kweThempeli laseBiraja eliseSifundeni saseJajpur e-Odisha. Eduze kweDashashwamedha Ghat yalo Mfula iBaitarani kukhona ithempeli lamaSaptamatruka, lapho kukhona oNkulunkulukazi Abayisikhombisa Bomama—okungukuthi uChamunda, uVarahi, uIndrani, uVaishnavi, uBrahmani, uKaumari, uMaheshwari, noNarasimhi.

UMahapurush ubhala ukuthi lapho igazi ligeleza ezindebeni, okungukuthi, emilonyeni, yoMama bamaSaptamatruka, kuyoba uphawu okufanele abazinikele (bhaktas) baqonde ngalo ukuthi iNkosi uKalki isizalwe noma ukuzalwa kwayo kuseduze kakhulu.

Kwaziwa kahle ukuthi lesi sigameko sesivele senzekile eThempelini lamaSaptamatruka, okufakazela ukuthi isiprofetho sikaMahapurush Achyutananda Das siyiqiniso.

Ngokusho kwabapristi beThempeli laseBiraja, eJajpur, kanye neThempeli lamaSaptamatruka, kanye nabantu bendawo lapho, ukuphuma kwegazi emilonyeni yoMama bamaSaptamatruka sekwenzekile ngonyaka ka-2005.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ‘iShibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଯେ ଦିନ ବିରଜା ଆସ୍ଥାନ ଛୋଡ଼ିବ ଭୀମ ସିଂହନାଦ ହେବ,
ଗର୍ଭସ୍ଥିତ ଶିଶୁଭୂମିରେ ପଡ଼ିବେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ଯିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Je dina Birajā āsthāna choḍiba bhīma siṃhanāda heba,
garbhasthita śiśubhūmire paḍibe Bhubaneśwaraku jiba.

Incazelo:
Lapha, uMahapurush uchaza isikhathi sokuzalwa kukaMahaprabhu ngolimi olungokomfanekiso. Ubhala ukuthi ngosuku uMaa Biraja eshiya ngalo isihlalo sakhe, ngaleso sikhathi ingane (iNkosi uKalki) iyongena esibelethweni sikanina, ozohamba kamuva aye eBhubaneswar.

Kwaziwa kahle ukuthi uMaa Biraja washiya isihlalo sakhe ngonyaka ka-2004. Ngenyanga kaSepthemba 2004, abanye abantu bezepolitiki bahlela futhi beba isithombe sikaMaa Biraja (esifakwe eThempelini laseBiraja (Biraja), eJajpur). Ngemuva kwezinyanga eziyisi-6, ngoMashi 2005, isithombe (murti) sikaMama sabuyela ethempelini.

Kulezi zindimana eziyisithupha ezingenhla, kufakazelwe ngokucacile ukuthi uMahaprabhu Kalki usizalwe, futhi isikhathi sakhe siphakathi konyaka ka-2005-2008. Ngakho-ke, bonke abafundi mabacacelwe ukuthi i-avatar kaKalki ikhona emhlabeni njengamanje.

8.1 UMahaprabhu uzalelwe kuphi?

Ngokusho kwamaPurana (Puranas), indawo yokuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki yidolobhana laseSambhal. IBhavishya Malika iphinde isho ukuthi iNkosi uKalki iyozalelwa edolobhaneni laseSambhal. Wonke umuntu uyavumelana ngaleli phuzu.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ‘iChakada Madana’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ କଷଣ ଦେଖି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଭଗବାନ,
କଳିରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇଣ।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ପିତାଙ୍କର ନାମଟି ଅଟଇ,
ମାତା ନାମ ସୁମତି ଯେ କହିଲୁ ବୁଝାଇ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhaktaṅka kaṣaṇa dekhi prabhu bhagabāna,
Kaḷire Sambaḷa nagre janama hoiṇa.
Biṣṇusharmā pitāṅkara nāmaṭi aṭai,
mātā nāma Sumati je kahilu bujhāi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ebona usizi lwabazinikele esiqongweni senkathi yeKali, iNkosi uKalki iyozalelwa eSambal Nagar. Igama likayise kaMahaprabhu liyoba uVishnusharma negama likanina liyoba uSumati.

UMahapurush wabuye wabhala ku-‘Chakada Madana’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ହେବେ ଜନମ,
ଛତିଆ ବଟରେ ଲାଗିବ ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି ସଂଗ୍ରାମ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Sambaḷa nagare prabhu se gupte hebe janama,
Chhatiā baṭare lāgiba dibārātri saṅgrāma.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, uMahaprabhu Kalki uyozalelwa ngasese eSambal Nagar. Impi noma ukulwa okungapheli kuyogqashuka eChhatia Bata.

U-Achyutananda Das Ji wabuye wabhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ‘iChakada Madana’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ମାରୁ ମହରଗ ରାଜକର ଆସି ଘରେ ଘରେ ପଶିଯିବ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ କଳ୍କୀ ଉଦୟ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Māru maharaga rājakara āsi ghare ghare paśijiba,
bāraṇa beḷare Sambaḷanagre Kaḷkī udaya hoiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho ukufa, indlala, nezintela kungena kuwo wonke amakhaya nokungahlaliseki kusakazeka yonke indawo, iNkosi uKalki iyovela eSambal Nagar ngaleso sikhathi sokugcina seKaliyuga.

UMahapurush wabuye wabhala ku-‘Chakada Madana’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

କହନ୍ତି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଭକତ ମୋ ଗଳା ମାଳ,
ଭକତ ନିମନ୍ତେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ ହେବି କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kahanti Achyuta śuṇa Rāmadāsa bhakata mo gaḷā māḷa,
bhakata nimante Sambaḷanagre hebi Kaḷkī abatāra.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku-‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳକୀର ସୀମା କାଳ ପୂରିଗଲେ କଳ୍କୀ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଯଶା ପୁରେ,
ସମ୍ବଳ ଗ୍ରାମରେ ଜାତ ହୋଇଥିବେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାର କାଳରେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷakīra sīmā kāḷa pūrigale Kaḷkī Biṣṇujashā pure,
Sambaḷa grāmare jāta hoithibe mlechha saṃhāra kāḷare.

Incazelo:
"Lapho isikhathi seKaliyuga sesiphelile, uKalki uyozalelwa endlini kaVishnuyasha edolobhaneni laseSambal ukuze kubhujiswe amaMlechhas."

Nokho, lapho 'iSambal' ikhona kusengumthombo wokungaboni ngaso linye emiqondweni yabantu abaningi nanamuhla. Kulesi sihloko, sizokuxazulula lokhu.

8.2 Yini ngempela iBhavishya Malika eyishoyo ngalesi sihloko?

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ‘iJaiphula Malika’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ବେଳ କାଳ ଜାଣି,
ଓଡିଶାରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନ୍ମିବେ ପୁଣି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କେହି ତାଙ୍କ ମାୟାକୁ ନ ଚିହ୍ନି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Sehi beḷa kāḷa jāṇi,
Oḍishāre prabhu janmibe puṇi lo jāiphula,
kehi tāṅka māyāku na chinhi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngaleso sikhathi lapho isono sisesiqongweni saso, iNkosi uKalki iyozalelwa esifundazweni sase-Odisha. Inkohliso (maya) yeNkosi iyoba namandla kangangokuthi akekho oyokwazi ukumyazi.

UMahapurush uphinde abhale kwenye yezincwadi zakhe zeMalika –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁରେ କଳ୍କୀର ଜନମ,
ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ରାଜହଂସ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଜନମି ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ଏହା କହିଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ୍ୟ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Utkala bhuiñre Kaḷkīra janama,
grāma nāma Rājahansa.
Kaḷkī janami līḷā prakāśiba,
ehā kahili prakāshya.

Incazelo:
"Ukuzalwa kukaKalki kuyoba ezweni lase-Utkala (e-Odisha (Odisha)), edolobhaneni elibizwa ngeRajahansa. Ngemuva kokuzalwa, uKalki uyobonakalisa izenzo zakhe zaphezulu (leela)."

Manje, mayelana nokuthi iNkosi uKalki iyozalelwa kuphi e-Odisha, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das yena uqobo wabhala kwenye incwadi yeMalika, ‘iBhabishya Jatipataka Gita’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଓଡିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ନଦୀ ତୀର,
ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁବିପ୍ର ଦ୍ୱିଜବର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Oḍiśā deśare gupta Gaṅgā nadī tīra,
Sambaḷa nagre Biṣṇubipra dwijabara.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, indawo yokuzalwa kukaMahaprabhu iyoba eduze kwamabhange eGupta Ganga (uMfula iBaitarani (Baitarani)) eSambal Nagar, e-Odisha. Kuleyo ndawo, endlini yeBrahmin omuhle ocula izindumiso zeNkosi uVishnu, uMahaprabhu Kalki uyozalwa.

Kulezi zindimana zombili ezingenhla, kucacile ngokuphelele ukuthi ngokusho kweBhavishya Malika, iNkosi uKalki iyozalelwa eSambal, eliseSifundazweni saseNdiya sase-Odisha.

8.3 Ikuphi iSambal e-Odisha?

Omunye wamaPanchasakha, uMahapurush Jagannath Das, wabhala encwadini yeMalika ‘iBhabishyat Parardha’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରରେ,
ଦୁଇ ଜଣ ଅଛନ୍ତି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଘରେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Jājapura Sambaḷa nagrare,
dui jaṇa achhanti brāhmaṇa ghare.

Incazelo:
Endimaneni engenhla, uMahapurush ubhala – “Jajapura Sambala nagrare”. Okusho ukuthi, “ESambal Nagar yaseJajpur”. Kucacile ukuthi iSambal Nagar iseJajpur.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala encwadini yakhe iMalika ‘iKalikalpa Gita’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷୀର ନଦୀ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଯେ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ଖ୍ୟାତ ଯେ କମଳପୁର ଗୁପ୍ତ ଯେ ସମ୍ବଳ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kṣhīra nadī dakṣhiṇa je Birajā maṇḍala,
khyāta je Kamalapura gupta je Sambaḷa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, eningizimu yoMfula iKhira nasenyakatho yeMahanadi kukhona indawo yethempeli laseBiraja. Eduze kwalesi sifunda saseBiraja kukhona idolobhana elaziwa ngeKamalapur. Yilokhu okubizwa ngasese ngeSambal village.

Ngale ndimana, uMahapurush wembulile ukuthi iSambal iseSifundeni saseBiraja noma esifundeni saseJajpur e-Odisha.

Masikuqonde lapho kutholakala khona iSambal e-Odisha ngezinye izindimana ezimbalwa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ‘Gurubhakti Gita’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘର,
ଜନମିଣ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଚକ୍ରଧର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Biṣṇusharmā ghara,
janamiṇa tapasyā karibe Chakradhara.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, uChakradhara (iNkosi uVishnu (Vishnu)) uyozalelwa endlini yomkhulekeli ohlala eSambal Nagar, eGaya, futhi ngemuva kwalokho enze ukuzindla okungcwele.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das waphinda wabala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଜାତ,
ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Biṣṇusharmā gruhe prabhu hoibe jāta,
Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Harihara kṣhetra.

Incazelo:
"INkosi iyozalelwa endlini kaVishnusharma, esifundeni saseHarihara saseSambal Nagar, eGaya."

Ngokwezindimana ezimbili ezingenhla, ukuzalwa kweNkosi (iNkosi) kuzokwenzeka eSambal Nagar yaseGaya (Gaya). Manje, ukuthi iyiphi iGaya leyo nokuthi ikuphi e-Odisha kwatshelwa yiNkosi uKrishna uqobo kumaGopas (Gopas) akhe ayishumi nambili, okwacashunwa uMahapurush Achyutananda Das encwadini yakhe yeMalika ‘Biraja Mahatmya’. UMahapurush wabala –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାର ସୁତ ନିହାର ବଚନ ଏ ଅଟେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଠାର,
ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥେ ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଗ୍ରାମଟି ସମ୍ବଳ ପୁର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Śuṇa bāra suta nihāra bachana e aṭe Achyuta ṭhāra,
Nābhigayā tīrthe Harihara kṣhetra grāmaṭi Sambaḷa pura.

Incazelo:
"Lalelani nina madodana ayishumi nambili, leli yizwi eliqinisekile elivela ku-Achyuta: isigodi iSambalpur sisesifundeni saseHarihara esizeni esingcwele saseNabhigaya."

Okusho ukuthi, isifunda saseHarihara sisesizeni esingcwele saseNabhigaya, esibizwa ngeSambal.

Ezingxoxweni ezingenhla, sibonile ezindaweni eziningi ukuthi uMahapurush wachaza indawo yokuzalwa kaMahaprabhu njengesifunda saseBiraja noma isiza esingcwele saseGaya (Nabhigaya (Nabhigaya)). Manje sizokwazi kahle ukuthi lezi zifunda zikuphi, ukuthi yiziphi izinkolelo zasenganekwaneni ezihlobene nazo, nokuthi zibaluleke ngani.

8.3.1 Isifunda saseBiraja

I-'Biraja Temple' yithempeli elikhulu elitholakala emabhange oMfula iBaitarani eJajpur, e-Odisha, lapho kukhulekelwa khona u-Adi Shakti ngesimo sika-'Biraja'. Empeleni, ezikhathini zasendulo, uBrahma Ji uqobo lwakhe wenza iYajna (umhlatshelo) lapha eBrahmakunda, ngemuva kwalokho uMama uParvati waphuma emlilweni womhlatshelo futhi wayala uBrahma Ji ukuba amise yena ngegama elithi 'Biraja'. Lesi sifunda sibizwa nangokuthi isifunda saseBiraja ngegama likaNkulunkulukazi uBiraja.

Kwakungale ndawo lapho kwawa khona inkaba (Nabhi) kaNkulunkulukazi uSati, okwenza kube ngesinye sezindawo ezingcwele ezingama-51 zamaShakti Peethas. Leli yilona kuphela ithempeli lapho uNkulunkulukazi ekhulekelwa khona ngesimo sikaMahishasura Mardini onezingalo ezimbili.

Le ndawo idume nangokuthi iNabhigaya ngePinda Daan (iminikelo yabokhokho). Empeleni, yilona kuphela iShakti Peetha lapho kwenziwa khona iPinda Daan. Eduze neBrahmakunda, kukhona umthombo ojulile obizwa ngeNabhikunda, ngamanzi awo abantu benza iPinda Daan kubokhokho babo. Le ndawo iphinde ikhulunywe naseSkanda Purana.

8.3.2 INabhigaya

Kukhulunywa kumaPuranas ukuthi eSatya Yuga, kwakukhona idimoni elibizwa ngeGayasura. Yize azalelwa esizweni samadimoni, inhliziyo yakhe yaphefumulelwa yiDharma nokuzindla okungcwele. Wayekholwa kakhulu, enamandla amakhulu, futhi enomzimba omkhulu. Wayehlala esebenzela inhlalakahle yomhlaba kanye nenzuzo yabanye. Kukhulunywa kumaPuranas ukuthi idimoni lenza ukuzindla okunzima, okwajabulisa iNkosi uVishnu, futhi iNkosi yamcela ukuba acele isibusiso. UGayasura wacela isibusiso esiyingqayizivele. Wathi, “Kwangathi izidalwa zingafinyelela ezulwini ngokuthinta kwami nje.” INkosi uVishnu yamnika lesi sibusiso ibona ukuzindla kwakhe okukhulu. Kodwa ngenxa yalokho, iYamaloka (umhlaba wabafileyo) yaqala ukuba yihlane, ngoba noma ubani uGayasura amthintayo waya ngqo ezulwini. Lokhu kwaba inkinga enkulu konkulunkulu noYamraj. Bonke onkulunkulu basondela kuTrinity (uBrahma (Brahma), uVishnu, uMahesh) futhi bancenga ukuba kutholakale isisombululo kule nkinga. Onkulunkulu bacela iTrinity ukuba imise uGayasura. ITrinity yanquma ukumisa uGayasura, owayebangela izithiyo emithethweni yokudala yize elungile. Bobathathu bathatha isimo samaBrahmins amathathu, baya kuGayasura, bathi bafuna ukwenza iYajna ngokuqhubekayo izinsuku eziyisikhombisa futhi ngalokhu, babedinga indawo enkulu nengcwele. Bacela uGayasura ukuba anikele ngomzimba wakhe ngeYajna, kodwa wayengafanele ukunyakaza kulezi zinsuku eziyisikhombisa. UGayasura wavuma lokhu. UGayasura wasakaza ikhanda lakhe eGaya (eBihar (Bihar)) lapho iNkosi uVishnu yayihlezi khona, wabeka isifunda senkaba yakhe eNabhigaya (eJajpur (Jajpur), e-Odisha) lapho uBrahma Ji wayehlezi khona, futhi wabeka izinyawo zakhe ePithapuram (e-Andhra Pradesh) lapho iNkosi uShiva yayihlezi khona, futhi kanjalo iYajna yaqala. ITrinity yenza iYajna ngokuqhubekayo izinsuku eziyisikhombisa.

UGayasura waqhubeka ebala izinsuku ngokuzwa ukukhala kweqhude ekuseni. Ekwazi lokhu, ngosuku lokugcina, iNkosi uShiva yathatha isimo seqhude futhi yakhala ngaphambi kokuba ubusuku buphele. Ezwa umsindo weqhude, uGayasura wacabanga ukuthi sekuseni futhi wanyakazisa umzimba wakhe ecabanga ukuthi izinsuku eziyisikhombisa seziphelile. ITrinity yavele yabonakala phambi kwakhe yathi uGayasura wephule isithembiso sakhe sokugcina umzimba wakhe unganyakazi izinsuku eziyisikhombisa. Bathi isijeziso sokuphula isithembiso kwakungukufa. UGayasura olungile wakwamukela lokhu ngaphandle kokukhononda. ITrinity yajabula ngokukholwa kwakhe futhi yamcela ukuba acele isibusiso. Khona-ke uGayasura wacela kubo ukuba igama lakhe likhunjulwe kanye neTrinity. Wabacela ukuba bahlobise umzimba wakhe unomphela ngezingubo zamaBrahmins. Waphinde wacela isibusiso sokuthi uma abokhokho banoma ubani benikezwa iminikelo (iPinda Daan (Pinda Daan) neTarpan) kulezi zindawo ezintathu, bangaphinde bazalwe.

Ekwamukela isifiso sikaGayasura, iNkosi uVishnu yathatha isimo sikaGadadhar eGaya, uBrahma Ji ukhulekelwa ngesimo sikaGirija Devi eNabhigaya, futhi iNkosi uShiva ikhulekelwa ePithapuram njengoKukkuteshwar (iNkosi eyathatha isimo seqhude). Ngale ndlela, lezi zindawo ezintathu zaduma ngePinda Daan neTarpan:

  • IShirogaya (Gaya, Bihar)
  • INabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha)
  • IPadagaya (Pithapuram (Pithapuram), Andhra Pradesh)

Ubufakazi bokuthi isiza esingcwele saseNabhigaya sitholakala eJajpur buphinde butholakale emibhalweni yeMalika yamaPanchasakha Mahapurushas.

Encwadini yeMalika ‘Chakada Madana’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁରଠାରେ ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥ ଏକଥା ଜାଣ ସତ୍ୱର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Jājapurathāre Nābhigayā tīrtha ekathā jāṇa satwara.

Incazelo:
"Yazi lokhu ngokuqiniseka okuphelele, isiza esingcwele saseNabhigaya siseJajpur."

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, ngesikhathi ebikezela ukuzalwa kwakhe kweshumi eminyakeni engaba ngu-600 edlule, wabala encwadini yeMalika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦଶମ ଜନମ ମୋହର ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଯାଜପୁର ନାଭିଗୟା ହରିହର ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Daśama janama mohara hoiba prakāśa,
Jājapura Nābhigayā Harihara khyāta.

Incazelo:
"Ukuzalwa kwami kweshumi kuzobonakala esifundeni esidumile saseHarihara saseNabhigaya, eJajpur."

Ngale ndimana futhi, kuyacaca ukuthi isiza esingcwele saseNabhigaya sitholakala eJajpur.

UMahapurush ubhala ngalesi sihloko esifanayo encwadini yakhe yeMalika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ପଞ୍ଚକୋଶ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ନାଭିଗୟା ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘରେ ହେବ ଖେଳ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Jājapura pañchakośa Birajā maṇḍala,
Nābhigayā Biṣṇusharmā ghare heba kheḷa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, isifunda saseBiraja sitholakala ngaphakathi kwendawo engamakhosha amahlanu eJajpur. INkosi uKalki iyozalelwa endlini yomkhulekeli kaVishnu ohlala kuleso sifunda saseBiraja esifanayo, iNabhigaya.

Ngakho-ke, engxoxweni engenhla, kuyacaca ngokuphelele ukuthi iSambal Nagar, isiza esingcwele saseGaya, nesifunda saseBiraja lapho amaPanchasakhas abikezela khona ukuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki, empeleni sitholakala esifundeni saseJajpur sase-Odisha.

Ukuze sithole ukucaca okwengeziwe ngendawo yokuzalwa kweNkosi uKalki, sizothatha ezinye izindimana ezikhulunywe eMalika.

UMahapurush Shishu Ananta Das wabala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଉଶ୍ୱାସୀ ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷkī abatāre Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśi bhaktaṅku uśwāsī dharmaku tumbhe pāḷiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iKalki Avatar iyozalelwa esifundazweni sase-Odisha. UMahaprabhu Kalki uzobhubhisa ababi, ahlenge abazinikele, futhi aphinde amise iDharma eMhlabeni.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, ecaphuna iNkosi, ubhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଦେଖିବ ଜାଣ,
ଆମ୍ଭ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ ଯାଜପୁରେଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Indu pare bindu dekhiba jāṇa,
āmbha ābirbhāba Jājapureṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho kubonakala ichashaza noma inkanyezi ngaphezu kwenyanga esibhakabhakeni, yazi ukuthi ukubonakala koMninimandla onke (Supreme Lord) kuzokwenzeka eJajpur.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, encwadini yakhe yeMalika ‘Jai Birachita Chautisha’, ubhala ephinda amagama eNkosi uqobo lwayo ngqo –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଯେତେବେଳେ ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡିକରି ଯିବୁ,
ଯାଜପୁର ବିପ୍ର ଘରେ ଯାଇଁ ଜନମିବୁ।
ଜଗିଥିବୁ ହେ। ଯେ ଯେତେ କହିଲେ ନ ଶୁଣିବୁ ହେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Jetebeḷe Nīḷāchaḷa chhāḍikari jibu,
Jājapura bipra ghare jāiñ janamibu.
Jagithibu he. Je jete kahile na śuṇibu he.

Incazelo:
"Uma usuka eNilachala (ePuri), uzoya uzalelwe endlini kaBrahmin eJajpur. Qaphela; ungalaleli noma yini umuntu ayishoyo."

Encwadini yeMalika ‘Tatwabodhini’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହେବେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଭାବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Jājanagre prabhu janama hebe,
janama hoibe brāhmaṇa bhābe.

Incazelo:
"INkosi iyozalelwa eJajanagar; Iyoza njengoBrahmin (Brahmin)."

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ogama lakhe lingu-‘Bigyanakalpa’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ମହାପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ,
ଗରୁଡକୁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନି ଲୀଳା ଯେ କରିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Jājanagre Mahāprabhu janama hoibe,
Garuḍaku saṅge gheni līḷā je karibe.

Incazelo:
"UMahaprabhu uzalelwa eJajanagar; ethatha uGaruda kanye naye, uzokwenza umdlalo wakhe waphezulu."

Kusukela emavesini angenhla, akusekho ukungabaza ukuthi ukuzalwa kukaMahaprabhu Kalki kwakuzokwenzeka nakanjani eSambal Nagar, esizeni sokuhambela esingcwele saseNabhigaya, esifundeni saseBiraja, esifundeni saseJajpur, e-Odisha.

Uma sibheka iBhagavata Purana neMahabharata ezabhalwa ngaphambi kweBhavishya Malika, nazo zikhomba eJajpur, e-Odisha mayelana nendawo yokuzalwa yeNkosi (Lord) Kalki.

Ezikhathini zangaphambili, uMaharishi Vedavyas wathi indawo yokuzalwa yeNkosi Kalki kwakuyi-'isigodi saseSambhal' eShrimad Bhagavata Mahapurana. Wayebhale –

ISanskrit Sloka:

संभलग्राममुख्यस्य ब्राह्मणस्य महात्मनः ।
भवने विष्णुयशसः कल्किः प्रादुर्भविष्यति ।।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Śambhalagrāmamukhyasya brāhmaṇasya mahātmanaḥ |
bhavane viṣṇuyaśasaḥ kalkiḥ prādurbhaviṣyati ||

Incazelo:
"Endlini kaBrahmin onomphefumulo omkhulu, umholi wesigodi saseSambhal, ogama lakhe linguVishnuyasha, iNkosi Kalki izobonakala."

Kamuva, ku-'Vanaparva' yeMahabharata, uMaharishi Vyas ngokwakhe wakuguqula lokhu kancane futhi waqamba indawo yokuzalwa yeNkosi Kalki ngokuthi 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama'. Wabhala –

ISanskrit Sloka:

कल्कि विष्णुयशा नाम द्विजः कालप्रचोदितः ।
उत्पत्स्यते महावीर्यो महाबुद्धिपराक्रमः ।।
सम्भूतः सम्भलग्रामे ब्राह्मणावसथे शुभे ।
मनसा तस्य सर्वाणि वाहनान्यायुधानि च ।।
उपस्थास्यन्ति योधाश्च शस्त्राणि कवचानि च ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kalki Vishnuyashā nāma dwijaḥ kālaprachoditaḥ |
utpatsyate mahāvīryo mahābuddhiparākramaḥ ||
sambhūtaḥ sambhalagrāme brāhmaṇāvasathe shubhe |
manasā tasya sarvāṇi vāhanānyāyudhāni cha ||
upasthāsyanti yodhāshcha shastrāṇi kavachāni cha |

Incazelo:
Ngamavesi angenhla, uMaharishi Vyas wathi iNkosi Kalki izozalelwa e-'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama', okusho 'isigodi saseSambhal' 'esakhiwe' noma 'esisunguliwe', isigodi saseSambhal esasisungulwe njengekoloni lamaBrahmin ukuze kwenziwe amaYajna (Yajnas) nokukhulekela. Wabhala ukuthi iNkosi Kalki izozalelwa endlini kaBrahmin omuhle owayehlala kulesi sigodi saseSambhal futhi ehlabelela izindumiso zeNkosi Vishnu.

Manje, uma sihlola futhi sifuna emakhasini omlando, sizothola ukuthi indawo yokuhlala yamaBrahmin eyasungulelwa iYajna nokukhulekela itholakala esifundeni saseJajpur e-Odisha.

Eqinisweni, igama elithi Jajpur lanikezwa ngekhulu le-10 ngemuva kwesihloko seNkosi yaseSomavamsi uJajati (Yayati (Yayati)). INkosi uJajati yasungula leli dolobha futhi yenza i-Abhinav Jajatinagar inhloko-dolobha yayo. Kamuva, iguqula igama layo, yaqale yaba iJajanagar yabe isiba iJajpur. INkosi (King) uJajati Keshari yayilethe cishe amaBrahmin ayi-10,000 evela eKannauj e-Uttar Pradesh futhi yabahlalisa eJajpur, e-Odisha, ukuze benze ama-Ashwamedha Yajnas ayishumi (Dashashwamedha (Dashashwamedha)). Ngisho nanamuhla, iDashashwamedha Ghat emabhange oMfula (River) iBaitarani isikhumbuza ngalesi sigameko. Noma kunjalo, uMaharishi Vyas wayekhombe leli koloni lamaBrahmin elahlala ngasempumalanga yethempeli laseBiraja eJajpur ngokulibiza ngokuthi 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama' ku-'Vanaparva' yeMahabharata.

9 Khandagiri – E-Bhubaneswar noma kwenye indawo?

Noma yimuphi umuntu onentshisekelo kwiBhavishya Malika akanakungabaza ukuthi uMahaprabhu Kalki uzalelwa kuphi. Bazi kahle ukuthi i-avatar kaKalki izozalelwa esigodini saseSambal esiseJajpur (Jajpur), e-Odisha.

Nabo abanakungabaza ukuthi ngemuva kokuzalwa eJajpur, iNkosi (Lord) izohlala endaweni egama layo linguKhandagiri.

Omunye wamaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Shishu Ananta Das, wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଳିଯୁଗେ କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ହରି,
ଶ୍ରୀ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ehi kaliyuge Kalki abatare Hari,
Shri khaṇḍagirire thibe prabhu bije kari.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, eKaliyuga, iNkosi Hari izohlala eKhandagiri ekuzalweni kwayo njengoKalki (Kalki).

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku-‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’ yakhe –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଗୋପନେ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଯେ ଆସି,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ବସି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Gopane Mukunda je asi,
Khandagirire thibe basi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi izohlala ngokuyimfihlo eKhandagiri.

UMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wabhala futhi komunye wemibhalo yakhe yeMalika, 'Patta Madana' –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭକତ ସୁମେଳ ହୋଇ ଏକ ଠୁଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଶେ ଯିବେ,
ଅନନ୍ତ ମୂରତି ଦର୍ଶନ ସାରିଣ ସର୍ବେ ସର୍ବଙ୍କୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhakata sumeḷa hoi eka ṭhuḷa Khaṇḍagiri pāshe jibe,
ananta mūrati darshana sāriṇa sarbe sarbaṅku chinhibe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, endaweni egama layo linguKhandagiri, kuzoba nomhlangano wabazinikele. Lapho, abazinikele bazoba ne-darshan (umbono) yeNkosi Kalki futhi bazobonana.

Kuze kube manje, wonke umuntu uyavuma, kodwa ukungabaza kwabantu kuvela mayelana nokuthi le Khandagiri ikuphi. Namuhla, ngamavidiyo athile nama-PDF, kwenziwa izimangalo ezahlukahlukene mayelana neKhandagiri. Ngokusho kwabanye abantu, le ndawo egama layo linguKhandagiri, lapho iNkosi izohlala khona, itholakala eJajpur, e-Odisha, kanti abanye bathi iKhandagiri itholakala esifundeni saseDhenkanal e-Odisha.

9.1 Ikuphi iKhandagiri?

Ngalesi sihloko, ngifuna ukusho ukuthi noma yimaphi amagama ashiwo ngenhla mayelana neKhandagiri (Jajpur, Dhenkanal), womabili ahluke ngokuphelele.

Echaza ukuthi ikuphi iKhandagiri, omunye wamaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Yasovanta Das, wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Basanta Manjari’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମହିମା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଭକ୍ତ ତହିଁ ସେଠାରେ ଅଛଇ।
ଗୁପତ ଲୀଳା ହୋଇବ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ,
ତପସ୍ୟାରେ ରହିଥିବେ ଋଷିଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗରେ।
ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ସେକାଳରେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବ,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସେହି ଭସାଇଣ ଦେବ।
ତ୍ରିଭୁବନ ସ୍ୱର କଥା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ବାରଭୁଜା ଦୁର୍ଗାମାତା ଜଗି ରହିଛଇଁ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ବାବୁ ଶୁଣ ମନଦେଇ,
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଜଗିଅଛି ସେଠାବକୁ ତହିଁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Khaṇḍagiri mahimā kahita nuhañi,
lakṣha lakṣha bhakta tahin seṭhāre achhai.
Gupata līḷā hoiba Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre,
tapasyāre rahithibe ruṣhiṅka saṅgare.
Gupta Gaṅgā sekāḷare bāhara hoiba,
Bhubaneśwaraku sehi bhasāiṇa deba.
Tribhubana swara kathā kahita nuhañi,
bārabhuja Durgāmātā jagi rahichhaiṃ.
Āhuri kathāe bābu śuṇa manadei,
Bhūāsuṇī jagiachhi seṭhābaku tahiñ.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ubukhazikhazi beKhandagiri abuchazeki. Kunabazinikele abayizigidi lapho. Imisebenzi yaphezulu eyimfihlo (leelas (leelas)) izokwenzeka eKhandagiri. Izazi nezindela eziningi zenza ukuzila lapho. UMfula (River) ofihliwe (Gupta Ganga (Gupta Ganga)) ugeza lapho, ozodala incithakalo eBhubaneswar ngesikhathi somsebenzi wokubhujiswa kukaKalki. Ubukhazikhazi bale Bhubaneswar nabo bukhulu kakhulu. Lapho, uMama uBarabhuji noMama (Mother) uBhuasuni bakhona, abazokwenza umsebenzi wokubhujiswa ekupheleni kweKali Yuga.

Ku-shloka engenhla, uMahapurush wachaza ukuthi iKhandagiri itholakala eBhubaneswar.

Echaza indawo yeKhandagiri, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das naye wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ,
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପକୁ ଧ୍ୟାନ କରନ୍ତି।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହିଯାଏ ଗୁପ୍ତର,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ହୁଏ ସତ୍ୱର।
ଘଟଣା ସ୍ଥାନ ଯେ ଅଟଇ ସେହି,
କଳିର ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ସେଠାହିଁ।
ଉଦୟଗିରି ଯେ ଅପୋଡା ସ୍ଥାନ,
ସେଠାବରେ ବହୁ ସାଧୁ ଆଶ୍ରମ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhubaneśwarare lāgiba goḷa, ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sādhu achhanti, Kaḷkī rūpaku dhyāna karanti.
Kṣhīra dhāra bahijāe guptara, gumphāre bhajana hue satwara.
Ghaṭaṇā sthāna je aṭai sehi, Kaḷira bhārata yuddha seṭhāhiñ.
Udayagiri je apoḍā sthāna, seṭhābare bahu sādhu āśrama.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kuzoba nempi eBhubaneswar, futhi ihlathi lase-Ekamra likhona lapho. Khona-ke uMahapurush wathi iKhandagiri nayo ikhona lapho, lapho izazi nabangcwele abaningi abaphelele bezohlala khona futhi bahlabelele njalo igama likaKalki futhi bazindle ngesimo Sakhe. Kulezo zindawo ezingaphansi komhlaba zaseKhandagiri, ama-bhajan nama-kirtan azoba khona ubusuku nemini. Lapho, uMfula ofihliwe (Kshira Nadi (Kshira Nadi)) nawo uzogeleza. Le Khandagiri izoba "Indawo Yesigameko" (Ghatana Sthana (Ghatana Sthana)), lapho kuzokwenzeka khona impi esele yeMahabharata. Eduze kukhona i-Udayagiri, lapho kukhona khona ama-ashram abangcwele abaningi abaphelele.

Ngalesi shloka esingenhla, uMahapurush wachaza ngokucacile ukuthi iKhandagiri lapho abangcwele abaningi bezohlala khona, lapho ukuhlabelela okuyimfihlo kwegama likaKalki kuzoqhubeka khona emihumeni, nalapho kuzokwenzeka khona impi esele yeMahabharata, itholakala eBhubaneswar, lapho kukhona khona nehlathi lase-Ekamra.

Lapha ngingathanda ukudonsela ukunaka kwabafundi eqinisweni elibalulekile. Emibhalweni yeMalika, iBhubaneswar ivame ukubizwa ngokuthi i-Ekamra Kshetra, i-Ekamra Van, noma i-Ekamra Kanan. Eqinisweni, ngokusho kwe-Ekamra Purana yekhulu le-13, kwakukhona isihlahla somango esisodwa kuphela eduze nendawo kankulunkulu olawulayo uLingaraj. Ngakho-ke, indawo yaseLingaraj nayo ibizwa ngokuthi i-Ekamra Kshetra (indawo enesihlahla somango esisodwa).

Lesi yisona sizathu esenza ukuthi oMahapurushas izikhathi eziningi babize iBhubaneswar ngokuthi iLingaraj Kshetra (indawo yethempeli laseLingaraj (Lingaraj)) noma i-Ekamra Van Kshetra (indawo yesihlahla somango esisodwa).

Akukhona lokhu kuphela, ngenxa yendawo yeBindu Sarovar eBhubaneswar, amaPanchasakhas emibhalweni yabo yeMalika nawo abhekise iBhubaneswar njengesifunda saseBindu Sarovar.

UMahapurush wabhala echaza ku-'Garuda Gita' –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ଏ ସବୁ ମୋ ଗୁପ୍ତ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଆଉ ପୃଥ୍ବୀରେ ଯେତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନ ପୁରାଣେ ଅଛି ବର୍ଣ୍ଣନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhubaneśwara je ekāmra kānana e sabu mo gupta sthāna,
āu pruthwīre jete siddha sthāna purāṇe achhi barṇana.

Incazelo:
INkosi Krishna itshela umzinikele wayo, uGaruda, ukuthi i-Ekamra Kanan esiseBhubaneswar iyindawo eyimfihlo neSiddha.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika 'Chhayalisha Patala' –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଏକାମ୍ରରେ,
ରହିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ତିନିଘରେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhubaneśwara Ekāmrare, rahibe bhakta tini ghare.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, abazinikele beNkosi bazohlala esifundeni saseBhubaneswar Ekamra.

UMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika 'Chumbaka Malika' –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ସୀମା ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ,
ପଞ୍ଚବିଂଶ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବେ ତଟରେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhubaneśwara sīmā Khaṇḍagirire, pañchabiṃśha bhakta thibe taṭare.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iKhandagiri isemngceleni weBhubaneswar lapho abazinikele abangama-25 bezobamba iqhaza emisebenzini yaphezulu.

UMahapurush ubuye abhale ku-'Chakada Madana' –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଗୋଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାର,
ଭାବି କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ନିଜ ନାମ ସ୍ମର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhubaneśwarare goḷa Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāra,
bhābi kahai Achyutī nija nāma smara.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kuzoba nempi endaweni yaseKhandagiri eBhubaneswar.

Omunye wama (Panchasakhas), u (Mahapurush Yasovanta Das), ubhala embhalweni wakhe we (Malika) –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଠାରେ ଜାଣ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସେ ସରୋବର ପୁଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhubaneśwara ṭhāre jāṇa,
Bindu se sarobara puṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, yazi ukuthi i (Bindu Sarovar) itholakala e (Bhubaneswar).

U ubhala embhalweni wakhe we ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜ ସ୍ଥଳୀ,
ଦୁଇ ପୁରୁଷ ଏକଜଣ ସ୍ତିରୀ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhubaneśwara Liṅgarāja sthaḷī,
dui puruṣha ekajaṇa stirī.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, i iyisihlalo se, okusho ukuthi ithempeli le litholakala e.

Uma sisebenzisa usizo lwe Google, ngisho nalapho singakwazi kalula ukwazi ukuthi noma ngabe i (Khandagiri) noma i (Ekamra Kanan) noma i noma ithempeli le, konke kutholakala e uqobo.

I, okuyoba yindawo yokuhlala ye (Nkosi), lapho abangcwele abaningi beyobe behlabelela igama lika—ukuchaza ukuthi le itholakala e (Ekamra Kshetra), u (Mahapurush Achyutananda Das) wabhala futhi ku ‘Chakada Madana’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଦଦେଶେ ଭରତ ପାଦୁକା,
ଛଦ୍ମବେଶୀ ସାଧୁ ପୂଜା କରୁଅଛି ଏକା।
ଗୁପ୍ତଗଙ୍ଗା ବହିଅଛି ଗୁପ୍ତ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଗୁପ୍ତ ବେଶେ ରହିଛନ୍ତି ପୁରି।
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଅଟଇ ଶିବ ଯୋଗ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶୁକପକ୍ଷୀ ପାଇଲା ଯେଉଁଠି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଜ୍ଞାନ।
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଦେବୀ ଅଛି ଜାଣିଥା ସତ୍ୱର,
ସେ ଦେବୀ ଘାତରେ ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ କମ୍ପମାନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Khaṇḍagiri pādadeśe Bharata pādukā, chhadmabeśī sādhu pūjā karuachhi ekā.
Guptagaṅgā bahiachhi gupta Khaṇḍagiri, siddha sādhu gupta beśe rahichhanti puri.
Ekāmra bana aṭai Śiba yoga sthāna, śukapakṣhī pāilā jeuṇṭhi brahmajñāna.
Bhūāsuṇୀ debī achhi jāṇithā satwara, se debī ghātare pruthibī heba kampamāna.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ezansi kwezintaba ze kukhona i (Bharata Paduka), ekhonzwa umngcwele ofihliwe. Kuleyo efanayo, umfula i ugeleza ngasese. Lapho (e Khandagiri), abangcwele abaningi abaphelele bahlala befihliwe. Ihlathi le (Ekamra) litholakala lapho, okuyindawo ye (Nkosi Shiva) ye (Yoga). Lapho, inyoni i (Shuka) yake yathola ulwazi lwe (Brahma-knowledge). Eduze kukhona uNkulunkulukazi u (Bhuasuni), ozokwenza umsebenzi wokubhujiswa eMhlabeni ekupheleni kwe (Kali Yuga).

Ngingathanda ukutshela abafundi lapha ukuthi kwelinye lamavesi angenhla, u wathi i itholakala endaweni yehlathi le futhi abangcwele abaningi benza ukuzila ukudla e. Umfula nawo ugeleza lapho ngasese. Kuleli vesi lamanje, u (Mahapurush) uphinda into efanayo.

U wabhala futhi ku ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ yakhe –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ଲୀଳାମୟଙ୍କର ସତ୍ୟ ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ,
ଲୀଳା କରୁଥିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମାଧବ ସର୍ବେ ଆନନ୍ଦ ହୋଇଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Līḷā prakāśiba līḷāmayankara satya je Ekāmra bana,
līḷā karuthibe Ananta Mādhaba sarbe ānanda hoiṇa.

Incazelo:
Lapha u waphinda wathi indawo yokuhlala ye yi. Kodwa akazange athi i noma i. U wabiza leyo ndawo efanayo ngokuthi ‘Ekamra Van’. U wabhala ukuthi imisebenzi yaphezulu ye (Leelamaya) izobonakala isuka endaweni ye. Kuleyo ndawo efanayo ye (Ekamra Van), i (Nkosi Ananta Madhava), okungukuthi, u, izokwenza imisebenzi yaphezulu, ukubona nokuzwa lokho injabulo yabazinikele ngeke yazi mingcele.

U wabhala futhi ku ‘Aagata Bhavishyata Malika’ yakhe –

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳା ଧଳା ଅଶ୍ୱ ଉପରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ରହିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନରେ ଯେତେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ଋଷି,
ସକଳେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ନାମକୁ ଘୋଷି।
ସକଳେ ସେବେଳେ ଠୁଳ ଯେ ହେବେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗର,
କପିଳେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଭେଟିଣ ହୋଇବେ ସୂପକାର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷā dhaḷā ashwa upare prabhu hebe bāhāra,
Bhubaneshwarare rahibe prabhu raṅgā adhara.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sthānare jete achhanti ruṣi,
sakaḷe bāhāra hoibe prabhu nāmaku ghoṣi.
Sakaḷe sebeḷe ṭhuḷa je hebe Bindu Sāgara,
Kapileshwarare bheṭiṇa hoibe sūpakāra.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, i izosuka iyobhubhisa ngehhashi elithi “Kala-Dhala” noma elimnyama nelimhlophe. Ngaphambi kwalokho, i izohlala e. Bonke abahlakaniphileyo nezindela abenza ukuzila ukudla endaweni ephelele ye bazophuma emihumeni ngaleso sikhathi, bememezela igama le, futhi bazoqoqana e etholakala e. Khona-ke, abapheki bazohlangana nabo bonke ethempelini eliseduze le (Kapileshwar).

Kuleli vesi elingenhla futhi, i ibizwa ngokuthi indawo ephelele eyoba yindawo yokuhlala yabahlakaniphileyo nezindela abaningi. Futhi kuthiwa ukuthi noma ngabe i noma i noma iThempeli le, konke lokhu kutholakala e futhi kuseduze komunye nomunye.

Omunye wama, u (Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das), lapho ebuzwa umfundi wakhe u (Baranga Das) ukuthi i (Nkosi Kalki) izozalelwa kuphi futhi izohlala kuphi, uchaza embhalweni wakhe we ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ହେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ କହିବା ସେ ରଙ୍ଗ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଅବତାର ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିରଜା କ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ଜନମ ଲଭିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମିଶ୍ର ଗୃହେଣ।
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ଗୃହକୁ ଛାଡିବେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଯାଇଁ,
ସିଦ୍ଧଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ସିଦ୍ଧଙ୍କ ସଦନ ରହିବେ ସେ ଭାବଗ୍ରାହୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ସୁବର୍ଣ୍ଣଚ୍ୟୁତ ଗିରିରେ,
ସେହି ସ୍ଥାନେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରିଥିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ଜନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ଅନନ୍ତ ବାସୁଦେବ ବିପ୍ରଲିଙ୍ଗ ଭେଟ ସେ ଯହିଁ ଆଗରେ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ବିଧି ସାରିଣ ସିଦ୍ଧ କେଦାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ସାରିବେ ସମସ୍ତ ଭକତ ମିଳି,
ନିଜ କଳା ଭକ୍ତି କଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବେ ଛାଡିବେ କଳିର ମଳି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Shuṇa he bāraṅga kahibā se raṅga prabhu abatāra sthāna,
Shrī Birajā kṣetre janama labhibe Ananta Mishra gruheṇa.
Janama hoibe gruhaku chhāḍibe tapasyā karibe jāiñ,
Siddhagiri sthāna siddhaṅka sadana rahibe se bhābagrāhī.
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna Ekāmra kānana Subarṇachyuta girire,
sehi sthāne prabhu bije karithibe bhakta jana saṅgatare.
Shrī Ananta Bāsudeba bipraliṅga bheṭa se jahiñ āgare,
Bindusāgare snāna bidhi sāriṇa siddha Kedāra madhyare.
Shrī Bindu Sāgare snāna sāribe samasta bhakata miḷi,
nija kaḷā bhakti kaḷā prakāshibe chhāḍibe kaḷira maḷi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, i izozalelwa endlini yomuntu ozinikele ogama lakhe lingu (Ananta Mishra) e (Shri Viraja Kshetra). Ngemuva kokuzalwa, i izoshiya ikhaya Layo iye endaweni yabangcwele abaphelele ukuyokwenza ukuzila ukudla. U akazange agcine nje ngokuthi "indawo ephelele," waphinde wathi igama laleyo ndawo lizoba yi, eyayizotholakala e. Kuleyo ndawo efanayo ye ye, i izohlala nabazinikele Bayo. U wathi ithempeli le (Shri Ananta Vasudev) lizoba lapho, kanye ne (Linga), okungukuthi, ithempeli le lizoba lapho. I izogeza e (Bindu Sagar) futhi izovakashela ithempeli le (Siddha Kedara) (Kedareshwar).

U waphinde wathi bonke abazinikele bazogeza kuleyo efanayo, ngemuva kwalokho ukukhanya kwaphezulu kuzobonakala kubazinikele, futhi bonke bazokhululeka ekungcoleni kwe.

Ngaleli vesi elingenhla, u (Sishu Ananta Dasji) wachaza ukuthi lapho kukhona ithempeli le, lapho kukhona ithempeli le, lapho kukhona u, kuleyo efanayo (indawo ye Ekamra Van), i itholakala. Le iyindawo yabangcwele abaphelele, futhi i izohlala lapha.

Ukuzalwa kabusha kuka (Achyutananda Dasji), uMngcwele u (Hadi Das), wabhala embhalweni wakhe we ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦେଇ ଶୁଣ ସୁଜନେ କଳିଯୁଗ ବିଚାର,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ଜନମ ହେବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ଚକ୍ରଧର।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହି ଯାଉଛି ସ୍ଥାନ ଅମରାବତୀ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଗୁପତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି।
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ଲାଗିଛି ରାତ୍ର ଦିନ ମଧ୍ୟରେ,
ଗୁପତେ ଖେଳ ସେ ଖେଳିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେହି ଠାବରେ।
ଝାଡଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ସେ ଠାବ,
ଝଲମଲ ଜ୍ୟୋତି ବାହାରେ ଲୀଳା ଚହଟ ହେବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Karṇa dei shuṇa sujane kaḷiyuga bichāra,
Kaḷkī rūpare janama hebe Shrī Chakradhara.
Kṣīra dhāra bahi jāuchhi sthāna Amarābatī,
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna gupate siddha sādhu achhanti.
Gumphāre bhajana lāgichhi rātra dina madhyare,
gupate kheḷa se kheḷibe prabhu sehi ṭhābare.
Jhāḍakhaṇḍagiri gumphā madhyare prabhuṅkara se ṭhāba,
jhalamala jyoti bāhāre līḷā chahaṭa heba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kwe, i (Nkosi Maha Vishnu) izozalelwa eMhlabeni ngokuzalwa kabusha kuka. Imisebenzi Yakhe yaphezulu izoqala ukwenzeka endaweni yomfula i (Kshira) (Vaitarani), e (Amaravati), okungukuthi, e. Khona-ke imisebenzi Yakhe izokwenzeka e lapho abangcwele abaphelele beyobe bematasa nge (bhajan-kirtan) imini nobusuku emihumeni ngasese. U wathi i izohlala futhi yenze imisebenzi Yakhe kuleyo ndawo efanayo ye noma i (Jhadakhandagiri).

UMngcwele u wabhala embhalweni wakhe we ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଘଟଣା ପ୍ରତିମା ପୁରୁଷ ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜଙ୍କ ପୁର,
ଘୋର କଳି ମହାଭାରତ ହେବ ସେହି ଠାବର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ghaṭaṇā pratimā puruṣha Liṅgarājaṅka pura,
ghora kaḷi mahābhārata heba sehi ṭhābara.

Incazelo:
U ubhala ukuthi "Indawo Yesigameko" izoba yindawo ye noma i, futhi impi esele ye izokwenzeka lapho. Kuyaziwa ukuthi ngaphambilini sabona ivesi lapho u abhala khona – “Ghatana sthana je atai sehi, kalira bharata yuddha sethahin”. U wabhala leli vesi mayelana ne embhalweni wakhe we ‘Chakada Madana’ futhi wathi i iyi “Ghatana Sthana,” lapho impi esele ye (Mahabharata) izokwenzeka khona.

Kamuva, u wabhala – I noma indawo ye iyi “Ghatana Sthana” futhi impi ye (Kali Mahabharata) izokwenzeka lapho.

Lokhu kusho ukuthi lokho u (Achyutananda Das) akusho nge ku ‘Chakada Madana’ yilokho kanye ukuzalwa kwakhe kabusha, u, akusho ngendawo ye ku ‘Kali Chautisha’. Lokhu kufakazela ukuthi zombili izindawo, okungukuthi, i ne, zikhomba endaweni efanayo. Ngamafuphi, i ise.

Qaphela – Kuze kube manje emavesini amaningi angenhla, sifundile ukuthi i e iyindawo yabangcwele abaphelele nabangcwele. Labo bangcwele bamatasa nge ye imini nobusuku emihumeni ye. Manje sizofunda ukuthi yibaphi abahlakaniphileyo nezindela abazobe benza ukuzila ukudla kwe kuleyo nokuthi bangaki abahlakaniphileyo sebebonke abazobe benza i emihumeni.

U ubhala ku ‘Aagata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika’ yakhe –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିକି ଯେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର କେତେକ ଦୂର,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ସାଧୁସନ୍ଥ ନିକର।
ନାରଦ ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଶମ୍ଭୁ ଯେ ଅଗସ୍ତି ଉପଲସ୍ତି,
ଦୁର୍ବାସା କଶ୍ୟପ ଚ୍ୟବନ କୁମ୍ଭଋଷି ସନ୍ତତି।
ପରାଶର ବଶିଷ୍ଠାଦି ହୋ ବିଶ୍ୱାମିତ୍ରଙ୍କୁ ଘେନି,
ଷାଠିଏ ସହସ୍ର ଋଷି ହୋ ଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପୁଣି।
ଦେବଋଷି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଋଷି ଯେ ସର୍ବେ ମେଳି ହୋଇଣ,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ କରନ୍ତି କଳ୍କୀ ନାମ ପୁଣ।
ଦେବତାମାନେ ଯେ ସେଠାରେ ରାତ୍ରେ ଗୋଷ୍ଠୀ କରନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀର ଆଦ୍ୟ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ନିତ୍ୟେ ଜପୁଛନ୍ତି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Khaṇḍagiriki je Bhubaneshwara keteka dūra,
Khaṇḍagiri madhye achhanti sādhusantha nikara.
Nārada Mārkaṇḍa Shambhu je Agasti Upalasti,
Durbāsā Kashyapa Chyabana Kumbharuṣi santati.
Parāshara Bashiṣṭhādi ho Bishwāmitraṅku gheni,
ṣāṭhie sahasra ruṣi ho giri madhyare puṇi.
Debaruṣi Brahmaruṣi je sarbe meḷi hoiṇa,
gumphāre bhajana karanti Kaḷkī nāma puṇa.
Debatāmāne je seṭhāre rātre goṣṭhī karanti,
Kaḷkīra ādya mantraku nitye japuchhanti.

Incazelo:
U ucaphune ingxoxo ka (Krishna-Garuda) embhalweni wakhe we. U (Garuda) uthi indawo ye ayikude ne (iseduze kakhulu). Kunabangcwele abaningi e. Lapho, u (Narada), u (Markandeya), u (Shambhu), u (Agastya), u (Upalasti), u (Durvasa), u (Kashyapa), u (Chyavana), “Kumbharushi Santati” okusho u (Dridhasyu), u (Parashara), u (Vashishtha), u (Vishwamitra), njll., bebonke abahlakaniphileyo abayishumi nambili futhi kubandakanya nabo sebebonke abahlakaniphileyo abangu-60,000, ama (Devarishis), nama (Brahmarishis) bamatasa nge (name-bhajan) yegama le emihumeni. Onkulunkulu babamba imihlangano kuleyo mihume ebusuku futhi nsuku zonke bahlabelela i (mantra) ye.

Kuleli vesi, u (Garudaji) akazange nje asho ngabahlakaniphileyo abenza ukuzila ukudla emihumeni ye kodwa waphinde wathi umhume we ngeke ube kude ne, noma kunalokho, uzotholakala eduze kakhulu.

Emavesini angu-15 angenhla, kumelwe ukuba sekucacile kubafundi ukuthi ngokwe (Bhavishya Malika), i—lapho u ezohlala khona, lapho abangcwele abaphelele abangenakubalwa beyobe behlala khona, lapho i yegama lika izoqhubeka khona ngokuqhubekayo emihumeni, lapho umfula uzogeleza khona ngasese, lapho impi esele ye izokwenzeka khona—itholakala kuphela e noma endaweni ye noma endaweni ye, futhi akukho kwenye indawo.

10 Inhloso Yokuzalwa kabusha kweNkosi Kalki

Njengoba sonke sazi, i ithatha ukuzalwa kabusha okuyishumi kuloMhlaba ukuze isungule ukulunga. Noma ngabe i (Satya Yuga), i (Treta Yuga), noma i (Dvapara Yuga), inhloso yokuzalwa kabusha kwe (Nkosi) eMhlabeni ihlala ifana—ukusungulwa kwe (Dharma).

Ku (Shrimad Bhagavad Gita), i (Nkosi Krishna) yathi ku (Arjuna) –

ISanskrit Sloka:

परित्राणाय साधूनां विनाशाय च दुष्कृताम् ।
धर्मसंस्थापनार्थाय संभवामि युगे युगे ।।

Incazelo:
Ukuze kuvikelwe abalungileyo (abazinikele), ukuze kubhujiswe ababi, nokuze kusungulwe ngokuqinile ukulunga, ngizibonakalisa mina isikhathi ngesikhathi.

Emibhalweni ye (Bhavishya Malika) futhi, kushiwo ukuthi ukuze kubhujiswe ababi, kuvikelwe abazinikele nabangcwele, futhi kusungulwe kabusha i, i (Nkosi Maha Vishnu) izokwehla eMhlabeni ngokuzalwa Kabusha Kwayo kweshumi nokokugcina, u.

Ingxoxo phakathi kwe nomngane u e yacashunwa eminyakeni engaba ngu-600 edlule e (Kali Yuga) ngu (Mahapurush Achyutananda Das) embhalweni wakhe we (Malika) ‘Chausathi Patala’. U (Mahapurush) wabhala –

I-Odia Sloka:

ପାପ ଭାରା ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ସହି ନପାରି,
ମୋ ଆଗରେ କଲା ବେଗେ ଗୁହାରି।
କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ମୁହିଁ ହୋଇବି,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ବିନାଶିବି।
ବସି ନରପତି ହୃଦ ଦେଶରେ
ପ୍ରଜା ପାଳିବି ଉତ୍ତମ ଭାବରେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Pāpa bhārā pṛthwī sahi napāri,
mo āgare kalā bege guhāri.
Kaḷkī abatāra muhiñ hoibi,
duṣṭa prāṇīmānaṅku bināshibi.
Basi narapati hṛda deshare,
prajā pāḷibi uttama bhābare.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, uMhlaba, ungakwazi ukuthwala umthwalo wezono ekupheleni kwe, uzoncenga phambi Kwami. Khona-ke ngizothatha ukuzalwa kabusha kuka futhi ngizobhubhisa ababi futhi ngikhulule abazinikele. Ngihlala e (India), ngizophatha ukubusa komhlaba wonke futhi ngizobusa izakhamuzi ngendlela efanele neqotho.

U ubhala komunye umbhalo we –

I-Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭୟଙ୍କର ଉତ୍ପାତ ହେବ ଧରିତ୍ରୀ ବକ୍ଷରେ ଜାଣ,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାରି ଭାରା ଉଶ୍ୱାସିବେ ପୁଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mahābhayaṅkara utpāta heba dharitrī bakṣhare jāṇa,
Kaḷkī rūpare mlechha saṃhāri bhārā uśwāsibe puṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kuzoba nesiphithiphithi esesabekayo eMhlabeni. U uqobo uzobhubhisa ababi futhi akhulule uMhlaba emthwalweni wesono.

Ecaphuna ingxoxo ka (Krishna-Arjuna), u ubhala futhi embhalweni wakhe we –

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳେ ମୁହିଁ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହେବି,
ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିଣ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନମାନଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷi śeṣha beḷe muñhi Kaḷkī rūpa hebi,
santhaṅku rakhiṇa duṣhṭajanamānanku nāśhibi.

Incazelo:
U (Shri Krishna) uthi ekupheleni kwe, ngizothatha ukuzalwa kabusha kuka. Ngivikela abangcwele, abantu abangcwele, nabantu abanenhliziyo ehlanzekile, ngizobhubhisa bonke ababi nezoni.

Ecaphuna amazwi e futhi, u ubhala embhalweni wakhe we ‘Chausathi Patala’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ବେଶୀ ପାପ ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପେ ମୁଁ ଉଦୟ ହେବି,
ଓଡିଶା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ମନ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଯାଇ କରିବି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Beśī pāpa jetebeḷe heba Kaḷkī rūpe mu udaya hebi,
Oḍiśā madhyare sthāne sthāne mana parīkṣhā jāi karibi.

Incazelo:
INkosi ithi eKali Yuga, lapho isono sifinyelela umvuthwandaba, ngizovela ngesimo sami sikaKalki. Ngizohlola abazinikele ezindaweni ezahlukahlukene e-Odisha, nginqume phakathi kokulungile nokungalungile, bese ngenza ngokufanele.

Ecaphuna amazwi eNkosi, omunye wamaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Balaram Das, wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi 'Kali Aagata Bhavishyanta' –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଯେ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ମାତିବେ,
ଖପର କରେ ଘେନିଥିବେ।
ରୋଧିର ବୃଷ୍ଟି ଯେ ହୋଇବ,
କେ ଅବା କାହିଁ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ଯାହାକୁ ରଖିବିଟି ଟିକିଏ ମୁହିଁ,
ସେ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନେ ଥିବେ ରହି।
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଲୋକ ଯେ ନାଶ ହେବେ,
ସନ୍ଥ ଯେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ ରହିବେ।
ଅନେକ ଉତ୍ପାତ ଲାଗିବ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଚହଳ ପଡିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chaṇḍī je Chāmuṇḍā mātibe,
khapara kare ghenithibe.
Rodhira bṛṣṭi je hoiba,
ke abā kāhiñ nāsha jiba.
Jāhāku rakhibiṭi ṭikie muhiñ,
se prāṇīmāne thibe rahi.
Duṣṭa loka je nāsha hebe,
santha je abashya rahibe.
Aneka utpāta lāgiba,
shūnyare chahaḷa paḍiba.

Incazelo:
INkosi ithi ngesikhathi sokusungulwa kweDharma ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, uMama u-Ashta Chandis namanye amaYoginis bazobhubhisa kakhulu. Kuzona imvula yegazi esibhakabhakeni. Kuzoba nezinkinga eziningi futhi izinhlekelele zizovela esikhaleni, okungukuthi, emkhathini. Kuphela labo engibavikela bona abazosinda. Ababi bazobhujiswa, futhi abangcwele nabantu abangcwele bazokhululwa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi 'Kali Kalpa Gita' –

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣhatrīmānanku nikṣhatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, iNkosi izothatha isimo sayo sikaKalki bese ihamba umhlaba wonke futhi izokhulula uMama uMhlaba ngokubhubhisa ababi.

Ku-'Kali Kalpa Gita' uqobo, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala futhi –

I-Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନିଧନ ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ସଂହାରଣ,
କରିବେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଦେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ମହୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
କଳିକଳ୍ପ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mlechha nidhana duṣhṭa saṃharaṇa,
karibe niśhche deba Kaḷkī Rāma.
Emanta mahī je heba uśwāsa,
Kaḷikalpa kahe Achyuta dāsa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi uKalki izowukhulula ngokuqinisekile uMhlaba ngokubhubhisa amaMlechhas (abantu abangamahlaya/ababi) nangokuqeda ababi.

10.1 Ukuza KweNkosi uKalki

Omunye wamaPanchasakhas, uMphefumulo Omkhulu uSishu Ananta Das, wabhala ngalesi sihloko embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi 'Chumbaka Malika':

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଆଶ୍ୱାସି ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷkī abatāra Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśhi bhaktaṅku āśwāsi dharmaku tumbhe pāliba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi Enkulu uKalki izozalelwa e-Odisha. Ngemuva kwalokho, iNkosi izobhubhisa ababi futhi ivikele abazinikele ukuze iphinde isungule iDharma.

11 Izenzo Eziyi-11 Zokubhujiswa: Isiqalo Nesiphetho Esicacile?

Njengoba sazi ezinkomba zeBhabishya Malika, kulesi sikhathi soguquko phakathi kweKali Yuga neSatya Yuga, kuzokwenzeka izigameko zokubhujiswa ezingakaze zibonwe emhlabeni wonke ngaphansi kobuholi beNkosi uKalki, lapho wonke amaMlecchas (abantu abangamahlaya), izoni, ababi, nabangalungile bazobhujiswa. IBhabishya Malika iphinde inikeze isiqinisekiso sokuthi bonke abazinikele bazohlala bephephile ngesikhathi salezi zigameko futhi akudingeki besabe.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Chhayalisha Patala’, ucaphuna amazwi eNkosi Enkulu lapho iNkosi inikeza khona imininingwane yokubhujiswa okuyingxenye (Khanda Pralaya) okuzayo.

UMahapurush ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

କାଳଭାବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟରେ,
ସୃଷ୍ଟି ନାଶ କରି ଲୀଳା କରେ ପାପ ଭାରେ।
ମାତ୍ର ମହାପ୍ରଳୟକୁ ରହିଥିବୁ ଟାକି,
ସେ କାଳରେ ଚରାଚର ନ ରହିବେ ବାକି।
ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ସହିତ ସଂହାରିଣୀ ଶକ୍ତି,
ଖଣ୍ଡା ଖପର ହାତରେ ବୁଲି ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି।
ରୋଗ ଶୋକ ଦୁଃଖ ଜାଲ ସଂସାରେ ପାତିବେ,
ପର ଆପଣାର ଭେଦ କିଛି ନ ରଖିବେ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ପବନ ଯେ ରୁଦ୍ର ମୂର୍ତ୍ତି ଧରି,
ଉପାଡ଼ିବ ସୁମେରୁ ସହିତ ଯେତେ ଗିରି।
ଚାରିମେଘ ଚାରିଦିଗେ ଆଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ କରିବେ,
ମହାଘୋର ବିପତ୍ତିକୁ ଆଣି ଘଟାଇବେ।
ଅନ୍ଧାର ହୋଇବ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ବଢ଼ିବ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ନକ୍ଷତ୍ର ଉଲକା ଛିଣ୍ଡିବ ନାହିଁ ଆଦି ଅନ୍ତ।
ଭୈରବୀ ରଡ଼ିବେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ କାନ୍ଦିବ ସକଳ,
ପାପମାତ୍ରା ପ୍ରବଳରେ ହେବ ଏହି ଫଳ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kālabhābe mahāshakti khaṇḍa praḷayare,
Sṛṣṭi nāsha kari līḷā kare pāpa bhāre.
Mātra mahāpraḷayaku rahithibu ṭāki,
Se kāḷare charāchara na rahibe bāki.
Chaṇḍī Chāmuṇḍā sahita saṃhāriṇī shakti,
Khaṇḍā khapara hātare buli dibārātri.
Roga shoka duḥkha jāla saṃsāre pātibe,
Para āpaṇāra bheda kichhi na rakhibe.
Aṇachāsha pabana je rudra mūrtti dhari,
Upāḍiba Sumeru sahita jete giri.
Chārimegha chāridige āchhanna karibe,
Mahāghora bipattiku āṇi ghaṭāibe.
Andhāra hoiba pṛthwī baḍhiba utpāta,
Nakṣatra ulakā chhiṇḍiba nāhiñ ādi anta.
Bhairabī raḍibe pṛthwī kāndiba sakaḷa,
Pāpamātrā prabaḷare heba ehi phaḷa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, Amandla Aphakeme ngesimo Sesikhathi (Kala) azoletha ukubhujiswa okuyingxenye futhi abhubhise umhlaba. Ngesikhathi sokubhujiswa, zonke izidalwa eziphilayo zizothinteka. UChandi-Chamunda namandla okubhujiswa bazozulazula imini nobusuku bephethe izinkemba (Khanda) nezikhumba (Khapar). Ubuhlungu, ukuhlupheka, izifo, nosizi kuzosakazeka emhlabeni wonke. Zonke izinhlukaniso phakathi 'kokwakho' naba 'ngabafokazi' zizonyamalala. Imimoya engamashumi amane nesishiyagalolunye, ithatha isimo esinolaka sikaRudra (Rudra), izosiphula zonke izintaba kuhlanganise neSumeru enkulu. Amafu amane, okungukuthi u-Avarta, uSamvarta, uPushkara, noDrona, azungeze izinhlangothi ezine futhi adale izinhlekelele ezimbi. Ubumnyama obukhulu buzogubuzela uMhlaba; ilanga nenyanga ngeke kuphume. Ngaleso sikhathi, izinkanyezi ezishisayo zizowa esibhakabhakeni futhi zibe imbangela yokubhujiswa. Kulobo bumnyama, uMama uBhairavi uzobhonga kakhulu futhi abhubhise izoni ezingenakubalwa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, ecaphuna amazwi eNkosi, ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

କାହିଁ ଜଳ ରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ଜଗତ ନାଶିବି,
କାହିଁ ପବନ ରୂପରେ ସକଳ ଗ୍ରାସିବି।
କାହିଁ ଯୋଗମାୟା ନେଇ ସଂହାର କରିବି,
କାହିଁ ନିଜେ ବିଶ୍ଵରୂପ ଧାରଣ କରିବି।
କାହିଁ ମଉନ ବ୍ରତ ମୁଁ ଧାରଣ କରିବି,
କାହିଁ କାଳରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ପାପୀଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kāhiñ jala rūpe muhiñ jagata nāshibi,
kāhiñ pabana rūpare sakaḷa grāsibi.
kāhiñ yogamāyā nei saṃhāra karibi,
kāhiñ nije bishwarūpa dhāraṇa karibi.
kāhiñ mauna brata muñ dhāraṇa karibi,
kāhiñ kāḷarūpe muhiñ pāpīṅku nāshibi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi uKalki izoletha ukubhujiswa okukhulu kulo mhlaba ngezimo zayo ezahlukahlukene. Kwezinye izindawo, izothatha isimo samanzi futhi icwilise yonke into; kwezinye, izophephetha yonke into ngesikhukhula somoya. Kwezinye izimo, izoletha ukubhujiswa ngenkathi igcina isifungo sokuthula, nakwezinye, izobulala ababi ngesimo Sesikhathi. Kwezinye izindawo, izothatha isimo sayo Somhlaba Wonke, nakwezinye, izoletha ukubhujiswa ngamandla ayo eYogamaya.

Ngokusho kweBhabishya Malika, iNkosi uKalki izoletha lokhu kubhujiswa ngezikhukhula zamanzi ezinolaka, ukuzamazama komhlaba okukhulu, iziphepho zomoya ezibhubhisayo, izikhukhula zomlilo, izinhlekelele zasemkhathini, izifo eziyingozi, nempi yomhlaba yenuzi.

11.1 Ukuzamazama Komhlaba

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ହିମାଳୟ ଏଭରେଷ୍ଟ ଟଳି ଯେ ପଡିବ,
ଯେବେ ଏହି ଧରା ପରେ ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Himālaya Everest ṭaḷi je paḍiba,
Jebe ehi dharā pare bhūmikampa heba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngesikhathi sezenzo zokubhujiswa zikaKalki (Kalki) ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, kuzoba nokuzamazama komhlaba okunamandla kangangokuthi ngisho namaHimalayas nezintaba zawo ezifana ne-Everest zizochithakala. Abazinikele bazohlala bephephile, kanti izoni zizobhujiswa.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das uphinde abhale embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପେ ପ୍ରକମ୍ପିତ ହୋଇବ ଧରଣୀ,
ମାଟି ତଳେ ଲୁଚିଯିବ କୋଠାବାଡି ପୁଣି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhūmikampe prakampita hoiba dharaṇī,
māṭi taḷe luchijiba koṭhābāḍi puṇi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, wonke uMhlaba uzothuthumela ngokuzamazama komhlaba, futhi ngenxa yalokho kuzamazama komhlaba, izakhiwo ezinkulu zizongcwatshwa ngaphansi komhlabathi.

UMahapurush wabhala komunye wemibhalo yakhe yeMalika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ପାତାଳେ ବାସୁକୀ ଟେକିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ,
ତିନିଥର ଯେ କମ୍ପିବ ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Pātāḷe Bāsukī ṭekiba muṇḍa,
tini thara je kampiba brahmāṇḍa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngenxa yomthwalo omkhulu wezenzo zesono ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, inyoka uVasuki, ephethe uMhlaba ekhanda layo, izonyakazisa ikhanda layo kaningi. Ngenxa yalokhu, kuzokwenzeka ukuzamazama komhlaba okukhulu okuthathu kulo Mhlaba.

Umgcwele omkhulu owazalelwa e-Odisha ngekhulu lama-20, uThakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ ଅପାର,
ନାଶ ହୋଇବେ ନାରୀ ନର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhūmikampa heba apāra,
nāśha hoibe nārī nara.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, esigabeni sokugcina seKali Yuga, kuzoba nokuzamazama komhlaba okungenakubalwa lapho amadoda nabesifazane abaningi bezobhubha khona.

11.2 Isikhukhula Samanzi (Jala Pralaya)

Ngenkathi exoxa ngezinto ezimbi zesikhukhula samanzi, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତକୁ ଛାଡି ଦେଲେ ସବୁ ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଯିବ, ମହାସାଗର ଜଳରେ ଡୁବି ମରୁଥିବ।
ଧରଣୀ ଦେହରେ ଜଳ ମାରିବ ଲହରୀ, ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇଯିବ ଚଉଦିଗ ଭାରି।
ଅଛଇ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ଉତ୍ତର ମେରୁରେ, ତରଳିବ ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କ ରଶ୍ମିରେ।
ରୌଦ୍ରତାପେ ଯେତେବେଳେ ମେଦିନୀ କମ୍ପିବ, ସେତିକିବେଳେ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ତରଳିବ।
ବହିବ ପାଣିର ସୁଅ ସମୁଦ୍ରକୁ ପୁଣ, ଆଟଲାଣ୍ଟିକ ବଢ଼ିବ କାହିଁ କେତେ ଜାଣ।
ସମୁଦ୍ରର ପାଣି ଧୀରେ ବଢ଼ି ବଢ଼ି ଯିବ, ସାଗର ମାତିବ ଶେଷେ ଆଉ ଯାହା ଥିବ।
ଅନାଉ ଅନାଉ କାହିଁ ଜଳ ମାଡିଯିବ, ମାଟିର ଲକ୍ଷେ ଘନ ଫୁଟ ଉପରେ ରହିବ।
ଭୂଗୋଳର ସ୍ଥିତି ବଡ଼ ବିଚିତ୍ର ତୁ ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ରହିଛି ସର୍ବ ଉର୍ଦ୍ଧ୍ଵରେ ତ ପୁଣ।
ସୃଷ୍ଟିର କର୍ତ୍ତା ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତପତି ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ଭୂମିରେ କରିବେ ବୈକୁଣ୍ଠ ସ୍ଥାପନ।
ପାଣିର ଘେରରେ ଆଉ ସବୁ ମହାଦେଶ, ଧୀରେ ଜାଣ ବୁଡିଯିବ ସେ ମହାବିନାଶ।
ଦେଖିବ ଏ ଦୁଇ ଆଖି ସମୟ ଆସିଛି, ସାମନା କରି ପାରିଲେ ସମୟକୁ ଇଛି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bhāratku chāḍidele sabu rāṣṭra jiba, Mahāsāgara jalare ḍubi maruthiba.
Dharaṇī dehare jala māriba laharī, Jalārṇaba hoijiba chaudiga bhāri.
Achai baraphagaḍa uttara merure, taraḷiba dhīre dhīre sūryāṅka raśmire.
Raudratāpe jetebeḷe medinī kampiba, setikibeḷe baraphagaḍa taraḷiba.
Bahiba pāṇira sua samudruku puṇa, Āṭlāṇṭika baḍhiba kāhiñ kete jāṇa.
Samudrara pāṇi dhīre baḍhi baḍhi jiba, sāgara mātiba śeṣe āu jāhā thiba.
Anāu anāu kāhiñ jala māḍijiba, māṭira lakṣhe ghana phuṭa upare rahiba.
Bhūgoḷara sthiti baḍa bichitra tu jāṇa, Bhārata rahichi sarba urdhware ta puṇa.
Sruṣṭira kartā prabhu jagatpati jāṇa, Bhārata bhūmire karibe Baikuṇṭha sthāpana.
Pāṇira gherare āu sabu mahādeśa, dhīre jāṇa buḍijiba se mahābināśa.
Dekhiba e dui ākhi samaya āsichi, sāmnā karipārile samayaku ichhi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngaphandle kwe-India, cishe zonke izizwe zizocwila emanzini olwandle. Kuzoba nesikhukhula samanzi eMhlabeni, futhi zonke izinhlangothi ezine zizocwila. Ngenxa yokushisa okwandayo kwelanga, amaqhwa akhona eNyakatho Pole azoqala ukuncibilika, okuzothinta kakhulu uLwandle i-Atlantic. Izinga lamanzi olwandle lizoqhubeka likhuphuka, okuzodala ukuthi amadolobha asogwini acwile. Kwezinye izindawo, amagagasi e-tsunami aphakeme njengezintaba azovela. Yonke indawo izoshintsha. Kodwa iNkosi Enkulu izovikela i-India, futhi umthelela walesi sikhukhula samanzi uzoba mncane e-India. Ngaphandle kwe-India, wonke amanye amazwekazi azoba yizisulu zesikhukhula samanzi.

11.3 Isikhukhula Somoya (Pavan Pralaya)

Omunye wamaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Patta Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମହୀନ ହେବେ ସକଳ ଏ ଜନ ଧର୍ମାଧର୍ମ ନ ରହିବ,
ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ବହିବ ଏମନ୍ତ ପୃଥିବୀ ଟଳଟଳ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dharmahīna hebe sakaḷa e jana dharmādharma na rahiba,
aṇachāśha bāyu bahiba emanta pruthibī ṭaḷaṭaḷa dhruba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, wonke umuntu uzoba ongalungile; akekho ozoba nankolo/msebenzi osele. Lesi kuzoba isizathu sokuthi iNkosi Enkulu uKalki isungule iDharma. Imimoya engamashumi amane nesishiyagalolunye izophephetha ngendlela yokuthi uMhlaba uzothuthumela.

UMahapurush ubhala komunye wemibhalo yakhe yeMalika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ପବନ ବହି ଅଣଚାଶ,
କ୍ଷିତିକି କରିଦେବ ଧ୍ଵଂସ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Pabana bahi aṇachāśha,
kṣhitiki karideba dhwaṃsa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngesikhathi somsebenzi wokubhujiswa weNkosi uKalki, imimoya engamashumi amane nesishiyagalolunye izophephetha futhi ilethe ukubhujiswa eMhlabeni.

Umgcwele oyimpumputhe owazalelwa e-Odisha ngekhulu le-19, uBhima Bhoi, naye wabhala ngalesi sihloko embhalweni wakhe weMalika. UMahapurush ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପବନ ବହିବ ପର୍ବତ ଦୋହଲିଯିବ,
ଚାରିମେଘ ମିଳି ବର୍ଷିବେ ଚାରିଦିଗ ଅନ୍ଧାର ଘୋଟିବ।
ମୋ ଭାଇ, ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଦେଖାଯିବ ନାହିଁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Aṇachāśha khaṇḍa pabana bahiba parbata dohalijiba,
chārimegha miḷi barṣhibe chāridiga andhāra ghoṭiba.
Mo bhāi, chandra sūrya dekhājiba nāhiñ.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, kuzofika isikhathi lapho imimoya engamashumi amane nesishiyagalolunye izophephetha futhi ngisho nezintaba namagquma kuzonyakaza. Amafu amane okuthiwa u-Avarta, uSamvarta, uPushkara, noDrona azona ndawonye. Ilanga nenyanga ngeke kubonakale esibhakabhakeni, futhi ubumnyama obukhulu buzogubuzela zonke izinhlangothi ezine.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala futhi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଟଳଟଳ ହେଉଥିବ ସପ୍ତଦ୍ଵୀପ ମହୀ,
ସେ ବେଳେ ଥଳକୂଳ ରହିବଟି ନାହିଁ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ହୋଇ ପବନ ବହିବ,
ସରକାର ବୁଦ୍ଧିବିଦ୍ୟା ଉଭେଇ ଯିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ṭaḷaṭaḷa heuthibā saptadwīpa mahī,
se beḷe thaḷakūḷa rahibāṭi nāhiñ.
aṇachāśha khaṇଡa hoi pabana bahiba,
sarakāra buddhibidyā ubhei jiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngesikhathi sokusungulwa kweDharma ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, kuzoba nesiphithiphithi kuwo wonke uMhlaba kuhlanganise namazwekazi ayisikhombisa. Iphephetha ngolaka olukhulu kakhulu, imimoya engamashumi amane nesishiyagalolunye izobhubhisa zonke izakhiwo. Ngaleso sikhathi, akekho ozophepha. Amandla kahulumeni nokuhlakanipha kwawo ngeke kusize. Kuphela labo abalandela indlela yeDharma futhi abazinikele ngokuphelele eNkosini Enkulu abazothola ukuvikelwa.

I-Odia Sloka:

କର ପତ୍ର ଯୋଡି କହଇ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଗୁରୁପାଦେ ଶିର ଦେଇ,
ପ୍ରଳୟ କାଳର ବାରଣ କହିବା କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ ଯହିଁ।
ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଏମନ୍ତ କାଳ ଯେ ଦିନେ ଆସି ପହଞ୍ଚିବ,
ଦଶ ଗ୍ରାମ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଜଣେକ ରହିବେ ଦେବତାଙ୍କ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ।
ସେ କାଳେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ଭାଦ୍ରବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣିମା ଦିନ,
ଏମନ୍ତ ପବନ ବହିବ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ସକଳ ହୋଇବ ଲୀନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kara patra joḍi kahai Bāraṅga gurupāde śira dei,
praḷaya kāḷara bāraṇa kahibā kaḷi śeṣha heba jahiñ.
Śuṇare Bāraṅga emanta kāḷa je dine āsi pahañchiba,
daśha grāma madhye jaṇeka rahibe debatāṅka ābirbhāba.
Se kāḷe Bāraṅga aṇachāśha bāyu Bhādraba pūrṇimā dina,
emanta pabana bahiba Bāraṅga sakaḷa hoiba līna.

Incazelo:
Lapho ebuzwa umfundi uBaranga ngomsebenzi wokubhujiswa kaKalki owenzeka ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, uMahapurush Sishu Ananta Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika ukuthi isintu kuzofanele sibone usuku lapho umuntu oyedwa kuphela, njengonkulunkulu, ezosinda phakathi kwemizi eyi-10. Ngaleso sikhathi, imimoya engamashumi amane nesishiyagalolunye izophephetha ngesivinini esikhulu futhi ibhubhise zonke izakhiwo. Lokho kuzoba usuku lwenyanga egcwele enyangeni kaBhadra.

11.4 Uzamcolo Womlilo (Agni Pralaya)

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ethi 'Gupta Kheda Malika':

I-Odia Sloka:

ଧନ, ସମ୍ପତ୍ତି ଓ ଟଙ୍କା ଯାହା ଅଛି ଜମା,
ସବୁଯିବ ପୋଡି ଭାଇ ନିଆଁର ଗଞ୍ଜମା।
ଚଉଦିଗେ ନିଆଁ କାହୁଁ ଦଗ୍ଧି ଏ ଧରଣୀ,
ଓଦା କଞ୍ଚା ନ ମାନିବ ସବୁ ଖାଏ ଅଗ୍ନି।
ଅଗ୍ନିର କରାଳ ରୂପ ଜୀବାତ୍ମା ଦେଖିବ,
ହାଁ ହାଁ ହୋଇ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଛାଡିଯିବ।
ସମଗ୍ର ବିଶ୍ଵରେ ଧ୍ଵଂସ ଲୀଳୋ ଯେ ଚାଲିବ,
କେଉଁଠି କମ୍ ବେଶୀ ଜାଣେ ତା ଦଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dhana, sampatti o ṭaṅkā jāhā achi jamā,
sabujiba poḍi bhāi niāñra gañjamā.
Chaudige niāñ kāhuñ dagdhi e dharaṇī,
odā kañchā na māniba sabu khāe agni.
Agnira karāḷarūpa jībātmā dekhiba,
hāñ hāñ hoi tāra prāṇa chāḍijiba.
Samagra biśware dhwaṃsa līḷā je chāliba,
keuṇṭhi kam beśī jāne tā daiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, umdanso womlilo uzobonakala ezinhlangothini zonke ezine. Umhlaba uzosha. Abantu abaningi bazofa. Ingcebo, impahla, nemali kuzosha kube umlotha. Lezi zenzo ezibhubhisayo zizoba umhlaba wonke, zibonakale kancane kwezinye izindawo futhi kakhulu kwezinye.

11.5 Inhlekelele Yasemkhathini/Emoyeni

I-Odia Sloka:

ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ଯାକ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

nānā utpāta jāka antarikhye heba,
bhakta binā anya kehi jāni na pāriba.

Incazelo:
UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ethi 'Chakada Madana' ukuthi ngesikhathi sokugcina seKali Yuga, lapho ukusungulwa kweDharma kuqhubeka, kuzoba nezinkinga eziningi emkhathini. Kuzoba nezinguquko eziningana emaplanethi nasezinkanyezini, kanye nasezindaweni nasejubaneni lazo. UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi ngaphandle kwabazinikele, abantu abavamile ngeke bakwazi ukwazi ngalezi zinto.

UMahapurush wabuye wabhala ngalesi sihloko encwadini yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’:

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷବେଳେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ରଣ ଗୋଳ ସେ କାଳେ ହୋଇବ ବହୁତ।
ଚାରିଦିଗମାନଙ୍କରେ ଲାଞ୍ଜାମାନ ପଡି,
ସେ କାଳେ ବହୁତ ଶବ୍ଦ ହେବ ଘଡ଼ଘଡ଼ି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷiyuga śheṣhabeḷe pruthbī utpāta,
raṇa goḷa se kāḷe hoiba bahuta.
chāridigamānaṅkare lāñjāmāna paḍi,
se kāḷe bahuta śhabda heba ghaḍaghaḍi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, esigabeni sokugcina seKali Yuga, kuzoba nezinkinga eziningi nezimpi ezinkulu Emhlabeni. Kuzoba khona ukuwa kwezinkanyezi/ama-asteroid ezinhlangothini zonke ezine futhi kuzozwakala imisindo evela esikhaleni.

11.6 Izifo Nobhubhane

OMahapurush babhala emibhalweni eminingana yeMalika ukuthi ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, izifo eziningi nezinhlupho zizoguqa umphakathi wesintu. IMalika yasho ngokucacile ukuthi odokotela bazohluleka ekuxilongeni nasekwelapheni lezo zifo nezinhlupho, futhi imithi abayinikezayo izobonakala ingasebenzi.

OMahapurush baphinde bathi empeleni, lezi zifo nezinhlupho zizolethwa amandla omama anjengoYogamaya (Yogamaya), Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, Dakinis, Pisachinis, njll. La mandla omama azohamba umhlaba wonke ngezindlela ezincane njengalezi zifo futhi angene emizimbeni yabantu.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Bigyanakalpa’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷ ପଡିବ ଲୋକେ ଯିବେ ବହୁ ମରି,
ଚଉଷଠି ରୋଗ ଭ୍ରମୁଥିବେ ଫେରି ଫେରି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Durbhikṣha paḍiba loke jibe bahu mari,
chauṣhaṭhi roga bhramuthibe pheri pheri.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngesikhathi sokusungula iDharma ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, kuzoba khona indlala nesomiso, okuholela ekufeni kwabantu abaningi. Izinhlobo ezingamashumi ayisithupha nane zezifo zizosakazeka emhlabeni wonke.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das uphinde abhale encwadini yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବାଡି ବେମାରୀରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Bāḍi bemārīre aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, izifo eziningi zizoba imbangela yokufa kwabantu abaningi kakhulu.

UMahapurush wabuye wabhala ku-‘Jaiphula Malika’ yakhe naku-‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅଚିହ୍ନା ରୋଗ ଘୋଟିବ,
ବଇଦର ବୁଦ୍ଧି ବଣା ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୌଷଧି କାମ ନ କରିବ।
ଅଚିହ୍ନା ବ୍ୟାଧି ଯେ ଜଗତ୍ରେ ଘୋଟିବ ବଇଦ ବଣା ହୋଇବେ,
ରୋଗ ନ ଚିହ୍ନିଣ ବଣା ହେଉଥିବେ ଅନେକ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ମରିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Achinhā roga ghoṭiba,
baidara buddhi baṇā hoiba lo jāiphula,
mahauṣadhi kāma na kariba.
Achinhā byādhi je jagatre ghoṭiba baida baṇā hoibe,
roga na chinhiṇa baṇā heuthibe aneka prāṇī maribe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, kuzofika izifo ezingaziwa kangangokuthi ukuhlakanipha kodokotela kuzoyeka ukusebenza. Lezi zifo ngeke zihlonzwe, futhi imithi ngeke isebenze ekwelapheni kwazo, ngenxa yalokho abantu abaningi kakhulu bazofa.

UMahapurush ubhala kwenye yemibhalo yakhe yeMalika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ରୋଗ ରୂପେ ଯୋଗମାୟା ଶରୀରେ ଖେଳିବ,
କାହାର ଆୟତ ପୁଣି ସେଠାରେ ନ ଥିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Roga rūpe Yogamāyā sharīre kheḷiba,
kāhāra āyata puṇi seṭhāre na thiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, uMama Yogamaya uzongena emizimbeni yabantu ngesimo sesifo.

UMahapurush uqhubeka ebhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଗଦ ମହୌଷଧି କରିବନି କାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ମାଡି ଆସିବଟି ରୋଗ,
ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣିବୁ ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀକୁ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଯୋଗ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Gada mahauṣhadhi karibani kārjya māḍi āsibaṭi roga,
Se kāḷe jānibu alpa dine pruthbīku praḷaya yoga.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, lapho kufika izifo ezinjalo nezinhlupho kangangokuthi imithi ibonakala ingasebenzi ekwelapheni kwazo, khona-ke kufanele kuqondwe ukuthi isikhathi sokuphela Emhlabeni siseduze.

UMahapurush uphinde abhale:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ୟାଧି ହୋଇ ପିଶାଚୀ ଆସିବେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ନାଶିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Byādhi hoi piśhāchī āsibe,
prāṇīmānanku gupte nāśhibe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, amandla omama azongena ngasese emizimbeni ngesimo sesifo futhi abhubhise abantu abaningi. Kodwa kanye nalokhu, uMahapurush uphinde wakhuluma encwadini yakhe yeMalika ngendlela umphakathi wesintu ongabalekela ngayo lezi zifo nezinhlupho.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chausathi Patala’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମାରେ ଯେଉଁ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ରହିବେ,
ରୋଗ କବଳରେ କେବେହିଁ ସେମାନେ ନ ପଡିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Satya śhānti dayā kṣhamāre jeuñ prāṇī rahibe,
roga kabaḷare kebehiñ semāne na paḍibe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, labo bantu abamukela izimfanelo ezinhle njengeqiniso, ukuthula, ububele, nokuthethelela ekuziphatheni kwabo bazohlala bekhululekile kulezi zifo.

11.7 Impi Yomhlaba Wonke

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Jaiphula Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗିବ ମହାସମର,
ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅତି ପ୍ରବଳ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ନାରଖାର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Lāgiba mahāsamara,
deśha deśha madhye ati prabala lo jāiphula,
pruthibī hoijiba nārakhāra.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kuzokwenzeka impi enkulu Emhlabeni. Kuzoba khona impi enkulu phakathi kwezizwe, okuholela ekubhujisweni komhlaba.

U-Achyutananda Das ji wabuye wabhala ku-‘Jaiphula Malika’ uqobo:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ପୁଣ,
ଭୟଙ୍କର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏହା କଳି ଶେଷ ବୋଲି ଜାଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Deśha deśha madhye puṇa,
bhayaṅkara juddha heba pramāṇa lo jāiphula,
ehā kaḷi śeṣha boli jāṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, esigabeni sokugcina seKali Yuga, kuzoqhuma izimpi ezinkulu kakhulu phakathi kwamazwe ahlukahlukene. Ngamanye amazwi, lapho kuqala impi esabekayo phakathi kwezizwe, kufanele kuqondwe njengokuphela kweKali Yuga.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅଣୁ ପରମାଣୁ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରି ଭୟଙ୍କର,
ଗୋଟିଏ ଅଣୁ ବୋମାର କରାମତି ସାର।
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଅଣୁବୋମା ଗର୍ଜିବ ମହୀରେ,
କାନ୍ଦିବ ଧରଣୀ ରାଣୀ ହା ହୁତାଶରେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Aṇu paramāṇu juddha bhāri bhayaṅkara,
goṭie aṇu bomāra karāmati sāra.
lakṣha lakṣha aṇubomā garjiba mahīre,
kāndiba dharaṇī rāṇī hā hutāśhare.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kuzokwenzeka impi ye-athomu-yenuzi Emhlabeni ezoba yethusa kakhulu. Umhlaba wonke uzonyakaza ngokusetshenziswa kwamabhomu enuzi angenakubalwa. UMama Umhlaba uzokhala.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das uthi ngalesi sikhathi samanje, kuzokwenzeka impi enkulu phakathi kweNdiya nePakistan. Ngokusho kukaMahapurush, le mpi phakathi kweNdiya nePakistan izogcina iphenduke impi yomhlaba wonke ngoba amazwe amaningi azosekela iPakistan kule mpi, kanti amanye amazwe azolwela iNdiya. Kule mpi, izizwe ezinkulu zomhlaba zizohlukaniswa zibe amakamu amabili.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku-‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ yakhe:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ଭାରତ ସପକ୍ଷ ହେବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଜାଣ,
ଇରାକ ଯେ ସମର୍ଥନ ଦେବ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chīna Pākisthāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa,
Ruṣhiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmāna Jāpāna.
Bhārata sapakṣha hebe mahāśhakti jāṇa,
Irāka je samarthana deba Pākisthāna.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kule mpi, amazwe afana neChina, iMelika, ne-Iraq azosekela iPakistan, kanti amandla afana neRussia, iJalimane, neJapane azolwela iNdiya.

UMahapurush ubhala encwadini yeMalika enesihloko esithi ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ,
ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପୁଣି ଚୀନ୍ ସଇନୁ ତୁର୍କୀ ଇରାନ ଜାଣ,
ବ୍ରିଟିଶ ଯେ ସଇନ୍ୟ ସବେ ମିଳିଣ।
ପାକସ୍ଥିାନକୁ ବଳ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ସର୍ବେ ଆବର,
ପାକିସ୍ଥାନକୁ ଦେବେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଲାଗିବ ଘୋର।
ଏହା ଦେଖି ରୁଷିଆ ଆସି ହୋଇବ ଠିଆ,
ଭାରତ ସମରକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ସେ ଏକୁଟିଆ।
ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ଶସ୍ତ୍ର ସଇନ୍ୟ ଯୋଗାଇ ଦେବ ଜାଣ,
ଭାରତ ସହିତ ମିଶି କରିବ ରଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākisthānṭi raṅge,
mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Puṇi Chīna sainu Turkī Irāna jāṇa,
Briṭisha je sainya sabe miḷiṇa.
Pākisthānku baḷa astra sarbe ābara,
Pākisthānku debe juddha lāgiba ghora.
Ehā dekhi Ruṣiā āsi hoiba ṭhiā,
Bhārata samarakṣetre se ekuṭiā.
Astra shastra sainya jogāi deba jāṇa,
Bhārata sahita mishi kariba raṇa.

Incazelo:
UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi izwe elibizwa ngePakistan lizobandakanyeka empini enkulu neNdiya. Kule mpi, amazwe afana neChina, iTurkey, i-Iran, iNgilandi, neMelika azosiza iPakistan futhi anikeze ukwesekwa kwezempi. Ebona konke lokhu, umngane omkhulu weNdiya, iRussia, uzoza kuqala futhi yedwa ukuzosiza iNdiya. Izohlinzeka iNdiya ngazo zonke izinhlobo zezikhali, ukubambisana kwezomnotho, nokwesekwa kwezempi futhi izokulwa impi enkulu ngokumelene nezizwe zezitha egameni leNdiya.

UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi hhayi iRussia kuphela, kodwa neJapane neJalimane nazo zizolwela iNdiya kule mpi. Ku-‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ uqobo, uqhubeka ebhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଜାପାନର ରାଗ ଅଛି ହିରୋଶିମା କଥା,
ପକାଇଥିଲା ବୋମା ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଜାଣିଥା।
ସେହି କ୍ରୋଧ ଲିଭିନାହିଁ ଜାପାନର ଜାଣ,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଭାରତ ପଟ ନେବେ ସେହି ପୁଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Jāpānara rāga achi Hirośhimā kathā,
pakāithilā bomā juddhe jāṇithā.
Sehi krodha libhināhiñ Jāpānara jāṇa,
juddhe Bhārata paṭa nebe sehi puṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iJapane ngeke ikwazi ukukhohlwa ukuwiswa kwebhomu le-athomu kuyo yiMelika. Intukuthelo yeJapane ngaseMelika ngeke yehle. Ilangabi lokuziphindiselela lizoqhubeka livutha enhliziyweni yayo, ngenxa yalokho iJapane izokulwa neMelika ngasohlangothini lweNdiya ngesikhathi sempi yomhlaba yesithathu. Ngesicelo seJapane, iJalimane nayo izokulwa ngasohlangothini lweNdiya.

12 Impi Eseleyo yeMahabharata eKali Yuga

AmaPanchasakhas abhala emibhalweni yeBhabishya Malika ukuthi iMpi Yomhlaba eyenzeka ekupheleni kweKali Yuga empeleni izoba impi esele yeMahabharata. OMahapurusha bacacisa ukuthi impi yezinsuku eziyi-18 yeMahabharata ekupheleni kweDwapara Yuga ayizange iqedwe ngokugcwele ngoba, ngaleso sikhathi, iNkosi Krishna yanquma ukungaqhubeki nengxenye yokugcina yempi ngaleso sikhathi, kodwa ukuyiqeda ekupheleni kweKali Yuga.

Ngaphezu kwalokhu, amaMahapurusha abhale ngokuningiliziwe ngale mpi emibhalweni yabo yeMalika futhi embula izinto eziningi, njengokuthi izokwenzeka nini, bangaki abantu abazofa ngesikhathi sayo, buzoba yini ubude bayo, izothatha hlobo luni, izokwenzeka ngaphansi kobuholi bakabani, obani abazobamba iqhaza ohlangothini lwaseNdiya, izophela kuphi, nokuthi kuzokwenzekani ngemuva kwale mpi, njll.

12.1 Izokwenzeka nini le mpi?

I-Odia Sloka:

ଯୁଗଶେଷ କଳି ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଯମ ମାଆ ସାତ ଭଉଣୀ ଧରିଥିବେ ଖପର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Yugaśheṣha kaḷi Bhārata juddha hoiba ghora,
Yama māā sāta bhauṇī dharithibe khapara.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, impi esele yeMahabharata izophela ekupheleni kweKali Yuga. Kule mpi, uYama Maa Sata Bhauni noma oNkulunkulukazi Abayisikhombisa Abangomama (iSaptamatruka) abatholakala eBiraja Khetra (uChamunda (Chamunda), uVarahi, uIndrani, uVaishnavi, uBrahmani, uKaumari, uMaheshwari, noNarasimhi) bazobhubhisa izoni.

Echaza ukuthi le mpi izokwenzeka nini ekupheleni kweKali Yuga, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna Shani āsi deuthibā ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
Dasha aṇā jiba chha-a aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, impi esele yaseNdiya (iMahabharata (Mahabharata)) izoqala ngesikhathi sokuhlangana kweMeena-Shani (iPisces-Saturn), lapho abantu abaningi kakhulu bezofa.

12.2 Izokwenzeka ngaluphi uhlobo le mpi futhi izokulwelwa kuphi esigabeni sokugcina?

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଓଳିଏ ବାକି ଅଛି ଜାଣ,
କଳିଯୁଗରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇ ପୂରଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mahābhārata juddha oḷie bāki achi jāṇa,
Kaḷiyugare biśhwajuddha hoi pūraṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, yazi ukuthi ingxenye yempi yeMahabharata isasele, ezophela njengeMpi Yomhlaba Wonke eKali Yuga.

UMahapurush uqhubeka nokubhala ku-‘Jaiphula Malika’ yakhe:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ପୁଣି ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କହେ ଯବନ ବାହି ଆସିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Oḷie Bhārata juddha,
Oḍiśhā deśhare puṇi hoiba lo jāiphula,
kahe jabana bāhi āsiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, impi esele yeMahabharata izokwenzeka eOdisha, futhi ngaleso sikhathi, amasosha amaningi ezizwe ezilwayo azongena ngaphakathi eNdiya.

USanta Achyutananda Das ubhala komunye wemibhalo yakhe yeMalika:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଳିଯୁଗରେ ମହାଭାରତଟି ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳରୁ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପତ୍ତି ହୋଇବ ଠିକେ ଠିକେ ଏହା ଶୁଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

E kaḷiyugare Mahābhārataṭi Utkaḷa bhūiñ pramāṇa,
Utkaḷaru sabu utpatti hoiba ṭhike ṭhike ehā śhuṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, impi esele yeMahabharata izokulwelwa eKali Yuga, ezokwenzeka ezweni laseUtkala noma eOdisha.
