12.3 Buzoba yini ubude besikhathi bale mpi?

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das uphinde wabikezela embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’ ukuthi le mpi esele yeMahabharata izohlala isikhathi esiyizinyanga eziyi-13 sezizonke.

UMahapurush wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଯେ ରହିଥିବ ଶୁଣ ହେତୁ ଦ୍ୱାରେ,
ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ସେତିକି ବେଳରେ।
ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ,
ସେ ଓଳିକେ ତେର ମାସ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Olie je rahithiba shuṇa hetu dwāre,
Bhārata juddha hoiba setiki xelare.
Sehi olikare nātha kali shesha hexa,
Se olike tera māsa bhoga je hoiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, impi esele yeMahabharata izohlala isikhathi esiyizinyanga eziyishumi nantathu sezizonke, futhi ngokuqedwa kwaleyo mpi, ithonya leKali Yuga kuloMhlaba liyophela ngokuphelele.

12.4 Obani abazobamba iqhaza ohlangothini lwaseNdiya esigabeni sokugcina?

AmaMahapurusha abhala ukuthi engxenyeni yokugcina yale mpi, ngenkathi ngakolunye uhlangothi kuzoba namabutho amakhulu ezizwe ezilwayo, ngakolunye uhlangothi, wonke amaqhawe enkathini yeMahabharata nezinkulungwane zabangcwele, izazi, nababoni bazobe bekhona ngaphansi kobuholi beNkosi Enkulu uKalki uqobo.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala ku-‘Bhabishyat Malika’ yakhe:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ବେଳେ ଭାରତ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହାରିଯିବ ତ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ତ।
ଭାରତ ନରନାରୀ ସର୍ବେ ଡାକିବେ ହରି,
ରକ୍ଷାକର ପ୍ରଭୁ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଗଲୁଟି ସରି।
ଆହେ କୁଞ୍ଜବିହାରୀ ବାରେ ଶୁଣ ଗୁହାରି,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ତେଜି ଥରେ ଆସ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ଦେଖ ନେତ୍ର ଫେଡ଼ିତ ପୁଣ୍ୟଭୂମି ଭାରତ,
ବିଦେଶୀଙ୍କ ହସ୍ତେ ହୁଏ କି ହନ୍ତସନ୍ତ।
ଭାରତର କାଳର ଶୁଣି ସେ ଆଦିମୂଳ,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ପୁର ତେଜି ହେବେ ବାହାର।
କଳ୍କୀ ଦେବ ନାମ ବହି ସେ ନାରାୟଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳ ଦେଶ କଟକରେ ଦେବେ ଦର୍ଶନ।
ସେହି ବେଳରେ ଜାଣ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବଳରାମ,
ଭୀମ ସହ ବାହାରିବେ ବୀର ଅର୍ଜୁନ।
ଭୀଷ୍ମ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ଆବର ଅଶ୍ୱତ୍ଥାମା ଯେ ବୀର,
କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଶଲ୍ୟ ଜୟଦ୍ରଥ ହେବେ ବାହାର।
ବେଲାଲସେନ ବୀର ପୁଣି ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବାଳବୀର ଅଭିମନ୍ୟୁ ଘେନି ସଙ୍ଗର।
ବୀର ବବ୍ରୁବାହନ ବାହାରିବଟି ଜାଣ,
ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ଘଟୋତ୍କଚ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନିଣ।
ସର୍ବେ ହୋଇଣ ମେଳି ବିଦେଶୀୟଙ୍କୁ ଦଳି,
ଦେବେ ମୁହୂର୍ତ୍ତକ ମଧ୍ଯେ ଲଗାଇ କଳି।
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଶକତି ବଳେ ଅପଲକ ନେତ୍ରରେ,
ଚାହିଁ ବିଜ୍ଞାନ ଶକ୍ତିକି ଜିଣିବେ ହେଳେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ehibeḷe Bhārata nishche hārijiba ta,
hāhākāra paḍijiba chāriāḍe ta.
Bhārata naranārī sarbe dākibe Hari,
rakhākara prabhu nishche galuṭi sari.
Āhe Kuñjabihārī bāre shuṇa guhāରି,
Baikuṇṭha teji thare āsa Shrī Hari.
Dekha netra pheḍita puṇyabhūmi Bhārata,
bideshīṅka haste hue ki hantasanta.
Bhāratara kāḷara shuṇi se ādimūḷa,
Baikuṇṭhapura teji hebe bāhāra.
Kalkī deba nāma bahi se Nārāyaṇa,
Utkaḷa desha Kaṭakare debe darshana.
Sehibeḷere jāṇa Shrī Kṛṣṇa Baḷarāma,
Bhīma saha bāhāribe bīra Arjuna.
Bhīṣma Droṇa ābara Ashwatthāmā je bīra,
Karṇa Shalya Jayadratha hebe bāhāra.
Belāḷasena bīra puṇi hebe bāhāra,
bāḷabīra Abhimanyu gheni saṅgara.
Bīra Babhrubāhana bāhāribaṭi jāṇa,
Ekalabya Ghaṭotkacha saṅge gheniṇa.
Sarbe hoiṇa meḷi bideshīyaṅku daḷi,
debe muhūrttaka madhye lagāi kaḷi.
Mantra shakti baḷe apalaka netrare,
chāhi bijñāna shaktiki jiṇibe heḷe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngesikhathi seMpi Yomhlaba Yesithathu, bebona ukuhlaselwa kweNdiya ngezinhlangothi ezine yizitha zangaphandle, abantu baseNdiya bazokwesaba futhi bakhale ngosizi. Bengenamandla, bonke bazokhumbula iNkosi futhi bancenge ukuze bavikelwe. Bazothi—O Nkosi, yizwa isicelo sethu, sisindise, shiya iVaikuntha uze eMhlabeni ukuze usivikele.

Ethintwe yilesi sicelo esidabukisayo sabazinikele futhi ebona ukuhlaselwa kweNdiya ngezandla zezitha zangaphandle, uNkulunkulu Ophakeme uNarayana (uMaha Vishnu (Maha Vishnu)) uqobo, ekuzalweni kwakhe njengoKalki (Kalki), uzovela eCuttack, eOdisha, futhi anikeze idarshan kubazinikele.

UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi kanye neNkosi Kalki ngaleso sikhathi, uBhima, uArjuna, uBhishma, uDrona, uAshwatthama, uKarna, uShalya, uJayadratha, uBarbarika, uAbhimanyu, uBabhruvahana, uEkalavya, noGhatotkacha, ababezobe sebezelwe, bazolwa ohlangothini lwaseNdiya futhi banqobe kabi izizwe ezilwayo. Amandla emantra aphezulu alawa maqhawe azobonakala ephakeme kunamandla ezitha asekelwe isayensi yesimanje.

Ngalolu daba olufanayo, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରସ୍ତାବିତ କଳି ଭାରତ ସରିବ ହେବ ଭାରତ ସମର।
ତିନିଯୁଗ ଯୋଦ୍ଧା ଏକ ଯୁଗରେ ମହାସମର ଲାଗିବ,
କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକ୍ତ ମନୋସ୍କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Prastābita kaḷi Bhārata sariba heba Bhārata samara.
Tinijuga joddhā eka jugare mahāsamara lāgiba,
kahai Achyuta bhakta hebe mukta manoskāmanā pūriba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, impi yeMahabharata izokulwelwa eKali Yuga, lapho kuzobamba iqhaza khona amaqhawe azo zonke izinkathi ezintathu ezedlule.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das uqhubeka nokubhala ku-‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ସମର ହେବ ଦେଲି ଚେତାଇ,
ସ୍ୱୟଂ ପରଂବ୍ରହ୍ମ ମୂରତି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତସାଇଁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Oḷie Bhārata samara heba deli chetāi,
swayaṃ Parambrahma mūrati prabhu Jagatasāiñ.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, impi esele yeMahabharata izokwenzeka ngaphansi kobuholi boNkulunkulu Ophakeme uNarayana (ukuzalwa kwakhe njengoKalki) uqobo.

Ngalolu daba, omunye wamaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Yashovanta Das, naye wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Nidagha Nighanta’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ରହି ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ,
ଆଦେଶ ଦେବେଟି ମାଧବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Oḷie rahi juddha heba,
ādeśha debeṭi Mādhaba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, impi esele yeMahabharata izophela, futhi uMadhava uqobo, iNkosi Enkulu uKalki, uzonikeza umyalo wokuqala le mpi.

Usanta omkhulu waseOdisha owazalwa ngekhulu lama-20, uThakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, naye wabhala ngalolu daba embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ତୋପର ତପର ସମର,
ହୋଇବ ଅତି ଭୟଙ୍କର।
ତପକୁ ତୋପ ନ ପାରିବ,
ତପର ବଳ ବେଶୀ ହେବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Topara tapara samara,
hoiba ati bhayaṅkara.
tapaku topa na pāriba,
tapara baḷa beśhī heba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kuzoba nempi enkulu kakhulu phakathi kwamandla ezibhamu ezizwe ezilwayo zaseNdiya namandla okuzila (iTapasya (Tapasya)) abantu abangcwele nezazi zaseNdiya abaningi kakhulu, lapho amandla emicibisholo yesimanje yezitha azophela phambi kwamandla okuzila aseNdiya, futhi ekugcineni iNdiya izophuma inqobile kule mpi enkulu.

12.5 Izophela kuphi impi eNdiya?

AmaMahapurusha abhala emibhalweni yeMalika ukuthi lapho amabutho angaphandle efika eOdisha, azolwa ezindaweni ezahlukene eOdisha, njengaseBirat Gadi (Birat Gadi) yaseCuttack, eChoudwar, eJobra, eBarabati, naseKaliaboda; ePuri; eJajpur; eGohira Tikiri yaseBhadrak; eParadip; eBalasore, njll.

Yize le mpi izokwenzeka kuzo zonke lezi zindawo eOdisha, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wathi ngokucacile izophela ngokuphelele eKhandagiri eBhubaneswar lapho iNkosi Enkulu uqobo ibhubhisa wonke amasosha ezizwe ezilwayo ngeSudarshana Chakra yayo.

UMahapurush wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Kalpatika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ ବହୁତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରୂପ ଧରି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକ୍ର ଧରି ବିନାଶିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Oḍiśhā deśhare Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre bahuta juddha hoiba,
Ananta Keśharī rūpa dhari prabhu chakra dhari bināśhiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kuzoba nempi enkulu kakhulu eKhandagiri eOdisha. Kuleyo ndawo efanayo, iNkosi Enkulu uKalki izobhubhisa amasosha amaningi kakhulu ngeSudarshana Chakra yayo.

12.6 Kuzokwenzekani ngale mpi futhi kuzokwenzekani ngemuva kwayo?

Ku-‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala ivesi eliyingxenye yalo yokuqala kanje:

I-Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Sehi oḷikare nātha kaḷi śheṣha heba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngokushesha nje lapho le mpi yeMahabharata isiphelile, ithonya leKali Yuga liyophela.

Bese uMahapurush Achyutananda Das naye abhala ivesi embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Chakada Madana’, umugqa walo wokuqala uthi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଯେ ସରିବ ପୃଥିବୀ ଶାନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Oḷie Bhārata juddha je sariba pruthibī śhānta hoiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngokuqedwa kwempi esele yeMahabharata, uMhlaba uzoba nokuthula. Bese konke ukuphazamiseka eMhlabeni kuzophela.

12.7 Incazelo yesimo samandla amakhulu afana neMelika neYurophu eMpini Yomhlaba

Amapanchasakha Mahapurushas abhala emibhalweni yabo yeMalika ukuthi iMpi Yomhlaba Yesithathu izoba yimbi kakhulu kangangokuthi zonke izizwe zizothinteka yiyo. Wonke amazwe athathwa namuhla njengamandla anamandla kakhulu emhlabeni, le mpi yokuphela kweKali Yuga izowashiya echithakele.

Echaza izimo zaseMelika kule mpi, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das wabhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଭାଇ ମୂଳୁ ଧ୍ୱଂସ ଯିବ,
ଗର୍ବରେ ଜଳୁଛି ରାଜା ବିଶ୍ୱ ମୁଖ୍ୟ ହେବ।
ଧରାକୁ ମଣୁଛି ସରା କହେ ମୁହିଁ ଶ୍ରେଷ୍ଠ,
ଉଡ଼ାଇବି ପୃଥିବୀକୁ କରେ ମୁଁ ଶପଥ।
କାହାରି ଗର୍ବ ସହି ନ ପାରିବେ ଧାତା,
ଜଳାଇ ଦେବେ ତାହାକୁ ଉଡ଼ାଇବେ ମଥା।
ନ୍ୟୁୟର୍କ ସହର ଧ୍ୱଂସ ପଦା ହୋଇଯିବ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଅଥର୍ବ ନିର୍ବେଦ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Āmerikā rāṣhṭra bhāi mūḷu dhwaṅsa jiba,
garbare jaḷuchi rājā biśhwa mukhya heba.
dharāku maṇuchi sarā kahe muiñ śhreṣhṭha,
uḍāibi pruthibīku kare muñ śhapatha.
kāhāri garba sahi na pāribe dhātā,
jaḷāi debe tāhāku uḍāibe mathā.
New York sahara dhwaṅsa padā hoijiba,
hāhākāra paḍijiba atharba nirbeda.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, isizwe esibizwa ngeMelika sizozibheka njengomholi womhlaba. Sigxile ekuziqhenyeni nasekuqhosheni, sizozibiza ngokuthi singcono kakhulu futhi sisabise ngokubhubhisa amanye amazwe empini. UMahapurush waphinde wathi iNkosi Enkulu ayikubekezeleli ukuziqhenya kwanoma ubani ngakho-ke izophinde iphule ukuziqhenya kweMelika. Idolobha laseNew York laseMelika lizobhujiswa, futhi leso sizwe sizobhekana nokubhujiswa okuphelele njengofakazi wokubhujiswa okukhulu.

Ku ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ uqobo, uMngcwele Achyutananda Das ubhala futhi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ଇଉରୋପ ମହାଦେଶ ଜାଣ,
ସ୍ଥିତି ହରାଇବ ତାର ବିଷାକ୍ତ ପବନ।
ସବୁରି ଧ୍ୱଂସର କାରଣ ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ନିଶ୍ଚିହ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଗର୍ବି ଅଟେ ପୁଣ।
ନିଜର ଇଜ୍ଜତ ପୃଥିବୀ ପରେ ଦେଖାଇଲା,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧକୁ ତ ଜୀବନରେ ସିଦ୍ଧହସ୍ତ କଲା।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Āmerikā Iuropa mahādesha jāṇa,
Sthiti harāiba tāra biṣākta pabana.
Saburi dhwaṃsara kāraṇa Āmerikā jāṇa,
Nishchinha hoiba juddhe garbi aṭe puṇa.
Nijara ijjata pṛthwī pare dekhāilā,
Juddhaku ta jībanare siddhahasta kalā.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, imbangela yeMpi Yomhlaba Yesithathu kuzoba isizwe esibizwa nge ‘Melika’. Leso sizwe sizosungula inhlonipho nokuziqhenya kwaso phezu kwawo wonke uMhlaba futhi sithole ubuchule ekulweni impi. UMahapurush waphinde wabhala ukuthi ngenxa yalokhu kuziqhenya, igama laso nomkhondo waso kuzosulwa eMpini Yomhlaba Yesithathu. Ngemuva kwempi, isimo sezulu lapho sizoba nobuthi, futhi ngenxa yalokho, abantu abaningi abangenakubalwa bazofa emazweni aseMelika naseYurophu.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku ‘Bhabishyat Malika’ yakhe:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶେଷେ ଇଉରୋପ ଦେଶ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ହୋଇବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ,
ବଞ୍ଚି ରହିବେ କେବଳ ଭକ୍ତ ଅଂଶ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Śheṣhe Iuropa deśha juddhe hoiba dhwaṅsa,
bañchi rahibe kebaḷa bhakta aṅśha.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kule mpi, izwekazi laseYurophu lizobhujiswa ngokuphelele. Ngaphandle kwabazinikele beNkosi, akekho ozosinda kulezo zizwe.

Ku ‘Jaiphula Malika’, uMngcwele Achyutananda Das ubhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଦେଶ,
ହାଡ଼ ମାଳ ହୋଇଯିବ ବିଶେଷ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୀ ଦୁଲୁ ଦୁଲୁ ହେବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Paśhchima uttara deśha,
hāḍa māḷa hoijiba biśheṣha lo jāiphula,
mahī dulu dulu heba dhwaṅsa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, amazwe atholakala ezinhlangothini zaseNtshonalanga naseNyakatho azobhujiswa eMpini Yomhlaba.

U-Achyutananda Das ji ubhala futhi ku ‘Jaiphula Malika’ uqobo:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଜର୍ମାନୀ ରୁଷିଆ ଆଦି,
ଇଟାଲୀ ଜାପାନ ତୁରସ୍କ ଗାଦି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସେହି ସରବେ ହୋଇବେ ରଦ୍ଦି।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Jarmānī Ruṣhiā ādi,
Itālī Jāpāna Turuska gādi lo jāiphula,
sehi sarabe hoibe raddi.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iJalimane, iRussia, i-Italy, iJapane, neTurkey, njll., izizwe zizobhujiswa kakhulu yiMpi Yomhlaba.

Njengoba sesishilo kakade, zonke izigameko ezingenhla zizokwenzeka ikakhulukazi ngesikhathi seMeena-Shani yoga, okungukuthi, phakathi kweminyaka ka-2025 no-2028.

NgoMashi 29, 2025, iSaturn yangena esibonakalweni sePisces (Meena). Ngezinye izikhathi ihamba ngemuva (Vakri) noma ngokuphambene futhi ngezinye izikhathi ihamba ngokuqondile (Margi) noma ngokuqondile, iSaturn izohlala esibonakalweni sePisces kuze kube uFebhuwari 23, 2028. Okwamanje, kusukela ngoJuni 3, 2027, kuya ku-Okthoba 20, 2027, kuzoba nokudlula okwesikhashana kweSaturn esibonakalweni se-Aries (Mesha).

Manje kuphakama umbuzo obalulekile lapha. Sekuyizinyanga eziyi-10 selokhu kwaqala iMeena-Shani yoga, kodwa lezi zigameko ezibhubhisayo azikaqali. Kungani kunjalo? Yiqiniso, izinhlekelele zemvelo zenzeka emhlabeni wonke, izimpi nazo ziyaqhubeka phakathi kwamazwe amaningana, kodwa azikwenzeki ezingeni elikhulu lapho izibikezelo zazo zenziwe khona ngamaPanchasakhas emibhalweni yeMalika! Umbuzo uphakama kaningi ezingqondweni zabaningi bethu ukuthi lezi zigameko zizokwanda nini!

Kuzoqala nini ngempela Ukubhujiswa Okukhulu? Ithini iBhabishya Malika ngalolu daba? IMalika ngokuqinisekile ithi izenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele zizokwenzeka eMeena-Shani, kodwa iMalika ichaza ukuqala kwalezi zenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele njengengxenye yokugcina yonyaka ka-2026 esikhundleni sonyaka ka-2025.

Omunye wamaPanchasakhas, uMahapurush Yashovanta Das, wabhala mayelana neziphazamiso ezingakaze zibonwe ezizokwenzeka emhlabeni wonke ngesikhathi seMeena-Shani emibhalweni yakhe yeMalika futhi wanikeza ulwazi mayelana nesikhathi sazo esiqondile. Kwesinye semibhalo yakhe yeMalika, wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଅକାଳେ ମଡ଼କ ପଡ଼ିବ ସେହି ସମୟରେ ଜାଣ।
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପରେ କମ୍ପିବ ସକଳ ମେଦିନୀ,
କାଳ ଭୈରବୀ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଯେ ରଡ଼ି।
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ତୃତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଜଳ ପ୍ରଳୟ ହୋଇବ ଭଲେ ତୁହି ଜାଣ।
ହୁତୁ ହୁତୁ ହୋଇ ଜଳୁଥିବେ ହିଙ୍ଗୁଳା ମାତିବେ,
ବଡ଼ ବଡ଼ କୋଠା ଘର ଜାଳି ଯେ ଦେବେ।
ଚୋର ଟାଉଟର ସଂଖ୍ୟା ପ୍ରବଳ ବଢ଼ିବ,
ସେଥିପାଇଁ ଦେବୀଙ୍କର କୋପେ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ନ ଦେଖିଲା ନେତ୍ର ପୁଣି ସେ ସମୟେ ଦେଖିବ,
ତ୍ରାହି ତ୍ରାହି ବୋଲି ପୁଣି ଡାକ ଛାଡୁଥିବ।
ହରି ଆଶ୍ରା କର ତୁହି ନିସ୍ତାର ପାଇବୁ,
ଦାସ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ କହେ ବିହନ ହୋଇବୁ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Mīna śhani yogare je dwitīya charaṇa,
akāḷe maḍaka paḍiba sehi samayare jāṇa.
bhūmikampare kampiba sakaḷa medinī,
kāḷa bhairabī chāḍibe śhūnyare je raḍi.
mīna śhani yogare je tṛutīya charaṇa,
jaḷa pralaya hoiba bhale tuhi jāṇa.
hutu hutu hoi jaḷuthibe hiṅguḷā mātibe,
baḍa baḍa koṭhā ghara jāḷi je debe.
chora ṭāuṭara saṅkhyā prabala baḍhiba,
sethipāiñ debīṅkara kope nāśha jiba.
na dekhilā netra puṇi se samaye dekhiba,
trāhi trāhi boli puṇi ḍāka chāḍuthiba.
Hari āśhrā kara tuhi nistāra pāibu,
dāsa Yaśhobanta kahe bihana hoibu.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, eminyakeni emibili nengxenye yeMeena-Shani yoga, ngesikhathi sesigaba sesibili (iminyaka ka-2026-2027) nesigaba sesithathu (iminyaka ka-2027-2028), kuzokwenzeka ukufa okungesikhathi esifanele ngenani elikhulu, futhi isiphithiphithi sizobusa yonke indawo. Kuzokwenzeka ukuzamazama komhlaba okukhulu, wonke uMhlaba uzothuthumela. Kuzoba nokukhala kukaBhairavi, okungukuthi, uMama Bhairavi uzokhipha ukubhonga okwesabekayo ngokungazelelwe esikhaleni. Ukwesabeka kokukhukhula kwamanzi kuzobonakala eMhlabeni. Ngenxa yolaka lukaMama Hingula, izindlu ezinkulu, izindlu ezinkulu, njll., zizosha kakhulu. UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi bonke abantu ababi bazoba izisulu zolaka lwamaMama Onkulunkulukazi. Ngaleso sikhathi, abantu bazobona konke amehlo abo angakaze akubone kuze kube yileso sikhathi, okungukuthi, konke kuzokwenzeka ngendlela engakaze ibonwe. Bonke abantu bazokhala bafune isihe. Labo abazinikela ezinyaweni zeNkosi uShri Hari futhi bathathe isiphephelo sakhe, uMahapurush wathi bona kuphela abazothola insindiso kukho konke lokhu futhi babe imbewu yeSatya Yuga.

Evesini elingenhla, uMahapurush Yashovanta Das wathi lezi zenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele zizoqala ngempumelelo kusukela esigabeni sesibili seMeena-Shani yoga, okungukuthi, iminyaka ka-2026-2027.

Ngesikhathi sale Kali Yuga, amaMahapurushas amaningi azala e-Odisha ngezikhathi ezithile. Ngekhulu lama-20, uMahapurush owazalelwa e-Odisha wembula izibikezelo ezatshelwa yiNkosi Enkulu uqobo, uMama Yogamaya, nonkulunkulu nonkulunkulukazi ukuze kuzuze abazinikele. UMahapurush wabhala:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଆଦେଶ ଦେବୀଙ୍କୁ ହେବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Binśha chabisha sāla sariba,
Śhrī Hari ādeśha debīṅku heba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngemuva kokuba ingxenye yokugcina yonyaka ka-2026 ifike noma unyaka ka-2026 uphele, iNkosi uKalki izonikeza umyalo kuwo wonke amandla aphezulu (oMama Ashta Chandis (Ashta Chandis), 64 oYoginis, amakhulu ayisishiyagalolunye oKatyayanis) ukuze kuqalwe ukubhujiswa.

UMahapurush wabe esebhala futhi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ସମୟ ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ ଯେବେ,
ସବୁ ଦେବା ଦେବୀ ବାହାରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Samaya binśha chabisha sāla sariba jebe,
sabu debā debī bāhāre biśhwajuddha karibe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngemuva konyaka ka-2026, bonke onkulunkulu nonkulunkulukazi bazokhipha ubizo lwempi yamaDeva-Asura. Ukuqala kwempi (iMpi Yomhlaba) kuzokwenzeka emhlabeni.

Kuyaphawuleka ukuthi kuwo womabili amavesi angenhla, uMahapurush wabhala ngokucacile “Unyaka ka-2026”.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Chakada Madana’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଛଅ ସାତ ମିଶାଇଣ ତେର ଯୋଗ କର,
ଏହିଠାରୁ ଘୋର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରତ ବକ୍ଷର।
ସେ ବେଳେ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟଟି ହେବ ଜାଣ,
ଛିନ୍ନଛତ୍ର ଏ ପୃଥିବୀ କହିଲି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chha-a sāta mishaiṇa tera yoga kara,
ehi thāru ghora juddha bhārata bakṣhara.
se beḷe khaṇḍa pralayaṭi heba jāṇa,
chhinnachhatra e pruthibī kahili pramāṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, hlanganisa isithupha (6) nesikhombisa (7) bese wengeza ishumi nantathu (13) esibalo, okungukuthi, 6+7+13=26, lapho impi enkulu izoqala khona eNdiya. Ngemuva kwaleso sikhathi esifanayo, izigameko zokubhujiswa okuyingxenye (Khanda Pralaya) zizokwenzeka futhi uMhlaba uzoqala ukubhujiswa.

Kuyaphawuleka ukuthi lapha uMahapurush Achyutananda Das, ngokungafani namavesi amabili angenhla, akazange abhale “Unyaka ka-2026” kodwa wabhala kuphela u-26. Mhlawumbe lapha, ngo-26, inkomba kaMahapurush Achyutananda Das iqonde onyakeni ka-2026 kuphela.

UMahapurush owazalwa ngekhulu lama-20 ubhala futhi:

I-Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ପରେ ତ କରିବୁ ବିଚାର,
ମନେ ନ କରିବୁ ଯଦି ଯିବୁ ଯମପୁର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Binśha chabisha pare ta karibu bichāra,
mane na karibu jadi jibu Yamapura.

Incazelo:
Evesini elingenhla, uMama Yogamaya uthi ngemuva konyaka ka-2026, wonke umuntu uzokwahlulelwa. Bonke labo abangakholwa, ababi, izoni, njll., bonke bazobhujiswa.

Ngakho-ke, kufanele kuqondwe ngokucacile ukuthi izenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele zikaKalki ezichazwe yiMalika zizokwenzeka ngesikhathi seMeena-Shani yoga uqobo, kodwa lezi ngeke zenzeke ngonyaka ka-2025 kodwa zizoqala engxenyeni yokugcina ka-2026.

Ukuthi lezi zenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele zizohlala iminyaka emingaki isiyonke, uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ngalolu daba embhalweni wakhe weMalika othi ‘Kaliyuga Malika’:

I-Odia Sloka:

ଚବିଶ ଠାବରୁ ଅଣତିରିଶ ଯାଏ ପ୍ରମାଦ ଅଛି ଅପାର,
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେଟି ହୋ ଗରୁଡ଼ ମହାକଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଭିତରେଟି ଗରୁଡ଼ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସରିବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Chabiśha ṭhābaru aṇatiriśha jāe pramāda achi apāra,
aṇatiriśha aṅkareṭi ho Garuḍa mahā Kaḷkī abatāra.
aṇatiriśha bhitareṭi Garuḍa sabu utpāta sariba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, izenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele zizoqala kunombolo 24 futhi zizohlala kuze kube inombolo 29. Zonke izenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele zizophela kunombolo 29.

Phawula ukuthi evesini elingenhla, uMahapurush uxoxe ngenombolo 24, hhayi unyaka ka-2024. Kodwa uMahapurush ukwenzile kwacaca ukuthi lezi zenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele, ziqala kunombolo 24 futhi zihlala kuze kube inombolo 29, zizoqhubeka iminyaka engu-6 isiyonke. UMahapurush akazange acacise ukuthi zizoqala ngamuphi unyaka futhi zizophela ngamuphi unyaka, kodwa wathi kusukela kunombolo 24 kuya kunombolo 29, okungukuthi, iminyaka engu-6 isiyonke, lezi zenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele zizoqhubeka.

Njengoba emavesini angenhla sesivele sazi ukuthi lezi zenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele zizoqala engxenyeni yokugcina yonyaka ka-2026, manje singafinyelela kalula kulesi siphetho sokuthi inombolo 24 ye ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ ikhomba unyaka ka-2026-2027. Ngokunjalo, inombolo 29 yale chungechunge olufanayo lwale mbhalo ofanayo weMalika ikhomba onyakeni ka-2031-2032.

UMahapurush Achyutananda Das, ngencwadi yakhe yeMalika Pothi, wachaza futhi –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଏହା ତ ହେଉଛି କାଳର ଭୟ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ତହିଁ,
ଏହା ତ କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳଟି ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳ କରୁଅଛନ୍ତି ହେତୁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଆଉ ବା ଏକତିରିଶ ସେ ତ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ।
ମହାମାରୀ ସେ ତ ଆସିବ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଆସ,
ତେତିଶି ମସିହା ସୁଦ୍ଧାଟି କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଠା ଠିକଣା ଯେ ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହି,
ଏଭଳି ସମୟ ଆସିବ ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କାହାରି ପତା ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ,
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନୀମାନେ ବାଟଟି ପାଇବେ ନାହିଁରେ ଜାଣ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Ehā ta heuchhi kāḷara bhaya Achyutī tahiñ,
ehā ta Kaḷkī kheḷaṭi dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kaḷkī kheḷa karuachhanti hetu pramāṇa,
āu bā ekatiriśa se ta heture jāṇa.
Mahāmārī se ta āsiba kahe Achyutī āsa,
Tetiriśhi masihā suddhāṭi kahe dīna Achyuta.
Ṭhā ṭhikaṇā je rahiba nāhiñ Achyutī kahi,
ebhaḷi samaya āsiba dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kāhāri patā rahiba nāhiñ heture jāṇa,
Baijñānīmāne bāṭaṭi pāibe nāhiñ re jāṇa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi uKalki izokwenza izenzo wayo (izenzo zakhe zokubhujiswa ezingcwele). Lezi zenzo zokubhujiswa ezingcwele zizoqhubeka kuze kube unyaka ka-2033. Okwamanje, kuzofika izifo eziningi eziwumqedazwe, ezizodala isiphithiphithi emphakathini wabantu. Kuzofika isikhathi lapho kuzoba khona isiphithiphithi yonke indawo. Bonke abantu bazoba sesimweni esibuhlungu. Ngisho nolwazi lososayensi ngeke lusebenze.

Nokho, u-Achyutananda Das ji waphinde wathi ku ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ yakhe ukuthi imisebenzi emikhulu yokubhujiswa izophela kunombolo 27.

UMahapurush wabhala –

I-Odia Sloka:

ସତାଇଶ ଅଙ୍କେ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଦିଗରୁ ସମୁଦ୍ର ଆସିବ ମାଡ଼ି,
ଉତ୍ତରରୁ ଗଙ୍ଗା ଉଛୁଳି ପଡ଼ିଣ ତହିଁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଦେବ ଧାଡ଼ି।
ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଦିଗରୁ ଜଳ ସ୍ରୋତ ଏକ ଆସି ତହିଁ ଯିବ ମିଶି,
ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟର ସୃଜନା କରିବ ପାପୀ ତହିଁ ଯିବେ ଭାସି।
ପୁଣ୍ୟାତ୍ମାମାନଙ୍କୁ ସ୍ୱୟଂ ବଳଦେବ ଉଦ୍ଧାର କରି ରଖିବେ,
ଭାରତର ଗିରି ପର୍ବତ ପାହାଡ଼େ ନେଇ ଯତନେ ପାଳିବେ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Satāisha aṅke dakhina digaru samudra āsiba māḍi,
uttararu Gaṅgā uchhuḷi paḍiṇa tahiñ saṅge deba dhāḍi.
Pashchima digaru jala srota eka āsi tahiñ jiba mishi,
khaṇḍa praḷayara sṛujanā kariba pāpī tahiñ jibe bhāsi.
Puṇyātmāmānaṅku swayaṃ Baḷadeba uddhāra kari rakhibe,
Bhāratara giri parbata pāhāḍe nei jatane pāḷibe.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, kunombolo 27, amagagasi olwandle azovela eNingizimu futhi ahlangane nomfula iGanges ogcwele ovela eNyakatho. Ngasikhathi sinye, amagagasi amakhulu e-tsunami azophinde avela eNtshonalanga futhi adale umonakalo. Kulokhu kubhujiswa okuyingxenye, izoni ezingenakubalwa zizofa. Noma ngubani ongabantu abangcwele nemiphefumulo emihle, uBaladev uqobo uzobasindisa futhi abagcine bephephile ngokucophelela ezintabeni nasemagqumeni aseNdiya.

Ngokusho kukaMahapurush, lesi kuzoba yisinhlekelele yokugcina phakathi kwezinhlekelele ezinkulu ezizokwenzeka kunombolo 27, okungukuthi, ngonyaka ka-2029-2030 ngokusho kokubala okukhulunywe ngakho ngenhla.

Manje ake sizame ukuqonda lesi sikhathi ngamavesi ambalwa. UMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala ku-‘Bhabishyata Malika’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ସତୁରି ଯାଏ ଠୁଳ,
ଲାଗି ରହିବ ତୃତୀୟ ମହାସମର।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଚମକ ପଡିବ ସ୍ଵର୍ଗ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ପାତାଳ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Pāñcha barṣha kāḷa saturi jāe ṭhuḷa,
lāgi rahiba tṛutīya mahāsamara.
pāñcha barṣha kāḷa juddha hoiba ghora,
chamaka paḍiba swarga martya pātāḷa.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iMpi Yesithathu Yomhlaba izohlala iminyaka emihlanu. Kuzoba nempi enzima iminyaka emihlanu; Izulu, Umhlaba, kanye Nomhlaba Ongaphansi kuzothuthumela.

U-Achyutananda Das ji ubhala kwenye yezincwadi zakhe zeMalika –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କହନ୍ତି ବାସବ ଶୁଣ,
ଚବିଶ ଠାରୁ ସତେଇଶ ଜାଣ।
ଏଥି ଭିତରେ ଯେ ହୋଇବ ଗୋଳ,
କାହାରି ନ ରହିବ ଥଳକୁଳ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Śhrī Hari kahanti Bāsaba śhuṇa,
chabiśha ṭhāru sataiśha jāṇa.
ethi bhitare je hoiba goḷa,
kāhāri na rahiba thaḷakuḷa.

Incazelo:
Evesini elingenhla, iNkosi (Lord) uShri Krishna, ngenkathi ekhuluma no-Indra, ithi kuzoba nempi enzima kusukela kunombolo 24 kuya kunombolo 27.

Kucacile kuwo womabili amavesi abhalwe ngenhla ukuthi impi enkulu noma iMpi Yesithathu Yomhlaba izohlala iminyaka engaba ngu-4-5. Okusho ukuthi, iqala ngo-2026-2027 iphele ngo-2029-2030.

Ngakho-ke, ukubhujiswa okukhulu kokugcina okukhulunywe ngakho yiMahapurush ku-‘Kaliyuga Malika’ kunombolo 27 noma unyaka ka-2029-2030 kufana ngokuphelele nawo womabili amavesi anikezwe ngenhla.

Ezingxoxweni ezingenhla, uma kufinyelelwa esiphethweni ngamafuphi:

Unyaka 2026 – 2027: Isikhathi sokubhujiswa sizoqala.
Unyaka 2029 – 2030: Izehlakalo ezimbi kakhulu nezibhubhisayo zizophela.
Unyaka 2031 – 2032: Zonke izehlakalo ezibhubhisayo zizophela.

Qaphela: Imisebenzi emikhulu yokubhujiswa ephela ngonyaka ka-2029 ihlanganisa yonke leyo evame ukuxoxwa ngayo, njenge – ukuzamazama komhlaba okukhulu, ukubhujiswa okukhulu kwamanzi, izinhlekelele zomoya, izinhlekelele zomlilo, ukuwa kwamatshe avela esibhakabhakeni, izifo-izifo eziwumshayabhuqe, impi yaseNdiya namazwe ayizitha, iMpi Yomhlaba, impi yeKali-Mahabharata, ukuphuka kweHirakud namanye amadamu, ubumnyama izinsuku eziyisikhombisa nobusuku obuyisikhombisa, iBhairavi Dak, njll.

13 Ukuqala kweSatyayuga

Ukuze angene eSatyayuga, kubalulekile ukuba umuntu ahambe endleleni yeDharma. Kufanele ahlale ekhuluma iqiniso futhi alandele iqiniso kuphela; akufanele athathe indlela yobuhlanya kodwa indlela yokuthula. Kufanele abe nemizwa yothando, ububele, kanye nesihe kuzo zonke izidalwa eziphilayo. Akufanele abangele ubuhlungu kunoma yisiphi isidalwa esiphilayo ngengqondo yakhe, inkulumo, kanye nokuziphatha. Kufanele abe umfuni okuhle kuzo zonke izidalwa ngaphandle kwesizathu, ukuziphatha kwakhe kufanele kube ukuthethelela abanye, kufanele abe nesimo esilula nesiqotho, akufanele agcine ubutha kunoma ubani, futhi kufanele azi ukuthi angazigcina kanjani izinzwa zakhe zilawuleka. Kufanele abe umkhulekeli kaNkulunkulu, ahlale egxilisa ingqondo yakhe kuNkulunkulu, achithe isikhathi ekuculeni amaculo, ekuhlabeleleni amagama kaNkulunkulu, ajoyine imihlangano engokomoya (Satsang), enze imithandazo yansuku zonke kathathu (Trisandhya), alandele imibhalo engcwele, futhi afunde iShrimad Bhagavat Mahapurana (eyaziwa njengeMahapurana (Mahapurana) phakathi kwamaPuranas angu-18) nsuku zonke. Kufanele abe ngumuntu ofunda noma ozwa izenzo zikaNkulunkulu. Kufanele adle ukudla okuhlanzekile nokweSattvic (okungadli inyama). Kanye nokwenza imisebenzi yakhe yasemhlabeni, kufanele futhi azibandakanye neYoga, ukuhlabelela amagama, njll. Kufanele ahlale ehlonipha abazali bakhe nabadala, abonise uthando kubancane, futhi asungule umuzwa wobungane nontanga.

Ngamunye wethu ufisa ukungena eSatyayuga ngokubaleka imisebenzi ebhubhisa ezayo, izinhlekelele zemvelo, kanye neMpi Yomhlaba, nangokuthola umusa weNkosi (Lord) uKalki. Kodwa ingabe ukungena eSatyayuga kulula kangako? Cha. Ukungena eSatyayuga akufani nokwenza isixazululo engilazini bese usiphuza ukuze noma ubani osiphuza ngale ndlela azongena ngokuqinisekile eSatyayuga!

Ukuze angene eSatyayuga, wonke umuntu udinga ukuletha izinguquko eziyisisekelo empilweni yakhe. Kubalulekile ukuthi kanye nokwenza imisebenzi yasemhlabeni, aqale ukuphila impilo engokomoya.

Echaza ukuthi isikhathi esizayo sizoba njani kubakhulekeli (devotees), uMahapurush Achyutananda Das ubhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika ethi ‘Chakada Madana’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ହଟି ଯାଇ ସତ୍ୟର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଭକତମାନେ ରହିବେ ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଉଲ୍ଲାସ।
ବହୁ କଷ୍ଟ ପାଇଛନ୍ତି ଭକତ ମୋହର,
ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନରେ ଦୁଃଖ କଷ୍ଟ ଯିବ ଦୂର।
ଏ କଳି ହଟିବ ଯୁଗ ଅନନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରଣ ହୋଇବ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Kaḷiyuga haṭi jāi satyara prakāśha,
bhakatamāne rahibe atyanta ullāsa.
bahu kaṣhṭa pāichhanti bhakata mohara,
alpa dinare duḥkha kaṣhṭa jiba dūra.
e kaḷi haṭiba juga ananta hoiba,
dīna Achyutī kāmanā pūraṇa hoiba.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, abakhulekeli kwadingeka bahlupheke kakhulu eKaliyuga, kodwa ngemuva kwesikhashana lapho iKaliyuga iphela futhi kuvela ukukhanya kwesikhathi saphakade (Ananta Yuga), abakhulekeli bazojabula kakhulu. Khona-ke zonke izinsizi nobuhlungu babo kuzophela.

USanta Bhima Bhoi ubhala kwenye yezincwadi zakhe zeMalika ethi ‘Stuti Chintamani’ –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ନିବାରିବେ ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିବେ ପାଳିବେ ଜନ ପରଜା,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ ଅନାଦି ହୋଇବେ ରାଜା।
ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ ଭକତ ସକଳ ହରି ହରିବେ ଯେ ଦୁଃଖ,
ହରିବୋଲ ହୁଳହୁଳି ଶବଦରେ ପୂରିବ ଏହି ତ୍ରୈଲୋକ୍ୟ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Duṣṭa nibāribe santhaṅku rakhibe pāḷibe jana parajā,
Kaḷiyuga sheṣe satya upujiba Anādi hoibe rājā.
Haraṣa hoibe bhakata sakaḷa hari haribe je duḥkha,
Haribola huḷuhuḷi shabadare pūriba ehi traiḷokya.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, iNkosi Ephakeme (Supreme Lord) izobhubhisa ababi futhi ivikele abangcwele nabakhulekeli, futhi inakekele izakhamuzi emhlabeni. IKaliyuga izophela futhi iSatyayuga izofika, futhi iNkosi uKalki Yona izoba umbusi womhlaba wonke. INkosi izosusa izinsizi zabo bonke abakhulekeli futhi ibagcine bejabule. Abakhulekeli bazojabula, futhi yonke imihlaba emithathu izohlabelela igama elithi 'Hari'.

13.1 Izokwenzeka nini iSatyayuga?

IBhabishya Malika ithi i-Ananta Yuga noma i-Adi Satyayuga izoqala ngonyaka ka-2033. UMahapurush wabhala encwadini yakhe yeMalika –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ଡାହାଣରେ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ରଖ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ଯବନ ଗଣ ବିନାଶଟି ଧ୍ରୁବ,
ଦୁଇ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କରୁ ଆରମ୍ଭ।
ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ଅସତ୍ୟ ମାନ ହଟି ଯାଉଥିବ,
ଅଧର୍ମର ନାଶେ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରଭାବୀ ହୋଇବ।
ତେତିଶିରେ ଚାରି ପାଦ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମା ତହୁଁ ବିକଶିବ।
ସରଳ ସଜ୍ଜନ ସାଧୁ ସନ୍ଥ ଜନ ମେଳ,
ହେବା ପାଇଁ ଏ ଧରା ରେ ଆସିଗଲା ବେଳ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Dui ḍāhāṇare shūna eka tini rakha,
sampūrṇṇa satya prakāsha hoiba pratyakṣa.
Mlechha jabana gaṇa bināshaṭi dhruba,
dui shūna eka tini aṅkaru ārambha.
Anyāya asatya māna haṭi jāuthiba,
adharmara nāshe dharma prabhābhī hoiba.
Tetishire chāri pāda dharma prakāshiba,
satya shānti dayā kṣamā tahuñ bikaśhiba.
Saraḷa sajjana sādhu santha jana meḷa,
hebā pāiñ e dharā re āsigalā beḷa.

Incazelo:
UMahapurush wabhala ukuthi kusukela ngonyaka ka-2013, ukubhujiswa kweKaliyuga nokukhanya kweQiniso kuzoqala kancane kancane. Kusukela ngaleso sikhathi kuqhubeke, ukubhujiswa kwababi nezoni kuzoqala. Amandla amanga, ukungalungi, kanye nokungalungi kuzoncipha, futhi ithonya leDharma lizoqala ukwanda. UMahapurush wabhala ngokuqhubekayo ukuthi ngokuqhubeka kanjena, ngonyaka ka-2033, ukukhanya kwazo zonke izinsika ezine zeDharma kuzovela, okusho ukuthi ukusungulwa okuphelele kweDharma kuzokwenzeka. Kusukela ngaleso sikhathi kuqhubeke, izimfanelo zeQiniso, Ukuthula, Ububele, kanye Nokuthethelela zizoqala ukuthuthuka ngokugcwele. Kuzoba nokuhlangana kwabo bonke abantu abahloniphekile, abanomusa nabangcwele, abangcwele, izazi.

UMahapurush ubhala futhi –

I-Odia Sloka:

ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଗୁପତୁ ଗୁପତ, ତେତିଶି ଅଙ୍କେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ଭକତମାନେ ଯେ ଜାଣିବେ, ସମସ୍ତେ ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ।
ସଂସାର ଉଶ୍ଵାସ ହୋଇବ, କାଳର ଭୟ ଯେ ଛାଡିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ବସ୍ତ୍ରରେ ସୁଖୀ ହେବେ, ଧର୍ମ ପଥକୁ ଆଚୋରିବେ।
ଶସ୍ୟ ଯେ ବହୁତ ହୋଇବ, ଲକ୍ଷ୍ମୀ କୃପାରୁ ସବୁ ହେବ।
ସତ୍ୟରେ ସର୍ବେ ଆତଜାତ, ସତ୍ୟ କଳି ଯେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ପୁରାଣ ଭାଗବତ ମାନ, କରିବେ ଗାନ ସଂକୀର୍ତ୍ତନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶ ଯିବ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
କହନ୍ତି ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ କରତା, ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନଥିବେ ଏକ ଜାଣ, ଭକ୍ତ ଭଗବାନ ମିଳନ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Atyanta gupatu gupata, Tetishi aṅke heba khyāta.
Bhakatamāne je jāṇibe, samaste haraṣa hoibe.
Saṃsāra ushwāsa hoiba, kāḷara bhaya je chhāḍiba.
Anna bastrare sukhī hebe, dharma pathaku āchoribe.
Shasya je bahuta hoiba, Laxmī kṛpāru sabu heba.
Satyare sarbe ātajāta, satya kaḷi je heba khyāta.
Purāṇa Bhāgabata māna, karibe gāna saṅkīrttana.
Kaḷi kalmaṣa nāsha jiba, Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Kahanti Brahmāṇḍa karatā, ananta jugara byabasthā.
Mlechha nathibe eka jāṇa, bhakta Bhagavān milana.

Incazelo:
UMahapurush wathi lokhu kuyindaba eyimfihlo kakhulu futhi izokwenzeka kunombolo 33 (unyaka ka-2033). Abakhulekeli kuphela abazokwazi lokhu. Bazojabula kakhulu ngokwazi lokhu. Umhlaba uzokhululeka esonweni; ngeke kube khona ukwesaba noma ukudideka engqondweni yanoma ubani. Wonke umuntu uzojabula, wonke umuntu uzoba nokudla nezingubo ezanele, wonke umuntu uzolandela indlela yeDharma. Khona-ke kuzoba nesivuno esihle kakhulu, futhi lokhu kuzoba ngenxa yomusa kaMama (Mother) uLakshmi. Wonke umuntu uzohamba endleleni yeQiniso; isikhathi saphakade sizofika. Wonke umuntu uzofunda amaPuranas neBhagavat futhi enze amaculo nokuhlabelela. IKaliyuga izobhujiswa ngokuphelele; lesi sitatimende ngeke sishintshe nakancane. UMahapurush waphinde wathi lesi kuzoba isikhathi saphakade futhi lokhu kuzoba ukuhlelwa kwesikhathi saphakade (Adya Satyayuga). Ngeke kube khona noyedwa ongalungile, omubi, noma umlechha emhlabeni. Abakhulekeli kuphela abazohlala futhi bonke bazohlangana noNkulunkulu.

UMahapurush owazalwa ngekhulu lama-20 (esixoxe ngaye ngaphambili), ngenkathi ecaphuna iNkosi Ephakeme, uMama uYogamaya, kanye nonkulunkulu nonkulunkulukazi, wabhala –

I-Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତିନି ଚାରି ତିନି ଧାଡିରେ ତ ଜାଣ,
ଏହି ଦିନ ଠାରୁ ହେବ ସତ୍ୟର ନିୟମ।
ଗୁରୁବାର ଦିନ ଥିବ ଶୁଣୁଥାଅ ନର,
ସେହି ଦିନଠାରୁ ସତ୍ୟ ଭୋଗ ତ ଆମର।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Tini tini chāri tini dhāḍire ta jāṇa,
ehi dina thāru heba satyara niyama.
Gurubāra dina thiba śhuṇuthāa nara,
sehi dinathāru satya bhoga ta amara.

Incazelo:
Lapha uMahapurush ucela ukubhala u-“3” kane ngokulandelana. Okungukuthi 3333. UMahapurush uthi kusukela ngalolu suku kuqhubeke, iSatyayuga izoqala emhlabeni. Lapha ngo-3333, uMahapurush usho – usuku lwesithathu, inyanga yesithathu, neminyaka engamashumi amathathu nantathu, okungukuthi, uMashi 3, 2033. UMahapurush uphinde abhale ukuthi lolo suku luzoba uLwesine futhi iSatyayuga izoqala ngalolo suku.

UMahapurush wabhala futhi –

I-Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତାରିଖ ତ ତିନି ମାସ ବିଂଶ ଶାଳ,
ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କ ତ ପରେ ସତ୍ୟ ସତ୍ୟ ବୋଲ।

Ukuhumusha (Transliteration):

Tini tārikha ta tini māsa biṃśha śhāḷa,
tetishā aṅka ta pare satya satya bola.

Incazelo:
Okusho ukuthi, ngemuva kosuku lwesithathu lwenyanga yesithathu yenombolo engamashumi amathathu nantathu yonyaka wamashumi amabili, okungukuthi, uMashi 3, 2033, iSatyayuga izofika.

Ngakho-ke, akukho ukungabaza manje ukuthi isikhathi esisha (Ananta Yuga noma Adi Satyayuga) sizoqala ngonyaka ka-2033. Kuzoba isikhathi somkhulekeli (devotee) noNkulunkulu kuphela. Ngamanye amazwi, abakhulekeli kuphela abazokwazi ukungena kulesi sikhathi. INkosi izovikela Umhlaba ngeQiniso, iDharma, kanye Nobulungiswa, futhi bonke abakhulekeli bazokwenza iQiniso, Ukuthula, Ububele, kanye Nokuthethelela ezimpilweni zabo.

14 Isifinyezo

IBhabishya Malika iqoqo lemibhalo eminingi ebhalwe ngamaPanchasakhas azalwa eminyakeni engaba ngu-600 edlule kanye nabanye abangcwele base-Odia namadoda amakhulu azalwa ngekhulu le-17, le-18, le-19, nele-20. Imibhalo yeMalika yabhalwa ikakhulukazi ngamaPanchasakhas eNkosi uShri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Lawa maPanchasakhas avela njengabangane abaseduze beNkosi kuzo zonke izikhathi ngamagama ahlukene, naseKaliyuga futhi, eminyakeni engaba ngu-600 edlule, lawa maPanchasakhas athola ubuseduze bokuzalwa kweNkosi (Lord), uChaitanya Mahaprabhu. Amagama alawa maPanchasakhas azalwa eKaliyuga yile – Achyutananda Das, Balaram Das, Jashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, kanye noJagannath Das.

AmaPanchasakhas athathwa njengokuzalwa okuyingxenye (Anshavatar) kweNkosi, futhi eminyakeni engaba ngu-600 edlule, imibhalo yeMalika yabhalwa yibo kuphela ngomyalo weNkosi. Inhloso eyodwa yokubhalwa kwemibhalo yeMalika yinhlalakahle yabakhulekeli – ukuvusa ukuqonda okulele kwabakhulekeli ekupheleni kweKaliyuga nokubasindisa ngokubakhombisa indlela. AmaPanchasakhas abhala ngokucacile emibhalweni yawo ukuthi le mibhalo akufanele yembulwe kunoma ubani ngaphandle kwabakhulekeli. AmaPanchasakhas aphinde acacisa ukuthi yilabo kuphela abazokwazi ukwamukela amazwi eMalika abaye baba abakhulekeli (devotees) beNkosi kusukela ekuzalweni nasezikhathini ezedlule.

Njengoba iMalika iyizwi likaMahaprabhu uQobo futhi yabhalwa ngamaPanchasakhas kuphela ngomyalo weNkosi, ngakho-ke wonke amazwi eMalika afana nomugqa oqoshwe etsheni ongenakuze ube nephutha futhi ongenakuze ushintshwe. Akukho okumangazayo kulokhu ukuthi iziprofetho ezingenakubalwa zeMalika ezibhalelwe isikhathi samanje ziye zafakazela ukuthi ziyiQiniso ngo-100%. Ngokwesibonelo – isiprofetho sokuzimela kweNdiya, isiprofetho sokuzalwa kukaGandhiji, isiprofetho sokwakhiwa kwezwe elibizwa ngePakistan, isiprofetho sokwakhiwa kwedamu laseHirakud e-Odisha, njll.

IBhabishya Malika ixoxa ngokuningiliziwe ngezihloko ezifana nokuphela kweKaliyuga, iKalki Avatar, ukubhujiswa kwababi nezoni, ukuvikelwa kwabakhulekeli, ukusungulwa kweDharma, ukufika kwe-Ananta Yuga, njll. IMalika ithi isikhathi seKaliyuga, esasingu-4,32,000 iminyaka, sizokwehliswa sibe iminyaka engu-5,000 kuphela ngenxa yezenzo ezahlukahlukene zesono ezenziwa eKaliyuga. Ngemuva kokuba sekudlule iminyaka engu-5,000, iNkosi uJagannath izoshiya iNilachala Dham Yakhe, iPuri, futhi izalwe emzimbeni womuntu eBiraja Kshetra, eJajpur yase-Odisha, bese ihlala eKhandagiri, eBhubaneswar. Ngokusho kweMalika, lokhu kuzoba iKalki Avatar kaNkulunkulu.

IMalika ithi ekupheleni kweKaliyuga, abakhulekeli bazothola izimpawu eziningi ezinjalo ezivela ethempeleni lasePuri Jagannath nakwezinye izindela namathempeli aseNdiya, ngokubona lokho bazoqonda ukuthi iKaliyuga isiphelile futhi iNkosi uKalki isizalwe. Ngokwesibonelo, ukundiza kude kwefulegi elikhulu ethempeleni laseJagannath, ukuwa okuphindaphindiwe kwamatshe ethempeleni, iNilachakra iba nciphile, ukuhlala njalo kokhozi (vulture) phezu kwethempeli, njll. Kuyaziwa ukuthi zonke lezi zimpawu ziye zabonwa abakhulekeli eminyakeni embalwa edlule.

IMalika ikhomba ekuzalweni kweNkosi uKalki ngonyaka othile phakathi kuka-2005-2008. Ngokusho kweMalika, inhloso yeKalki Avatar ukubhubhisa ababi, ukuvikela abakhulekeli, nokusungula kabusha iDharma emhlabeni. IMalika ithi le Yuga-parivartana izoqala ngo-2026-2027 ngesikhathi seMeena-Shani yoga yiNkosi uKalki futhi iphele ngokuphelele ngo-2031-2032. Ngaleso sikhathi, yonke imisebenzi emikhulu yokubhujiswa izokwenzeka ngonyaka ka-2029.

IMalika iphinde ithi ngesikhathi iNkosi izosungula iDharma emhlabeni, igama leNkosi yasePuri lizoba uShri Dibyasingha Deb, okusho ukuthi zonke izenzo zokubhujiswa zeNkosi zizokwenzeka ngesikhathi sokubusa kweNkosi yasePuri (Puri) uDibyasingha Deb. Ngokusho kweMalika, lezi zenzo zokubhujiswa zizoba ikakhulukazi ngokubhujiswa kweZinto Ezinhlanu (Panchabhuta) kanye neMpi Yomhlaba.

IMalika ithi ngemuva kokuba yonke imisebenzi ebhubhisa isiphele ngokuphelele ngonyaka ka-2031-2032, i-Adi-Satyayuga noma i-Anantayuga izoqala ngonyaka ka-2033.
